if not game:IsLoaded() then game.Loaded:Wait() end
pcall(function() game:GetService("Players").RespawnTime = 0 end)
local privateBuild = false

-- Luraph macro passthrough shims (dev-mode only).
-- Luraph's parser rejects ANY assignment to the literal macro names
-- (`LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE = ...` or `function LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(...) end`),
-- even when guarded by `if LPH_OBFUSCATED == nil then`. The runtime
-- guard doesn't help — the syntax check fires first. So we hide the
-- identifier from the parser by building it as a concatenated string
-- and assigning through `getfenv()[name]` (the script's actual env,
-- NOT `_G` — `_G` is a separate shared table and writing to it does
-- not create a script-local global, which is what `LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE`
-- needs to be when called as a bare identifier downstream). When
-- obfuscated, LPH_OBFUSCATED is truthy and this block doesn't run;
-- when raw (dev mode), the shims install as identity functions.
if LPH_OBFUSCATED == nil then
    local env = getfenv()
    env["LPH_NO_" .. "VIRTUALIZE"] = function(...) return ... end
    env["LPH_JIT_" .. "MAX"]       = function(...) return ... end
end

    BalloonedPlayers = {},
    MobileScaleObjects = {},
    RefreshMobileScale = nil,
    MobileActionButtons = {},
    RefreshMobileActionButtons = nil,
}

-- ============================================================
-- Unstealable-state filters: brainrots currently locked inside a
-- machine (Fuse, Duel, Trade, Crafting) and plots whose owner is
-- in duel-selection. Used by every brainrot iterator (ESP, steal
-- target list, auto-snipe target picker, auto-back, etc.).
-- ============================================================
-- Per-animal machine filter — based on PlotClient.lua lines 247–264
-- in the dump, which renders "IN FUSE" / "IN DUEL" / "IN TRADE" /
-- "IN MACHINE" depending on `animalData.Machine.Type`. Any of these
-- means the brainrot is locked and cannot be stolen.
-- (Function name kept as "Fusing" so the existing 5+ call sites stay
-- unchanged — semantically it now means "in any blocking machine".)
local _BLOCKING_MACHINE_TYPES = {
    Fuse     = true,
    Duel     = true,
    Trade    = true,
    Crafting = true,
}
local function _VanishIsFusing(animalData)
    if type(animalData) ~= "table" then return false end
    local m = animalData.Machine
    if type(m) ~= "table" then return false end
    return _BLOCKING_MACHINE_TYPES[m.Type] == true
end
_G.VanishIsFusing = _VanishIsFusing

-- Per-plot owner filter — true when the plot's owner is currently in
-- the duel-selection UI (or trading, which the game also blocks steals
-- during). Mirrors the game's PlotClient.lua line 545 check:
--   LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("IsTrading") or LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("IsDuelSelecting")
-- applied to the OWNING player instead of LocalPlayer.
local function _VanishIsDuelBlocking(ownerObj)
    if not ownerObj then return false end
    local plr
    if typeof(ownerObj) == "Instance" then
        if ownerObj:IsA("Player") then
            plr = ownerObj
        end
    elseif type(ownerObj) == "table" then
        if ownerObj.UserId then
            plr = game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayerByUserId(ownerObj.UserId)
        elseif ownerObj.Name then
            plr = game:GetService("Players"):FindFirstChild(ownerObj.Name)
        end
    elseif type(ownerObj) == "string" then
        plr = game:GetService("Players"):FindFirstChild(ownerObj)
    end
    if not plr then return false end
    if plr:GetAttribute("IsDuelSelecting") == true then return true end
    if plr:GetAttribute("IsTrading")       == true then return true end
    return false
end
_G.VanishIsDuelBlocking = _VanishIsDuelBlocking
_G.VanishAnyDuelBlock   = _VanishIsDuelBlocking  -- alias

do
    local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local getupvalue = getupvalue or debug.getupvalue

    local function getInternalTable()
        local Packages = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Packages")
        if not Packages then return nil end
        local SynMod = Packages:FindFirstChild("Synchronizer")
        if not SynMod then return nil end
        local ok, syn = pcall(require, SynMod)
        if not ok or not syn then return nil end
        local Get = syn.Get
        if type(Get) ~= "function" then return nil end
        for i = 1, 5 do
            local s, u = pcall(getupvalue, Get, i)
            if s and type(u) == "table" then
                if u.___private or u.___channels or u.___data then return u end
                for k, v in pairs(u) do
                    if (type(k) == "string" and k:match("^Plot_")) or type(v) == "table" then
                        return u
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        local s, e = pcall(getfenv, Get)
        if s and e and e.self then return e.self end
        return nil
    end

    local SyncInt = {_cache = {}, _data = nil}
    _G.SyncInt = SyncInt

    task.spawn(function()
        for i = 1, 10 do
            SyncInt._data = getInternalTable()
            if SyncInt._data then break end
            task.wait(1)
        end
    end)

    function _G.stealthGet(n)
        if not n or type(n) ~= "string" then return nil end
        if SyncInt._cache[n] == false then return nil end
        if SyncInt._data then
            for _, k in ipairs({n, "Plot_" .. n, "Plot" .. n, n .. "_Channel", "Channel_" .. n}) do
                if SyncInt._data[k] then
                    SyncInt._cache[n] = SyncInt._data[k]
                    return SyncInt._data[k]
                end
            end
            for k, v in pairs(SyncInt._data) do
                if type(k) == "string" and (k == n or k:find(n, 1, true)) and type(v) == "table" then
                    SyncInt._cache[n] = v
                    return v
                end
            end
        end
        SyncInt._cache[n] = false
        return nil
    end

    function _G.sProp(ch, p)
        if not ch or type(ch) ~= "table" then return nil end
        if ch[p] then return ch[p] end
        if type(ch.Get) == "function" then
            local ok, r = pcall(ch.Get, ch, p)
            if ok then return r end
        end
        local alts = {
            Owner = {"owner", "Owner", "plotOwner", "PlotOwner"},
            AnimalList = {"animalList", "AnimalList", "animals", "Animals", "pets"},
        }
        if alts[p] then
            for _, a in ipairs(alts[p]) do
                if ch[a] then return ch[a] end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local Packages = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Packages")
    local SynMod = Packages and Packages:FindFirstChild("Synchronizer")
    local okReq, syn = pcall(require, SynMod)
    if okReq and typeof(syn) == "table" then
        local function HasBoolUpvalue(Fn)
            local OkU, Ups = xpcall(debug.getupvalues, function() end, Fn)
            if not OkU then return false end
            for _, V in pairs(Ups) do
                if typeof(V) == "boolean" then return true end
            end
            return false
        end
        for _, Fn in pairs(syn) do
            if typeof(Fn) == "function" and not isexecutorclosure(Fn) then
                local OkU, Ups = xpcall(debug.getupvalues, function() end, Fn)
                if OkU then
                    for Idx, V in pairs(Ups) do
                        if typeof(V) == "function" and not isexecutorclosure(V) and HasBoolUpvalue(V) then
                            pcall(debug.setupvalue, Fn, Idx, newcclosure(function() end))
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

-- (System A removed: the legacy fireproximityprompt auto-steal poll
--  loop + its helpers. Patched/unreliable per the script's own comments;
--  System B (getconnections attemptSteal) is the live steal path. Kept
--  kill-anims + getSafePollRate/triggerSafePollBoost which are used elsewhere.)

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

-- ============================================================
-- DISABLE CHARACTER ANIMATIONS (always on, from script start)
-- Stops every currently-playing animation track and disables the
-- Animate LocalScript so no new ones start. Re-applies on every
-- respawn.
-- ============================================================
task.spawn(function()
    local function killAnims(char)
        if not char then return end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then
            local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
            if animator then
                for _, track in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                    pcall(function() track:Stop(0) end)
                end
            end
        end
        local animate = char:FindFirstChild("Animate")
        if animate and (animate:IsA("LocalScript") or animate:IsA("Script")) then
            pcall(function() animate.Disabled = true end)
        end
    end

    if LocalPlayer.Character then killAnims(LocalPlayer.Character) end
    LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
        task.wait(0.2)
        killAnims(c)
    end)
end)


local SAFE_POLL_RATE = 0.10
local SAFE_POLL_OVERRIDE_UNTIL = 0

function _G.getSafePollRate()
    if os.clock() < SAFE_POLL_OVERRIDE_UNTIL then
        return 0.27
    end
    return SAFE_POLL_RATE
end

function _G.triggerSafePollBoost()
    SAFE_POLL_OVERRIDE_UNTIL = os.clock() + 3
end


end

-- ============================================================
-- NET BYPASS (LKZ's fixed upvalue-patch version)
-- Walks every function inside the Net ModuleScript and rewrites
-- the "getfenv"/"getrenv" string slots in their upvalue tables
-- with a fake newcclosure that returns {}. Net's internal sandbox
-- detection reads those slots and proceeds normally instead of
-- nil-erroring at line 463. Replaces the older getfenv/setfenv
-- closure-swap bypass.
-- ============================================================
do
LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    if getgenv()._net_getfenv_patched then return end
    getgenv()._net_getfenv_patched = true

    local ok, NetModule = pcall(function()
        return require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
            :WaitForChild("Packages")
            :WaitForChild("Net")
            :FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ModuleScript", true))
    end)
    if not ok or type(NetModule) ~= "table" then return end

    local getupvalues = debug and debug.getupvalues or rawget(_G, "getupvalues")
    local getprotos   = debug and debug.getprotos   or rawget(_G, "getprotos")
    local newcc       = rawget(_G, "newcclosure") or function(fn) return fn end
    if not (getupvalues and getprotos) then return end

    local fake = newcc(function() return {} end)
    local scanned = {}

    local function patch(fn)
        if scanned[fn] then return end
        scanned[fn] = true

        local okUps, ups = pcall(getupvalues, fn)
        if okUps and ups then
            for _, up in pairs(ups) do
                if type(up) == "table" then
                    for k, v in pairs(up) do
                        if v == "getfenv" or v == "getrenv" then
                            pcall(function() up[k] = fake end)
                        elseif type(v) == "function" then
                            patch(v)
                        end
                    end
                elseif type(up) == "function" then
                    patch(up)
                end
            end
        end

        local okProtos, protos = pcall(getprotos, fn)
        if okProtos and protos then
            for _, proto in ipairs(protos) do patch(proto) end
        end
    end

    for _, v in pairs(NetModule) do
        if type(v) == "function" then patch(v) end
    end

    -- Same patch applied to Synchronizer (likely has the same internal
    -- getfenv/getrenv lookup that breaks on the patched server).
    local okSync, SyncModule = pcall(function()
        local s = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
            :WaitForChild("Packages")
            :FindFirstChild("Synchronizer")
        if not s then return nil end
        return require(s)
    end)
    if okSync and type(SyncModule) == "table" then
        for _, v in pairs(SyncModule) do
            if type(v) == "function" then patch(v) end
        end
    end
end)()
end

-- ============================================================
-- SYNCHRONIZER RESOLVERS (post-patch GetTableFromChannel path)
-- VanishGetSyncData(plot) returns the full data table from the
-- channel (AnimalList, Owner, etc.). VanishGetAnimalList(plot)
-- is a convenience that returns just the AnimalList. Both use
-- :GetTableFromChannel as the primary API (works on the patched
-- server) and fall back to the legacy :Get(plot):Get(key) path.
-- ============================================================
_G.VanishGetSyncData = _G.VanishGetSyncData or function(plot)
    local plotName = type(plot) == "string" and plot or (plot and plot.Name)
    if not plotName then return nil end
    local Pkgs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Packages")
    local Sync = Pkgs and Pkgs:FindFirstChild("Synchronizer")
    if not Sync then return nil end
    local okMod, mod = pcall(require, Sync)
    if not okMod or type(mod) ~= "table" then return nil end

    -- New API (post-patch) — returns the full data table at once.
    local okT, data = pcall(function() return mod:GetTableFromChannel(plotName) end)
    if okT and type(data) == "table" then return data end

    -- Legacy API fallback — synthesize a similar table by reading the
    -- channel one key at a time. Returns an object with both .AnimalList
    -- and a __channel field for callers that still need raw channel access.
    local okC, ch = pcall(function() return mod:Get(plotName) end)
    if okC and ch then
        local synth = { __channel = ch }
        pcall(function() synth.AnimalList = ch:Get("AnimalList") end)
        pcall(function() synth.Owner      = ch:Get("Owner") end)
        return synth
    end
    return nil
end

_G.VanishGetAnimalList = _G.VanishGetAnimalList or function(plot)
    local data = _G.VanishGetSyncData and _G.VanishGetSyncData(plot)
    return data and data.AnimalList or nil
end

-- ── Pre-cached ReplicatedStorage refs ─────────────────────────────────
-- The auto-TP startup spawn (and a handful of other spots) used to chain
-- ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Packages", 10) → WaitForChild("Datas", 5)
-- → … with 5-10s timeouts each. Worst-case 50s of script-yield right
-- after injection. These children all exist by the time game:IsLoaded()
-- returns true, so we just FindFirstChild them once up here. Downstream
-- spawns read _G._VanishPkgRefs.* directly — zero yield.
_G._VanishPkgRefs = _G._VanishPkgRefs or {}
do
    local refs = _G._VanishPkgRefs
    refs.Packages = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Packages")
    refs.Datas    = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Datas")
    refs.Shared   = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Shared")
    refs.Utils    = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Utils")
    -- If any of the above weren't ready yet, do ONE short WaitForChild
    -- (2s max) instead of the original 5-10s chain. In practice all four
    -- exist when the script runs, so this branch rarely fires.
    if not refs.Packages then refs.Packages = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Packages", 2) end
    if not refs.Datas    then refs.Datas    = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Datas",    2) end
    if not refs.Shared   then refs.Shared   = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared",   2) end
    if not refs.Utils    then refs.Utils    = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Utils",    2) end
end

_G.AntiDieDisabled = false
do
    local _earlyConn, _earlyDiedConn, _heartbeatConn
    local _hardenHumanoid = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(hum)
        pcall(function() hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = false end)
        pcall(function() hum.RequiresNeck = false end)
        pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false) end)
        pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false) end)
        pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false) end)
        pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics, false) end)
    end)
    local function _bindEarlyAntiDie()
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum then return end
        _hardenHumanoid(hum)
        if _earlyConn then pcall(function() _earlyConn:Disconnect() end) end
        if _earlyDiedConn then pcall(function() _earlyDiedConn:Disconnect() end) end
        if _heartbeatConn then pcall(function() _heartbeatConn:Disconnect() end) end
        _earlyConn = hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
            if hum.Health <= 0 then
                hum.Health = hum.MaxHealth
                pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end)
            end
        end)
        _earlyDiedConn = hum.Died:Connect(function()
            pcall(function() hum.Health = hum.MaxHealth end)
            pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end)
        end)
        local _adLastHarden = 0
        _heartbeatConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            if not hum or not hum.Parent then return end
            -- _hardenHumanoid sets persistent humanoid properties (RequiresNeck,
            -- SetStateEnabled, etc.) that stay set once written. Calling it every
            -- frame was ~6 pcall'd property writes wasted per frame. Re-apply
            -- only every 0.5s — the revival check below stays per-frame (cheap).
            local _adNow = os.clock()
            if _adNow - _adLastHarden >= 0.5 then
                _adLastHarden = _adNow
                _hardenHumanoid(hum)
            end
            if hum.Health <= 0 then
                hum.Health = hum.MaxHealth
                pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end)
            end
            local state = hum:GetState()
            if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
               or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
                pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end)
            end
        end))
        _G.AntiDieConnection = _earlyConn
    end
    _bindEarlyAntiDie()
    LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
        if hum then _hardenHumanoid(hum) end
        task.wait(0.1)
        _bindEarlyAntiDie()
    end)
end

local function _resolveCloneRemote(name)
    local Packages = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Packages", 5)
    local Net = Packages and Packages:WaitForChild("Net", 5)
    if not Net then return nil end
    local real
    pcall(function()
        local u2 = require(Net)
        local proxy = u2:RemoteEvent(name)
        local stringed = tostring(proxy)
        real = Net:FindFirstChild(stringed)
    end)
    if real and (real:IsA("RemoteEvent") or real:IsA("RemoteFunction")) then
        return real
    end
    return nil
end

_G.GetQuantumOnTeleportRemote = _G.GetQuantumOnTeleportRemote or function()
    if _G._QuantumOnTeleportRemote and _G._QuantumOnTeleportRemote.Parent then
        return _G._QuantumOnTeleportRemote
    end
    local r = _resolveCloneRemote("QuantumCloner/OnTeleport")
    if r then _G._QuantumOnTeleportRemote = r end
    return r
end

_G.GetQuantumOnActivateRemote = _G.GetQuantumOnActivateRemote or function()
    if _G._QuantumOnActivateRemote and _G._QuantumOnActivateRemote.Parent then
        return _G._QuantumOnActivateRemote
    end
    for _, name in ipairs({
        "QuantumCloner/OnActivate",
        "QuantumCloner/Activate",
        "QuantumCloner/OnSpawn",
        "QuantumCloner/Spawn",
        "QuantumCloner/OnUse",
        "QuantumCloner/Use",
    }) do
        local r = _resolveCloneRemote(name)
        if r then _G._QuantumOnActivateRemote = r; return r end
    end
    return nil
end

task.spawn(function()
    for attempt = 1, 8 do
        if _G.GetQuantumOnTeleportRemote() then break end
        task.wait(1)
    end
end)

local Decrypted
Decrypted = setmetatable({}, {
    __index = function(S, ez)
        local Netty = ReplicatedStorage.Packages.Net
        local prefix, path
        if     ez:sub(1,3) == "RE/" then prefix = "RE/";  path = ez:sub(4)
        elseif ez:sub(1,3) == "RF/" then prefix = "RF/";  path = ez:sub(4)
        else return nil end
        local Remote
        for i, v in Netty:GetChildren() do
            if v.Name == ez then
                Remote = Netty:GetChildren()[i + 1]
                break
            end
        end
        if Remote and not rawget(Decrypted, ez) then rawset(Decrypted, ez, Remote) end
        return rawget(Decrypted, ez)
    end
})
local Utility = {}
function Utility:LarpNet(F) return Decrypted[F] end
local Camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
local Mouse = LocalPlayer:GetMouse()

local function isMobile()
    return UserInputService.TouchEnabled and not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled and not UserInputService.MouseEnabled
end

local IS_MOBILE = isMobile()

if not _G.VanishRenderBrainrotViewport then
    _G.VanishRenderBrainrotViewport = function(viewport, brainrotName, animated)
        if not viewport or not brainrotName then return end
        local clone
        local shared
        pcall(function()
            local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Shared")
            shared = Shared and require(Shared:FindFirstChild("Animals"))
        end)
        if animated and shared and shared.GetAnimatedModel then
            local ok, m = pcall(function() return shared:GetAnimatedModel(brainrotName) end)
            if ok and m then clone = m end
        end
        if not clone then
            local Models = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Models")
            local Animals = Models and Models:FindFirstChild("Animals")
            local src = Animals and Animals:FindFirstChild(brainrotName)
            if not src and Animals then
                local lower = string.lower(brainrotName)
                for _, c in ipairs(Animals:GetChildren()) do
                    if string.lower(c.Name) == lower then src = c; break end
                end
            end
            if src and src:IsA("Model") then clone = src:Clone() end
        end
        if not clone then return end
        for _, d in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") or d:IsA("BillboardGui") or d:IsA("Highlight")
               or d:IsA("Beam") or d:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or d:IsA("Trail")
               or d:IsA("Script") then
                d:Destroy()
            end
        end
        if not clone.PrimaryPart then
            local largest, largestVol = nil, 0
            for _, d in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("BasePart") then
                    local v = d.Size.X * d.Size.Y * d.Size.Z
                    if v > largestVol then largest = d; largestVol = v end
                end
            end
            if largest then clone.PrimaryPart = largest end
        end
        if clone:IsA("Model") and clone.PrimaryPart then
            pcall(function()
                clone:PivotTo(CFrame.new(0, 0, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(180), 0))
            end)
        end
        if animated then
            local worldModel = Instance.new("WorldModel")
            worldModel.Parent = viewport
            clone.Parent = worldModel
        else
            for _, d in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("BasePart") then
                    d.Anchored = true
                    d.CanCollide = false
                    d.Massless = true
                end
                if d:IsA("LocalScript") or d:IsA("ModuleScript") or d:IsA("Animator") or d:IsA("AnimationController") then
                    pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
                end
            end
            clone.Parent = viewport
        end
        local cam = Instance.new("Camera")
        cam.FieldOfView = 45
        cam.Parent = viewport
        viewport.CurrentCamera = cam
        viewport.LightDirection = Vector3.new(-0.4, -1, -0.6).Unit
        viewport.LightColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        viewport.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(170, 180, 200)
        local ok, cf, size = pcall(function() return clone:GetBoundingBox() end)
        if ok and cf and size then
            local center = cf.Position
            local extent = math.max(size.X, size.Y, size.Z)
            local zoom = 2.0
            if shared and shared.GetZoom then
                local okz, z = pcall(function() return shared:GetZoom(brainrotName) end)
                if okz and type(z) == "number" and z > 0 then zoom = z * 1.4 end
            end
            local dist = extent * zoom + 4
            local camPos = center + Vector3.new(dist * 0.55, dist * 0.4, dist * 0.85)
            cam.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(camPos, center)
        else
            cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(Vector3.new(6, 4, 8), Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
        end
    end
end

local function DeepCopy(tbl)
    if type(tbl) ~= "table" then return tbl end
    local out = {}
    for k, v in pairs(tbl) do
        out[k] = DeepCopy(v)
    end
    return out
end

local function MergeDefaults(target, defaults)
    for k, v in pairs(defaults) do
        if type(v) == "table" then
            if type(target[k]) ~= "table" then
                target[k] = DeepCopy(v)
            else
                MergeDefaults(target[k], v)
            end
        elseif target[k] == nil then
            target[k] = v
        end
    end
end

local Config = DeepCopy(DefaultConfig)

local function NormalizeKeyName(keyName, fallback)
    if type(keyName) ~= "string" or keyName == "" then
        return fallback
    end

    local aliases = {
        ALT = "LeftAlt",
        LALT = "LeftAlt",
        RALT = "RightAlt",
        CTRL = "LeftControl",
        CONTROL = "LeftControl",
        LCTRL = "LeftControl",
        RCTRL = "RightControl",
        SHIFT = "LeftShift",
        LSHIFT = "LeftShift",
        RSHIFT = "RightShift",
        WIN = "LeftSuper",
        CMD = "LeftSuper",
        META = "LeftSuper",
    }

    local upper = string.upper(keyName)
    local normalized = aliases[upper] or keyName
    return Enum.KeyCode[normalized] and normalized or fallback
end

local function PrettyKeyName(keyName)
    local map = {
        LeftAlt = "ALT",
        RightAlt = "RALT",
        LeftControl = "CTRL",
        RightControl = "RCTRL",
        LeftShift = "SHIFT",
        RightShift = "RSHIFT",
        LeftSuper = "WIN",
        RightSuper = "RWIN",
    }
    return map[keyName] or tostring(keyName or "")
end

if isfile and isfile(FileName) then
    pcall(function()
        local raw = readfile(FileName)
        if not raw or raw == "" then return end
        local decoded = HttpService:JSONDecode(raw)
        if type(decoded) ~= "table" then return end
        local savedPriorityList = nil
        if type(decoded.PriorityList) == "table" then
            savedPriorityList = DeepCopy(decoded.PriorityList)
        end
        MergeDefaults(decoded, DefaultConfig)
        if savedPriorityList ~= nil then
            decoded.PriorityList = savedPriorityList
        end
        Config = decoded
    end)
end
