    Config.CancelAPPanelOnMoby = true
end

-- Sync legacy TpV2NoAP to TeleportMethod at load time so runAutoSnipe's
-- `_useV2 = Config.TpV2NoAP == true` check picks the AP path on join.
-- (UI dropdown rebuild also re-syncs, but that fires after the auto-TP
-- dispatcher may have already called runAutoSnipe.)
do
    local valid = { ["Tween Teleport"] = true }
    if type(Config.TeleportMethod) ~= "string" or not valid[Config.TeleportMethod] then
        Config.TeleportMethod = "Tween Teleport"
    end
    Config.TpV2NoAP = (Config.TeleportMethod == "Tween Teleport")
end

-- Returns the saved accent color from config, or the default blue if none saved.
-- Called at build-time by external UIs so their initial colors match the saved theme.
local function GetVanishAccent()
    if type(Config.UIAccentColor) == "table" then
        local r = type(Config.UIAccentColor.R) == "number" and Config.UIAccentColor.R or 0
        local g = type(Config.UIAccentColor.G) == "number" and Config.UIAccentColor.G or (170/255)
        local b = type(Config.UIAccentColor.B) == "number" and Config.UIAccentColor.B or 1
        return Color3.new(math.clamp(r,0,1), math.clamp(g,0,1), math.clamp(b,0,1))
    end
    return Color3.fromRGB(0, 170, 255)
end
if type(Config.CancelAPPanelOnKawaifu) ~= "boolean" then
    Config.CancelAPPanelOnKawaifu = true
end
if type(Config.CancelAPPanelOnAtlas) ~= "boolean" then
    Config.CancelAPPanelOnAtlas = true
end

-- Startup gate disabled — always allow continuous loops to run.
_G.VanishStartupReady   = true
_G.VanishAutoSnipeFired = false

if Config.TpSettings.TpOnLoad then
    _G._autoTPTurboPending = true
    _G.AutoTPEnabledFromSave = true

    task.spawn(function()
        if not game:IsLoaded() then game.Loaded:Wait() end

        local _lp = LocalPlayer
        local _char = _lp.Character or _lp.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        local _hrp = _char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
        local _hum = _char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
        if not _hrp or not _hum then
            _G._autoTPTurboPending = false
            return
        end

        local _Sync, _AnimalsData, _AnimalsShared, _NumberUtils
        pcall(function()
            -- Use top-level cache instead of chaining 6 WaitForChild calls
            -- (which previously could yield up to 50s on slow joins).
            local refs = _G._VanishPkgRefs or {}
            local Pkgs   = refs.Packages or ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Packages", 2)
            local Datas  = refs.Datas    or ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Datas",    2)
            local Shared = refs.Shared   or ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared",   2)
            local Utils  = refs.Utils    or ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Utils",    2)
            _Sync          = require(Pkgs:WaitForChild("Synchronizer", 2))
            _AnimalsData   = require(Datas:WaitForChild("Animals", 2))
            _AnimalsShared = require(Shared:WaitForChild("Animals", 2))
            _NumberUtils   = require(Utils:WaitForChild("NumberUtils", 2))
        end)
        if not _Sync then
            _G._autoTPTurboPending = false
            return
        end

        local _plots = Workspace:WaitForChild("Plots", 10)
        if not _plots then
            _G._autoTPTurboPending = false
            return
        end

        local function _parseMinGenInline(v)
            if type(v) == "number" then
                if v <= 0 then return 0 end
                return v * 1e6
            end
            if not v or v == "" then return 0 end
            local raw = tostring(v):lower():gsub(",", "")
            local num = tonumber(raw:match("([%d%.]+)"))
            if not num then return 0 end
            local suf = raw:match("[a-z]+$")
            if suf == "k" then num = num * 1e3
            elseif suf == "b" then num = num * 1e9
            elseif suf == "t" then num = num * 1e12
            else num = num * 1e6 end
            return num
        end
        local _minGen = _parseMinGenInline(Config.TpSettings.MinGenForTp)

        -- (_scanAllPlots removed — was dead code, zero call sites. The live
        --  scanner used by this block is _scanAllOwned below.)

        do
            local _prDeadline = os.clock() + 3
            while os.clock() < _prDeadline do
                local p = _G.PRIORITY_LIST or _G.SHARED_PRIORITY_ITEMS
                if p and #p > 0 then break end
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
        end

        --> ( Pure highest-gen pick — always returns the brainrot with the
        --    largest genValue across ALL owned plots. No priority list,
        --    no name filtering — most valuable wins. )
        local function _pickTarget(cache)
            local best, bestVal = nil, -math.huge
            for _, p in ipairs(cache) do
                if type(p.genValue) == "number" and p.genValue > bestVal then
                    best = p; bestVal = p.genValue
                end
            end
            return best
        end

        --> ( Accurate scan: tries the live PlotClient (getgc) first per plot
        --    for the most up-to-date AnimalList, then falls back to Synchronizer.
        --    Only owned plots (Owner is a real Player) are included. )
        local _Players = game:GetService("Players")
        -- LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE: getgc(true) returns a HUGE table; iterating it
        -- under Luraph's VM is ~50-200x slower than native. This is one of
        -- the biggest startup freeze contributors. Native execution drops
        -- the per-plot scan from ~hundreds of ms to <10 ms.
        local _liveClient = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(plot)
            if not getgc then return nil end
            for _, v in ipairs(getgc(true)) do
                if type(v) == "table"
                    and rawget(v, "PlotModel") == plot
                    and rawget(v, "Channel")
                    and rawget(v, "AnimalsModels")
                    and rawget(v, "AnimalsPrompts")
                then return v end
            end
            return nil
        end)
        local function _scanPlot(plot, out)
            pcall(function()
                local lc = _liveClient(plot)
                local al = lc and rawget(lc, "AnimalList")
                local ownerObj = lc and (rawget(lc, "Owner") or rawget(lc, "OwnerName"))
                if typeof(al) ~= "table" or not ownerObj then
                    local data = _G.VanishGetSyncData and _G.VanishGetSyncData(plot.Name)
                    if not data then return end
                    al = al or data.AnimalList
                    ownerObj = ownerObj or data.Owner
                end
                local ownerName
                if typeof(ownerObj) == "Instance" and ownerObj:IsA("Player") then ownerName = ownerObj.Name
                elseif type(ownerObj) == "table" and ownerObj.Name then ownerName = ownerObj.Name
                elseif type(ownerObj) == "string" then ownerName = ownerObj end
                if not ownerName or ownerName == _lp.Name or not _Players:FindFirstChild(ownerName) then return end
                if _G.VanishIsDuelBlocking and _G.VanishIsDuelBlocking(ownerName) then return end
                if typeof(al) ~= "table" then return end
                for slot, ad in pairs(al) do
                    if type(ad) == "table" and ad.Index then repeat
                        if _G.VanishIsFusing and _G.VanishIsFusing(ad) then break end
                        local info = _AnimalsData[ad.Index]
                        if not info then break end
                        local gv = 0
                        pcall(function() gv = _AnimalsShared:GetGeneration(ad.Index, ad.Mutation, ad.Traits, nil) or 0 end)
                        if type(gv) ~= "number" or gv < 1 then break end
                        if _minGen > 0 and gv < _minGen then break end
                        out[#out + 1] = {
                            name = info.DisplayName or ad.Index,
                            Index = ad.Index,
                            genText = "$" .. _NumberUtils:ToString(gv) .. "/s",
                            genValue = gv,
                            mutation = ad.Mutation or "None",
                            owner = ownerName,
                            plot = plot.Name,
                            slot = tostring(slot),
                            uid = plot.Name .. "_" .. tostring(slot),
                        }
                    until true end
                end
            end)
        end

        --> ( Multi-pass scan: scan ALL plots in parallel via task.spawn so
        --    Synchronizer:Get yields don't serialize. Aggregate results into
        --    one cache; pick the highest. Retry up to 1.5s if nothing yet. )
        local function _scanAllOwned()
            local out, threads = {}, {}
            for _, plot in ipairs(_plots:GetChildren()) do
                threads[#threads + 1] = task.spawn(function() _scanPlot(plot, out) end)
            end
            return out
        end

        -- Longer scan window (was 2.5s -> 6s) so slow Synchronizer
        -- populates don't cause auto-TP to silently bail on join.
        local _chosen
        local _deadline = os.clock() + 6
        repeat
            local _cache = _scanAllOwned()
            task.wait(0.1)
            if #_cache > 0 then
                _chosen = _pickTarget(_cache)
                if _chosen then break end
            end
            task.wait(0.05)
        until os.clock() >= _deadline

        -- DON'T bail on no _chosen. runAutoSnipe has its own fresh
        -- inline scanner — call it anyway and let it find a target
        -- with whatever data is current at fire time.
        local _selected
        if _chosen then
            _selected = {
                petName = _chosen.name, name = _chosen.name,
                mpsText = _chosen.genText, mpsValue = _chosen.genValue,
                genText = _chosen.genText, genValue = _chosen.genValue,
                owner = _chosen.owner, plot = _chosen.plot, slot = _chosen.slot,
                uid = _chosen.uid, mutation = _chosen.mutation, Index = _chosen.Index,
                animalData = _chosen,
            }
        end

        local _waitFn = os.clock() + 25
        while not _G.runAutoSnipe and os.clock() < _waitFn do task.wait(0.05) end

        if SharedState and _selected then SharedState.SelectedPetData = _selected end

        local _joinDelay = tonumber(Config.TpSettings.TpJoinDelay) or 0
        if _joinDelay > 0 then task.wait(_joinDelay) end

        if _G.runAutoSnipe then
            -- Retry up to 3 times if first call yields no movement.
            for attempt = 1, 3 do
                local ok = pcall(_G.runAutoSnipe)
                if ok then break end
                task.wait(0.5)
            end
        else
        end

        _G._autoTPTurboPending = false
        _G._priorityAutoTPDone = true
        _G._autoTPTurboPending = false
        _G._firstTPDone = true
    end)
end

function EnsureSavedKeybinds()
    Config.SavedKeybinds = Config.SavedKeybinds or {}
    Config.SavedKeybinds.MenuKey = NormalizeKeyName(Config.SavedKeybinds.MenuKey or Config.MenuKey, DefaultConfig.MenuKey or "LeftControl")
    Config.SavedKeybinds.ClickToAPKeybind = NormalizeKeyName(Config.SavedKeybinds.ClickToAPKeybind or Config.ClickToAPKeybind, DefaultConfig.ClickToAPKeybind or "L")
    Config.SavedKeybinds.ProximityAPKeybind = NormalizeKeyName(Config.SavedKeybinds.ProximityAPKeybind or Config.ProximityAPKeybind, DefaultConfig.ProximityAPKeybind or "P")
    Config.SavedKeybinds.StealSpeedKey = NormalizeKeyName(Config.SavedKeybinds.StealSpeedKey or Config.StealSpeedKey, DefaultConfig.StealSpeedKey or "C")
    Config.SavedKeybinds.FloatKeybind = NormalizeKeyName(Config.SavedKeybinds.FloatKeybind or Config.FloatKeybind, DefaultConfig.FloatKeybind or "Z")
    Config.SavedKeybinds.ResetKey = NormalizeKeyName(Config.SavedKeybinds.ResetKey or Config.ResetKey, DefaultConfig.ResetKey or "X")
    Config.SavedKeybinds.JobJoinerKey = NormalizeKeyName(Config.SavedKeybinds.JobJoinerKey or Config.JobJoinerKey, DefaultConfig.JobJoinerKey or "J")
    Config.SavedKeybinds.InvisToggleKey = NormalizeKeyName(Config.SavedKeybinds.InvisToggleKey or Config.InvisToggleKey, DefaultConfig.InvisToggleKey or "I")
    Config.SavedKeybinds.KickKey = NormalizeKeyName(Config.SavedKeybinds.KickKey or Config.KickKey, "")
    Config.SavedKeybinds.RagdollSelfKey = NormalizeKeyName(Config.SavedKeybinds.RagdollSelfKey or Config.RagdollSelfKey, "")
    Config.SavedKeybinds.RejoinKey = NormalizeKeyName(Config.SavedKeybinds.RejoinKey or Config.RejoinKey, "")
    Config.SavedKeybinds.TpKey = NormalizeKeyName(Config.SavedKeybinds.TpKey or Config.TpSettings.TpKey, DefaultConfig.TpSettings.TpKey or "T")
    Config.SavedKeybinds.CloneKey = NormalizeKeyName(Config.SavedKeybinds.CloneKey or Config.TpSettings.CloneKey, DefaultConfig.TpSettings.CloneKey or "V")
    Config.SavedKeybinds.CarpetSpeedKey = NormalizeKeyName(Config.SavedKeybinds.CarpetSpeedKey or Config.TpSettings.CarpetSpeedKey, DefaultConfig.TpSettings.CarpetSpeedKey or "Q")
end

function ApplySavedKeybindsToConfig()
    EnsureSavedKeybinds()
    Config.MenuKey = Config.SavedKeybinds.MenuKey
    Config.ClickToAPKeybind = Config.SavedKeybinds.ClickToAPKeybind
    Config.ProximityAPKeybind = Config.SavedKeybinds.ProximityAPKeybind
    Config.StealSpeedKey = Config.SavedKeybinds.StealSpeedKey
    Config.FloatKeybind = Config.SavedKeybinds.FloatKeybind
    Config.ResetKey = Config.SavedKeybinds.ResetKey
    Config.JobJoinerKey = Config.SavedKeybinds.JobJoinerKey
    Config.InvisToggleKey = Config.SavedKeybinds.InvisToggleKey
    Config.KickKey = Config.SavedKeybinds.KickKey
    Config.RagdollSelfKey = Config.SavedKeybinds.RagdollSelfKey
    Config.RejoinKey = Config.SavedKeybinds.RejoinKey
    Config.TpSettings.TpKey = Config.SavedKeybinds.TpKey
    Config.TpSettings.CloneKey = Config.SavedKeybinds.CloneKey
    Config.TpSettings.CarpetSpeedKey = Config.SavedKeybinds.CarpetSpeedKey
end

local function getAutoSnipeDelayFromOption(option)
    local map = {
        [1] = 0.10,
        [2] = 0.20,
        [3] = 0.30,
        [4] = 0.40,
    }
    return map[tonumber(option)] or 0.10
end

function NormalizeAllKeybinds()
    EnsureSavedKeybinds()
    ApplySavedKeybindsToConfig()
end

NormalizeAllKeybinds()

local function SaveConfig()
    if writefile then
        pcall(function()
            NormalizeAllKeybinds()
            local toSave = DeepCopy(Config)
            toSave.ProximityAP = false
            toSave.MenuKey = Config.SavedKeybinds.MenuKey
            toSave.ClickToAPKeybind = Config.SavedKeybinds.ClickToAPKeybind
            toSave.ProximityAPKeybind = Config.SavedKeybinds.ProximityAPKeybind
            toSave.StealSpeedKey = Config.SavedKeybinds.StealSpeedKey
            toSave.FloatKeybind = Config.SavedKeybinds.FloatKeybind
            toSave.ResetKey = Config.SavedKeybinds.ResetKey
            toSave.JobJoinerKey = Config.SavedKeybinds.JobJoinerKey
            toSave.InvisToggleKey = Config.SavedKeybinds.InvisToggleKey
            toSave.KickKey = Config.SavedKeybinds.KickKey
            toSave.RagdollSelfKey = Config.SavedKeybinds.RagdollSelfKey
            toSave.RejoinKey = Config.SavedKeybinds.RejoinKey
            toSave.TpSettings.TpKey = Config.SavedKeybinds.TpKey
            toSave.TpSettings.CloneKey = Config.SavedKeybinds.CloneKey
            toSave.TpSettings.CarpetSpeedKey = Config.SavedKeybinds.CarpetSpeedKey
            writefile(FileName, HttpService:JSONEncode(toSave))
        end)
    end
end

local function isMobyUser(player)
    if not player or not player.Character then return false end
    return player.Character:FindFirstChild("_moby_highlight") ~= nil
end

local HighlightName = "KaWaifu_NeonHighlight"
local function isKawaifuUser(player)
    if not player or not player.Character then return false end
    return player.Character:FindFirstChild(HighlightName) ~= nil
end

local function isAtlasUser(player)
    if not player then return false end
    local folder = Workspace:FindFirstChild("AtlasESPFolder")
    if not folder then return false end
    local pe = folder:FindFirstChild(player.Name .. "_ESP")
    if not pe then return false end
    local hl = pe:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Highlight")
    if hl and hl.FillColor == Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0) then return true end
    return false
end
_G._isAtlasUser = isAtlasUser

_G.InvisStealAngle = Config.InvisStealAngle
_G.SinkSliderValue = Config.SinkSliderValue
_G.AutoRecoverLagback = Config.AutoRecoverLagback
_G.AutoInvisDuringSteal = Config.AutoInvisDuringSteal
    _G.INVISIBLE_STEAL_KEY = Enum.KeyCode[Config.InvisToggleKey] or Enum.KeyCode.I
_G.invisibleStealEnabled = false
_G.RecoveryInProgress = false

local function getControls()
	local playerScripts = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts")
	local playerModule = require(playerScripts:WaitForChild("PlayerModule"))
	return playerModule:GetControls()
end

local Controls = getControls()

local function kickPlayer()
    local ok = pcall(function()
        game:Shutdown()
    end)
    if ok then return end
    pcall(function()
        LocalPlayer:Kick("")
    end)
end

local function walkForward(seconds)
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local hum = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local Controls = getControls()
    local lookVector = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
    Controls:Disable()
    local startTime = os.clock()
    local conn
    conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        if os.clock() - startTime >= seconds then
            conn:Disconnect()
            hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
            Controls:Enable()
            return
        end
        hum:Move(lookVector, false)
    end))
end

-- ============================================================
-- INPUT DISPATCH HELPERS (Net-bypass: firesignal / VirtualInputManager)
-- Used by Quantum Cloner activation + Admin Panel button clicks since
-- the game's Net package was patched. VIM is *created* via Instance.new
-- per executor compatibility — never stored as a service reference.
-- ============================================================
local function _newVIM()
    local ok, vim = pcall(Instance.new, "VirtualInputManager")
    if ok and vim then return vim end
    return nil
end

-- Fire a click on a GuiButton. `method` is "FireSignal" or
-- "VirtualInputManager". Falls back to the other method if the chosen
-- one is unavailable on this executor.
local function fireGuiClick(btn, method)
    if not btn or not btn.Parent then return false end
    method = method or Config.CloneMethod or "FireSignal"

    local function tryFireSignal()
        if typeof(firesignal) ~= "function" then return false end
        local ok = pcall(function()
            if btn.MouseButton1Down  then firesignal(btn.MouseButton1Down)  end
            if btn.MouseButton1Up    then firesignal(btn.MouseButton1Up)    end
            if btn.MouseButton1Click then firesignal(btn.MouseButton1Click) end
            if btn.Activated         then firesignal(btn.Activated)         end
        end)
        return ok
    end

    local function tryVIM()
        local vim = _newVIM()
        if not vim then return false end
        -- Resolve the screen pixel position of the button's center.
        -- VIM uses absolute screen coords (top-left of the whole window
        -- including the topbar). Roblox's GuiObject.AbsolutePosition is
        -- the position INSIDE the GUI inset when the parent ScreenGui has
        -- IgnoreGuiInset=false (default) — so we ADD GuiService:GetGuiInset()
        -- back in. When IgnoreGuiInset=true, AbsolutePosition is already in
        -- absolute screen coords and we add nothing.
        local addX, addY = 0, 0
        local sg = btn:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("ScreenGui")
        if not sg or sg.IgnoreGuiInset == false then
            local okInset, inset = pcall(function()
                return game:GetService("GuiService"):GetGuiInset()
            end)
            if okInset and inset then
                addX, addY = inset.X, inset.Y
            end
        end
        local cx = btn.AbsolutePosition.X + btn.AbsoluteSize.X * 0.5 + addX
        local cy = btn.AbsolutePosition.Y + btn.AbsoluteSize.Y * 0.5 + addY
        local ok = pcall(function()
            -- Move cursor first so any GuiObject under it gets MouseEnter
            -- processed before the down/up sequence. Some UIs gate clicks
            -- behind a hover state.
            vim:SendMouseMoveEvent(cx, cy, game)
            vim:SendMouseButtonEvent(cx, cy, 0, true,  game, 0)
            task.wait(0.03)  -- small gap so down + up register as a click
            vim:SendMouseButtonEvent(cx, cy, 0, false, game, 0)
        end)
        return ok
    end

    if method == "VirtualInputManager" then
        if tryVIM() then return true end
        return tryFireSignal()
    end
    if tryFireSignal() then return true end
    return tryVIM()
end
_G.fireGuiClick = fireGuiClick

-- Fire the QuantumCloner/OnTeleport remote directly (legacy "Remote"
-- method). Uses the existing _G.GetQuantumOnTeleportRemote resolver
-- (which uses the patched Net bypass). Returns true if the FireServer
-- call succeeded. Falls back caller-side if not.
local function _fireCloneRemoteSwap()
    local r = _G.GetQuantumOnTeleportRemote and _G.GetQuantumOnTeleportRemote()
    if not r then return false end
    local ok = pcall(function() r:FireServer() end)
    return ok
end

-- Resolve the Quantum Cloner's TeleportToClone button in PlayerGui
-- (the in-game UI button the cloner spawns to let you swap with the
-- placed clone). Forces the WHOLE parent chain Visible=true so the
-- click lands even if the engine has the panel hidden.
local function _getCloneTeleportButton()
    local pg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    if not pg then return nil end
    local tf = pg:FindFirstChild("ToolsFrames")
    if not tf then return nil end
    local qc = tf:FindFirstChild("QuantumCloner")
    if not qc then return nil end
    local btn = qc:FindFirstChild("TeleportToClone")
    if not btn then return nil end
    -- Force the entire parent chain visible — a single Visible=true on the
    -- button is useless if any ancestor is hidden. Also bump ScreenGui
    -- Enabled true (sometimes the panel ScreenGui is Enabled=false).
    pcall(function()
        local cur = btn
        while cur and cur ~= pg do
            if cur:IsA("GuiObject") then cur.Visible = true end
            if cur:IsA("ScreenGui")  then cur.Enabled = true end
            cur = cur.Parent
        end
    end)
    return btn
end

-- Unified swap dispatcher honoring Config.CloneMethod.
--   "Remote"              → fire QuantumCloner/OnTeleport remote
--   "FireSignal"          → firesignal on TeleportToClone GUI button
--   "VirtualInputManager" → VIM SendMouseButtonEvent on the button
-- Returns true on success. Falls back to GUI-click if "Remote" fails.
local function fireCloneSwap()
    local method = Config.CloneMethod or "FireSignal"
    if method == "Remote" then
        if _fireCloneRemoteSwap() then return true end
        -- Fall through to GUI click if the remote resolver failed.
    end
    local btn = _getCloneTeleportButton()
    if btn then
        return fireGuiClick(btn, method == "Remote" and "FireSignal" or method)
    end
    return false
end
_G.fireCloneSwap = fireCloneSwap

local function instantClone()
    if not LocalPlayer then return end
    if _G.isCloning then return end
    _G.isCloning = true
    local ok = pcall(function()
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not humanoid then return end

        local cloner = LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Quantum Cloner")
            or character:FindFirstChild("Quantum Cloner")
        if not cloner then return end

        _G.SuppressQuantumClonerEquipUntil = os.clock() + 1

        if cloner.Parent ~= character then
            pcall(function() humanoid:UnequipTools() end)
            humanoid:EquipTool(cloner)
            local equipDeadline = os.clock() + 0.2
            while cloner.Parent ~= character and os.clock() < equipDeadline do
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            end
        end

        local cloneName = tostring(LocalPlayer.UserId) .. "_Clone"
        local oldClone = Workspace:FindFirstChild(cloneName)

        local function fireActivate()
            -- Cover every activation path: native :Activate(), the tool's
            -- UseItem RemoteEvent (if present), and firesignal on .Activated.
            pcall(function() cloner:Activate() end)
            local ui = cloner:FindFirstChild("UseItem")
            if ui then
                if ui:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                    pcall(function() ui:FireServer() end)
                elseif ui:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
                    pcall(function() ui:InvokeServer() end)
                end
            end
            if typeof(firesignal) == "function" and cloner.Activated then
                pcall(function() firesignal(cloner.Activated) end)
            end
        end
        local function fireSwap()
            fireCloneSwap()
        end

        for _ = 1, 3 do fireActivate() end

        local cloneInst
        local nextRetry = os.clock() + 0.05
        local deadline = os.clock() + 1.5
        while os.clock() < deadline do
            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            local found = Workspace:FindFirstChild(cloneName)

            if found and found ~= oldClone then cloneInst = found; break end
            if os.clock() >= nextRetry then
                nextRetry = os.clock() + 0.05
                if cloner.Parent ~= character then
                    pcall(function() humanoid:EquipTool(cloner) end)
                end
                fireActivate()
            end
        end

        -- Fire the swap regardless of whether we detected a new clone.
        -- Reason: if an old clone is already out there from a previous
        -- activation, the swap can use that. Previously we early-returned
        -- on `not cloneInst` which made the keybind a no-op in that case.
        fireSwap()

        if _G.triggerSafePollBoost then
            _G.triggerSafePollBoost()
        end
    end)
    _G.isCloning = false

    -- TP After Clone: call the global handler (defined later in the file
    -- where findAdorneeGlobal and SharedState are already in scope).
    if _G.triggerTpAfterClone then
        _G.triggerTpAfterClone()
    end
end

-- ============================================================
-- ADMIN-COMMAND-BYPASS HELPERS
-- Used by auto-snipe when player owns the AdminCommands gamepass.
-- Flow: ragdoll self -> TP to brainrot -> place clone (no swap) ->
-- instant reset -> swap with clone (lands on brainrot) -> final TP.
-- ============================================================

_G.instantClone = instantClone

-- Swap with the existing clone using whichever method is selected
-- (Remote / FireSignal / VirtualInputManager).
local function swapWithClone()
    return fireCloneSwap()
end
_G.swapWithClone = swapWithClone

local function waitSecondsHeartbeat(sec)
    local t = 0
    while t < sec do
        t = t + RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
    end
end

local function waitUntilHeartbeat(predicate, timeoutSec)
    local t = 0
    while true do
        if predicate() then return true end
        local dt = RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
        t = t + dt
        if timeoutSec and t >= timeoutSec then
            return false
        end
    end
end

local function getClosestBaseSign(brainrotPart)
    if not brainrotPart or not brainrotPart:IsA("BasePart") then return nil end

    local closestPart = nil
    local closestDist = math.huge

    for _, label in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if label:IsA("TextLabel") then
            local txt = tostring(label.Text or "")
            if txt ~= "" and txt:lower():find("base", 1, true) then
                local gui = label:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("SurfaceGui")
                if gui then
                    local part = gui.Adornee or gui.Parent
                    if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local dist = (part.Position - brainrotPart.Position).Magnitude
                        if dist < closestDist then
                            closestDist = dist
                            closestPart = part
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return closestPart
end

local function riseToY(hrp, targetY)
    if not hrp then return end
    local MAX_TIME = 3.0
    local start = os.clock()

    local char = hrp.Parent
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end) end

    while hrp.Parent and hrp.Position.Y < targetY do
        local dist = targetY - hrp.Position.Y
        local step = math.min(dist, 4)
        hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, step, 0)
        if os.clock() - start > MAX_TIME then
            break
        end
        RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
    end
end

local function equipTpToolAndWait(hum)
    if not hum then return nil end

    local char = hum.Parent
    local toolName = Config.TpSettings.Tool or "Flying Carpet"
    local tool = LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild(toolName) or (char and char:FindFirstChild(toolName))

    if tool then
        if tool.Parent ~= char then
            hum:EquipTool(tool)
        else
            hum:EquipTool(tool)
        end
        task.wait(0.02)
    end

    return tool
end

local function prepMiniTpTool(hum, hrp)
    if not hum or not hrp then return end

    local char = hum.Parent
    local toolName = Config.TpSettings.Tool or "Flying Carpet"
    local tool = LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild(toolName) or (char and char:FindFirstChild(toolName))
    if not tool then return end

end

local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

local TP_V2_MED_POINTS = {
    {name = "MED1", pos = Vector3.new(-410.65, -5.68, -46.10)},
    {name = "MED2", pos = Vector3.new(-410.91, -5.68, 168.89)},
}

local TP_V2_SECOND_FLOOR_POINTS = {
    {name = "TP1",  pos = Vector3.new(-488.88, 15, 196.38), facing = "back"},
    {name = "TP2",  pos = Vector3.new(-487.79, 15, 138.13), facing = "front"},
    {name = "TP3",  pos = Vector3.new(-489.38, 15, 89.23),  facing = "back"},
    {name = "TP4",  pos = Vector3.new(-489.69, 15, 30.98),  facing = "front"},
    {name = "TP5",  pos = Vector3.new(-488.75, 15, -17.95), facing = "back"},
    {name = "TP6",  pos = Vector3.new(-490, 15, -75.90), facing = "front"},
    {name = "TP7",  pos = Vector3.new(-331.75, 15, -75.80), facing = "back"},
    {name = "TP8",  pos = Vector3.new(-329.98, 15, -18.16), facing = "front"},
    {name = "TP9",  pos = Vector3.new(-330.04, 15, 31.14),  facing = "back"},
    {name = "TP10", pos = Vector3.new(-331.28, 15, 88.92),  facing = "front"},
    {name = "TP11", pos = Vector3.new(-330.57, 15, 138.10), facing = "back"},
    {name = "TP12", pos = Vector3.new(-330.01, 15, 195.96), facing = "front"},
}

local function flatDistance(a, b)
    return (Vector3.new(a.X, 0, a.Z) - Vector3.new(b.X, 0, b.Z)).Magnitude
end

local function getClosestBaseSignToPosition(worldPos)
    local closestPart = nil
    local closestDist = math.huge

    for _, label in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if label:IsA("TextLabel") then
            local txt = tostring(label.Text or "")
            if txt ~= "" and txt:lower():find("base", 1, true) then
                local gui = label:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("SurfaceGui")
                if gui then
                    local part = gui.Adornee or gui.Parent
                    if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local dist = (part.Position - worldPos).Magnitude
                        if dist < closestDist then
                            closestDist = dist
                            closestPart = part
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return closestPart
end

local function getNearestTeleportV2MedPoint(fromPos)
    local bestPoint = nil
    local bestDist = math.huge

    for _, entry in ipairs(TP_V2_MED_POINTS) do
        local dist = flatDistance(fromPos, entry.pos)
        if dist < bestDist then
            bestDist = dist
            bestPoint = entry
        end
    end

    return bestPoint, bestDist
end

local TP_V2_ALLOWED_BY_MED = {
    MED1 = {
        TP6 = true, TP7 = true, TP8 = true, TP10 = true,
        TP12 = true, TP5 = true, TP3 = true, TP1 = true,
    },
    MED2 = {
        TP1 = true, TP2 = true, TP4 = true, TP6 = true,
        TP7 = true, TP9 = true, TP11 = true, TP12 = true,
    },
}

local function getBestTeleportV2SecondFloorPoint(medPoint, brainrotPos)
    local medPos = medPoint and medPoint.pos
    local medName = medPoint and medPoint.name
    if not medPos or not medName then return nil end

    local allowed = TP_V2_ALLOWED_BY_MED[medName]
    if not allowed then return nil end

    local bestPoint = nil
    local bestMedDist = math.huge
    local bestBrainrotDist = math.huge

    for _, entry in ipairs(TP_V2_SECOND_FLOOR_POINTS) do
        if allowed[entry.name] then
            local distToMed = flatDistance(medPos, entry.pos)
            local distToBrainrot = flatDistance(brainrotPos, entry.pos)

            if (distToBrainrot < bestBrainrotDist)
                or (math.abs(distToBrainrot - bestBrainrotDist) <= 0.001 and distToMed < bestMedDist) then
                bestPoint = entry
                bestMedDist = distToMed
                bestBrainrotDist = distToBrainrot
            end
        end
    end

    return bestPoint, bestMedDist, bestBrainrotDist
end

local function runTeleportV2SecondFloor(targetPart, hum, hrp)
    if not targetPart or not hum or not hrp then return false, "missing target/hum/hrp" end

    local initialPlayerPos = hrp.Position
    local initialBrainrotPos = targetPart.Position

    local medPoint = getNearestTeleportV2MedPoint(initialPlayerPos)
    if not medPoint then
        return false, "no MED point found"
    end

    local currentBrainrotPos = targetPart.Position
    local chosenPoint = getBestTeleportV2SecondFloorPoint(medPoint, currentBrainrotPos)
    if not chosenPoint then
        return false, "no TP1-TP12 point found"
    end

    local isFirstFloorBrainrot = currentBrainrotPos.Y <= 6.313370704650879
    local chosenTargetPos = isFirstFloorBrainrot
        and Vector3.new(chosenPoint.pos.X, 1, chosenPoint.pos.Z)
        or chosenPoint.pos

    local signPart = getClosestBaseSignToPosition(chosenTargetPos) or getClosestBaseSign(targetPart)
    local faceVector = nil
    if signPart then
        faceVector = chosenPoint.facing == "front" and signPart.CFrame.LookVector or -signPart.CFrame.LookVector
        if faceVector.Magnitude <= 0 then
            faceVector = nil
        end
    end

    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    local camConn = nil

    if faceVector then
        hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new()
        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + faceVector)
        hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new()
    end

    local function stopCameraLock() end

    local function finish(ok, msg)
        stopCameraLock()
        return ok, msg
    end

    hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new()

    local riseTargetY = 45

    if hum and hum.Parent then
        hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
    end
    task.wait(0.01)

    if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then
        return finish(false, "hrp lost during jump start")
    end

    local medFlatPos = Vector3.new(medPoint.pos.X, hrp.Position.Y, medPoint.pos.Z)
    hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new()
    hrp.CFrame = faceVector and CFrame.new(medFlatPos, medFlatPos + faceVector) or CFrame.new(medFlatPos)
    hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new()
    task.wait(0.1)

    if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then
        return finish(false, "hrp lost after MED reposition")
    end

    local reachedMedPoint = waitUntilHeartbeat(function()
        return hrp and hrp.Parent and flatDistance(hrp.Position, medPoint.pos) <= 2.5
    end, 1.5)

    if not reachedMedPoint then
        return finish(false, "failed to reach MED point")
    end

    riseToY(hrp, riseTargetY)

    hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new()

    hrp.CFrame = faceVector and CFrame.new(chosenTargetPos, chosenTargetPos + faceVector) or CFrame.new(chosenTargetPos)
    hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new()

    local reachedChosenPoint = waitUntilHeartbeat(function()
        return hrp and hrp.Parent and (hrp.Position - chosenTargetPos).Magnitude <= 2.5
    end, 3.0)

    if not reachedChosenPoint then
        return finish(false, "failed to reach TP1-TP12")
    end

waitSecondsHeartbeat(0.10)

    waitSecondsHeartbeat(0.05)

    local stillAtChosenPoint = waitUntilHeartbeat(function()
        return hrp and hrp.Parent and (hrp.Position - chosenTargetPos).Magnitude <= 2.5
    end, 0.75)

    if not stillAtChosenPoint then
        return finish(false, "moved away from TP1-TP12 before clone")
    end

    hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new()
    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new()

    local cloneStartPos = hrp.Position
    instantClone()
    waitUntilHeartbeat(function()
        return hrp and hrp.Parent and ((hrp.Position - cloneStartPos).Magnitude >= 0.35)
    end, 4.0)
    while _G.isCloning do task.wait() end

    return finish(true, chosenPoint.name)
end

local function triggerClosestUnlock(yLevel, maxY)
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return end

    local playerY = yLevel or hrp.Position.Y
    local Y_THRESHOLD = 5

    local bestPromptSameLevel = nil
    local shortestDistSameLevel = math.huge

    local bestPromptFallback = nil
    local shortestDistFallback = math.huge
    
    local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not plots then return end

    for _, obj in ipairs(plots:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and obj.Enabled then
            local part = obj.Parent
            if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                if maxY and part.Position.Y > maxY then
                else
                    local distance = (hrp.Position - part.Position).Magnitude
                    local yDifference = math.abs(playerY - part.Position.Y)

                    if distance < shortestDistFallback then
                        shortestDistFallback = distance
                        bestPromptFallback = obj
                    end

                    if yDifference <= Y_THRESHOLD then
                        if distance < shortestDistSameLevel then
                            shortestDistSameLevel = distance
                            bestPromptSameLevel = obj
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local targetPrompt = bestPromptSameLevel or bestPromptFallback

    if targetPrompt then
        if fireproximityprompt then
            fireproximityprompt(targetPrompt)
        else
            targetPrompt:InputBegan(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)
            task.wait(0.05)
            targetPrompt:InputEnded(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)
        end
    end

end

local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")

local unlockBaseGui = nil
local unlockBaseFrame = nil
local unlockBaseRoot = nil
local unlockBaseLockButton = nil
local unlockBaseButtonsBar = nil

local UNLOCK_UI_SAVE_FILE = "VanishButtonsRevamped_Pos.json"

local function saveUnlockUIData(pos, locked)
    pcall(function()
        if writefile then
            writefile(UNLOCK_UI_SAVE_FILE, HttpService:JSONEncode({
                xScale = pos.X.Scale,
                xOffset = pos.X.Offset,
                yScale = pos.Y.Scale,
                yOffset = pos.Y.Offset,
                locked = locked
            }))
        end
    end)
end

local function loadUnlockUIData()
    local defaultPos = UDim2.new(0.5, -102, 0, 132)
    local defaultLocked = false

    local ok, data = pcall(function()
        if readfile and isfile and isfile(UNLOCK_UI_SAVE_FILE) then
            return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(UNLOCK_UI_SAVE_FILE))
        end
    end)

    if ok and data then
        return UDim2.new(
            data.xScale or 0.5,
            data.xOffset or -102,
            data.yScale or 0,
            data.yOffset or 132
        ), data.locked == true
    end

    return defaultPos, defaultLocked
end

local function getUnlockHRP()
    local c = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not c then return end
    return c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or c:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
end

local function smartInteract(number)
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local hrp = getUnlockHRP()
    if not char or not hrp then return end

    local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not plots then return end

    local closestPlot, minDistance = nil, 40

    for _, plot in pairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
        local plotPos
        if plot:IsA("Model") then
            plotPos = plot.PrimaryPart and plot.PrimaryPart.Position or plot:GetPivot().Position
        else
            plotPos = plot.Position
        end

        local dist = (hrp.Position - plotPos).Magnitude
        if dist < minDistance then
            closestPlot = plot
            minDistance = dist
        end
    end

    if closestPlot and closestPlot:FindFirstChild("Unlock") then
        local items = {}

        for _, item in pairs(closestPlot.Unlock:GetChildren()) do
            local pos = item:IsA("Model") and item:GetPivot().Position or item.Position
            table.insert(items, {
                Obj = item,
                Y = pos.Y
            })
        end

        table.sort(items, function(a, b)
            return a.Y < b.Y
        end)

        if items[number] then
            for _, pr in pairs(items[number].Obj:GetDescendants()) do
                if pr:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                    pcall(function()
                        fireproximityprompt(pr)
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

local function getCurrentUnlockFloor()
    local hrp = getUnlockHRP()
    if not hrp then return 1 end

    local y = hrp.Position.Y
    if y < 12 then
        return 1
    elseif y < 30 then
        return 2
    else
        return 3
    end
end

local function autoUnlockCurrentFloor()
    local floor = getCurrentUnlockFloor()
    smartInteract(floor)
end

local function destroyUnlockBaseMenu()
    if unlockBaseGui then
        pcall(function()
            unlockBaseGui:Destroy()
        end)
    end
    unlockBaseGui = nil
    unlockBaseFrame = nil
    unlockBaseRoot = nil
    unlockBaseLockButton = nil
    unlockBaseButtonsBar = nil
end

-- Forward-declared stub so toggleUnlockBaseMenu (and any function defined
-- before line 2337) can call _VanishStarsHook without "attempt to call nil".
-- The real (still-empty) definition at line ~2337 just reassigns this same
-- local — both are no-ops since stars feature isn't wired here.
local function _VanishStarsHook() end

function toggleUnlockBaseMenu(state)
    if not state then
        destroyUnlockBaseMenu()
        return
    end

    destroyUnlockBaseMenu()

    local startPos, dragLocked = loadUnlockUIData()

    local function round(obj, radius)
        local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
        c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius)
        c.Parent = obj
        return c
    end

    local function stroke(obj, color, thickness, transparency)
        local s = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        s.Color = color
        s.Thickness = thickness or 1
        s.Transparency = transparency or 0
        s.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        s.Parent = obj
        return s
    end

    local function tween(obj, t, props, style, dir)
        TweenService:Create(
            obj,
            TweenInfo.new(t or 0.16, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, dir or Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
            props
        ):Play()
    end

    local function makeGradient(parent, c1, c2, rot)
        local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        g.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, c1),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, c2),
        })
        g.Rotation = rot or 0
        g.Parent = parent
        return g
    end

    local UIC = {
        BG = Color3.fromRGB(12, 14, 18),
        SURF = Color3.fromRGB(20, 22, 28),
        TEXT = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
        AQUA_STROKE = Color3.fromRGB(45, 50, 60),
        ACTIVE1 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 170, 255),
        ACTIVE2 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 140, 220),
        LOCK_RED_1 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 38, 45),
        LOCK_RED_2 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 32, 40),
        LOCK_GREEN_1 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 170, 255),
        LOCK_GREEN_2 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 140, 220),
    }

        do  -- GUI stagger: each ScreenGui starts ~0.45s after the previous
            _G.VanishGuiStaggerSlot = (_G.VanishGuiStaggerSlot or 0) + 1
            task.wait(0.45 * (_G.VanishGuiStaggerSlot - 1))
        end
    local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    sg.Name = "VanishUnlockBase"
    sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
    _VanishStarsHook(sg)
    sg.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    sg.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    sg.Parent = CoreGui

    unlockBaseGui = sg

    local root = Instance.new("Frame")
    root.Name = "UnlockRoot"
    root.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    root.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(168, 42)
    root.Position = startPos
    root.Parent = sg
    unlockBaseRoot = root

    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.Name = "UnlockButtonsBar"
    frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(168, 42)
    frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    frame.Parent = root
    unlockBaseFrame = frame
    unlockBaseButtonsBar = frame

    local UNLOCK_BG = Color3.fromRGB(12, 14, 18)
    local UNLOCK_BG_HOVER = Color3.fromRGB(20, 24, 32)
    local UNLOCK_TOP = Color3.fromRGB(20, 22, 28)
    local UNLOCK_MID = Color3.fromRGB(18, 20, 26)
    local UNLOCK_BOT = Color3.fromRGB(12, 14, 18)
    local UNLOCK_ACCENT = Color3.fromRGB(0, 170, 255)
    -- Match the rest of the hub's cyan accent outline (was grey 45,55,75).
    local UNLOCK_STROKE = Color3.fromRGB(0, 170, 255)
    local UNLOCK_COUNT = 3
    local BTN_SIZE = 54
    local BTN_GAP = 14
    local ROOT_H = BTN_SIZE + 8

    local unlockBtns = {}
    local clickingUnlockBtn = false
    local hoverInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.14, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)

    for i = 1, UNLOCK_COUNT do
        local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
        b.Name = "Unlock" .. tostring(i)
        b.AutoButtonColor = false
        b.BorderSizePixel = 0
        b.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(BTN_SIZE, BTN_SIZE)
        b.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((i - 1) * (BTN_SIZE + BTN_GAP), (ROOT_H - BTN_SIZE) / 2)
        b.BackgroundColor3 = UNLOCK_BG
        b.ClipsDescendants = true
        b.Text = ""
        b.Parent = frame
        round(b, 10)
        local bgGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        bgGrad.Rotation = 90
        bgGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.00, UNLOCK_TOP),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.50, UNLOCK_MID),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1.00, UNLOCK_BOT),
        })
        bgGrad.Parent = b
        local s = Instance.new("UIStroke", b)
        s.Color = UNLOCK_STROKE
        s.Thickness = 1.2
        s.Transparency = 0.55
        s.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border

        local numLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        numLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        numLabel.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        numLabel.Text = tostring(i)
        numLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        numLabel.TextSize = 22
        numLabel.TextColor3 = UNLOCK_ACCENT
        numLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        numLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
        numLabel.ZIndex = 2
        numLabel.Parent = b

        b.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() clickingUnlockBtn = true end)
        b.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() clickingUnlockBtn = false end)
        b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(b, hoverInfo, {BackgroundColor3 = UNLOCK_BG_HOVER}):Play()
        end)
        b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(b, hoverInfo, {BackgroundColor3 = UNLOCK_BG}):Play()
        end)
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            smartInteract(i)
        end)
        table.insert(unlockBtns, b)
    end

    root.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(UNLOCK_COUNT * BTN_SIZE + (UNLOCK_COUNT - 1) * BTN_GAP, ROOT_H)
    frame.Size = root.Size

    local dragging = false
    local dragStart
    local startRootPos

    root.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if dragLocked then return end
        if clickingUnlockBtn then return end
        -- Touch support: mobile users couldn't drag this panel without it.
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
           or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            dragStart = input.Position
            startRootPos = root.Position

            input.Changed:Connect(function()
                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    dragging = false
                    saveUnlockUIData(root.Position, dragLocked)
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if dragLocked then return end
        if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
                      or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local delta = input.Position - dragStart
            root.Position = UDim2.new(
                startRootPos.X.Scale,
                startRootPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                startRootPos.Y.Scale,
                startRootPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
            )
        end
    end)
end

local Theme = {
    Background      = Color3.fromRGB(8, 14, 35),
    Surface         = Color3.fromRGB(15, 24, 55),
    SurfaceHighlight= Color3.fromRGB(28, 42, 85),
    Accent1         = Color3.fromRGB(60, 130, 255),
    Accent2         = Color3.fromRGB(120, 180, 255),
    TextPrimary     = Color3.fromRGB(235, 240, 255),
    TextSecondary   = Color3.fromRGB(150, 170, 200),
    Success         = Color3.fromRGB(60, 180, 220),
    Error           = Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 100),
}
