    local mobileButtonScale = IS_MOBILE and 1.3 or 1

    local function createToggleRow(parent, text, y)
        local row = Instance.new("Frame", parent)
        row.Name = text:gsub("%s+", "") .. "Row"
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, math.floor(32 * mobileButtonScale))
        row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        row.BorderSizePixel = 0
        row.LayoutOrder = math.floor((y or 0) / math.max(1, (36 * mobileButtonScale))) + 1
        row.ZIndex = 103

        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 0)
        label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -64, 1, 0)
        label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        label.Text = text
        label.TextColor3 = MiniTargetColors.TEXT
        label.TextSize = 13 * mobileButtonScale
        label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        label.ZIndex = 104

        local stateBox = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        stateBox.Name = text:gsub("%s+", "") .. "Toggle"
        stateBox.AutoButtonColor = false
        stateBox.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(46 * mobileButtonScale), math.floor(22 * mobileButtonScale))
        stateBox.Position = UDim2.new(1, -math.floor(54 * mobileButtonScale), 0.5, -math.floor(11 * mobileButtonScale))
        stateBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)
        stateBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
        stateBox.Text = ""
        stateBox.ZIndex = 104
        do local c = Instance.new("UICorner"); c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0); c.Parent = stateBox end
        local stateStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", stateBox)
        stateStroke.Thickness = 0
        stateStroke.Transparency = 1

        local stateFill = Instance.new("Frame", stateBox)
        stateFill.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(20 * mobileButtonScale), math.floor(20 * mobileButtonScale))
        stateFill.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1)
        stateFill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
        stateFill.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        stateFill.BorderSizePixel = 0
        stateFill.ZIndex = 105
        do local c = Instance.new("UICorner"); c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0); c.Parent = stateFill end

        local stateText = Instance.new("TextLabel", stateBox)
        stateText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        stateText.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0)
        stateText.Visible = false
        stateText.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        stateText.Text = ""
        stateText.TextTransparency = 1

        local rowStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", row)
        rowStroke.Thickness = 0
        rowStroke.Transparency = 1

        return {
            row = row,
            label = label,
            button = stateBox,
            knob = stateFill,
            stateLabel = stateText,
            stroke = stateStroke,
            rowStroke = rowStroke,
        }
    end

    local nearestBtn = createToggleRow(toggleBtnContainer, "Pickup Nearest", 0)
    local highestBtn = createToggleRow(toggleBtnContainer, "Pickup Highest", 36*mobileButtonScale)
    local priorityBtn = createToggleRow(toggleBtnContainer, "Pickup Priority", 72*mobileButtonScale)
    local autoTurretBtn = createToggleRow(toggleBtnContainer, "Auto Turret", 108*mobileButtonScale)
    local autoKickBtn = createToggleRow(toggleBtnContainer, "Auto Kick", 144*mobileButtonScale)
    local autoSellBtn = createToggleRow(toggleBtnContainer, "Auto Sell", 180*mobileButtonScale)
    local instantStealBtn

    local function updateUI(enabled, allPets)
        syncAutoStealEnabledFromModes()
        local function paintToggle(ref, isOn, accentColor)
            local knobSize = math.floor(20 * mobileButtonScale)
            local trackW = math.floor(46 * mobileButtonScale)
            if isOn then
                miniTween(ref.button, 0.12, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent1})
                miniTween(ref.knob, 0.12, {Position = UDim2.fromOffset(trackW - knobSize - 1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)})
            else
                miniTween(ref.button, 0.12, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)})
                miniTween(ref.knob, 0.12, {Position = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)})
            end
            ref.knob.BackgroundTransparency = 0
            if ref.stateLabel then
                ref.stateLabel.Visible = false
            end
        end

        paintToggle(nearestBtn, stealNearestEnabled, Theme.Accent1)
        paintToggle(highestBtn, stealHighestEnabled, Theme.Accent1)
        paintToggle(priorityBtn, stealPriorityEnabled, Theme.Accent2)
        paintToggle(autoTurretBtn, Config.AutoDestroyTurrets, Theme.Accent1)
        paintToggle(autoKickBtn, Config.AutoKickOnSteal, Theme.Accent1)
        paintToggle(autoSellBtn, Config.AutoSellRandomBrainrot, Theme.Accent1)

        if selectedTargetUID and allPets then
            local found = false
            for i, p in ipairs(allPets) do
                if p.uid == selectedTargetUID then
                    selectedTargetIndex = i
                    found = true
                    break
                end
            end
        end

        if SharedState.ListNeedsRedraw then
            for _, c in ipairs(listHolder:GetChildren()) do if c:IsA("TextButton") then c:Destroy() end end
            petButtons = {}
            if allPets and #allPets > 0 then
                for i = 1, #allPets do
                    local petData = allPets[i]
                    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
                    btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, ROW_HEIGHT)
                    btn.BackgroundColor3 = AutoStealColors.SURF2
                    btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    btn.Text = ""
                    btn.AutoButtonColor = false
                    btn.Parent = listHolder
                    btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
                    btn.ClipsDescendants = true
                    btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
                    btn.ZIndex = 6
                    Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

                    local selectedOverlay = Instance.new("Frame", btn)
                    selectedOverlay.Name = "SelectedOverlay"
                    selectedOverlay.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                    selectedOverlay.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
                    selectedOverlay.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 10, 12)
                    selectedOverlay.BackgroundTransparency = 0.82
                    selectedOverlay.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    selectedOverlay.Visible = false
                    selectedOverlay.ZIndex = 2
                    Instance.new("UICorner", selectedOverlay).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9)

                    selectedOverlay.BackgroundColor3 = _vAccent
                    selectedOverlay.BackgroundTransparency = 0.78

                    local selectedOverlayGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient", selectedOverlay)
                    selectedOverlayGradient.Rotation = 0
                    selectedOverlayGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, _vAccent),
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, _vAccent),
                    })

                    local previewBox = Instance.new("Frame", btn)
                    previewBox.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26)
                    previewBox.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 3)
                    previewBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 32, 40)
                    previewBox.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
                    previewBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    previewBox.ZIndex = 7
                    Instance.new("UICorner", previewBox).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)

                    local viewport = Instance.new("ViewportFrame", previewBox)
                    viewport.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
                    viewport.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    viewport.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    viewport.LightDirection = Vector3.new(-0.4, -1, -0.6).Unit
                    viewport.LightColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    viewport.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(170, 180, 200)
                    viewport.ZIndex = 8
                    Instance.new("UICorner", viewport).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

                    task.spawn(function()
                        if not viewport.Parent then return end
                        if _G.HideStealTargetPictures then return end
                        local idx = (petData.animalData and petData.animalData.Index) or petData.Index
                        local displayName = (petData.animalData and petData.animalData.name) or petData.petName
                        local key = idx or displayName
                        if _G.VanishRenderBrainrotViewport then
                            -- Use animated=false to avoid calling GetAnimatedModel,
                            -- which has side effects on live in-game models even when
                            -- we only clone the result, causing floating brainrots.
                            pcall(_G.VanishRenderBrainrotViewport, viewport, key, false)
                        end
                    end)

                    local rankPalettes = {
                        [1] = { bg1 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 60),  bg2 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 150, 30),  text = Color3.fromRGB(40, 28, 0),    glow = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 100) },
                        [2] = { bg1 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 225, 235), bg2 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 168, 185), text = Color3.fromRGB(36, 38, 46),   glow = Color3.fromRGB(230, 235, 245) },
                        [3] = { bg1 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 130, 70),  bg2 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 90, 40),   text = Color3.fromRGB(46, 22, 8),    glow = Color3.fromRGB(240, 160, 90) },
                    }
                    local defaultPalette = { bg1 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 50, 70), bg2 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 28, 44), text = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), glow = _vAccent }
                    local pal = rankPalettes[i] or defaultPalette

                    local rankBox = Instance.new("Frame", btn)
                    rankBox.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18)
                    rankBox.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 7)
                    rankBox.BackgroundColor3 = pal.bg1
                    rankBox.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                    rankBox.ZIndex = 7
                    rankBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    Instance.new("UICorner", rankBox).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

                    local rankGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient", rankBox)
                    rankGradient.Rotation = 90
                    rankGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new(pal.bg1, pal.bg2)

                    local rankBoxStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", rankBox)
                    rankBoxStroke.Color = pal.glow
                    rankBoxStroke.Thickness = 1
                    rankBoxStroke.Transparency = (i <= 3) and 0.35 or 0.7
                    rankBoxStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border

                    local rankPad = Instance.new("UIPadding", rankBox)
                    rankPad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 1)
                    rankPad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 1)
                    rankPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 1)
                    rankPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 1)

                    local rankLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", rankBox)
                    rankLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                    rankLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    rankLabel.Text = tostring(i)
                    rankLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                    rankLabel.TextSize = 10
                    rankLabel.TextColor3 = pal.text
                    rankLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
                    rankLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
                    rankLabel.ZIndex = 8

                    local petName = (petData and petData.petName) or "Unknown"
                    local mpsText = (petData and petData.mpsText) or "$0/s"

                    local infoLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", btn)
                    infoLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -128, 0, 14)
                    infoLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(56, 3)
                    infoLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    infoLabel.RichText = false
                    infoLabel.Text = petName
                    infoLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                    infoLabel.TextSize = 11
                    infoLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    infoLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                    infoLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
                    infoLabel.ClipsDescendants = true
                    infoLabel.ZIndex = 7

                    local infoScale = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint", infoLabel)
                    infoScale.MinTextSize = 8
                    infoScale.MaxTextSize = 11

                    local rateLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", btn)
                    rateLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 66, 0, 14)
                    rateLabel.Position = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 9)
                    rateLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    rateLabel.RichText = false
                    rateLabel.Text = mpsText
                    rateLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                    rateLabel.TextSize = 11
                    rateLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 200, 130)
                    rateLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
                    rateLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
                    rateLabel.ZIndex = 7

                    local mutationLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", btn)
                    mutationLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -128, 0, 12)
                    mutationLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(56, 17)
                    mutationLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    local mutationText, mutationRich = getPetMutationLineText(petData)
                    mutationLabel.RichText = mutationRich
                    mutationLabel.Text = mutationText
                    mutationLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
                    mutationLabel.TextSize = 10
                    mutationLabel.TextColor3 = getMutationAccentColor(petData.mutation)
                    mutationLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                    mutationLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
                    mutationLabel.ClipsDescendants = true
                    mutationLabel.ZIndex = 7

                    local mutationScale = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint", mutationLabel)
                    mutationScale.MinTextSize = 8
                    mutationScale.MaxTextSize = 10
                    mutationLabel.Visible = mutationText ~= ""

                    petButtons[i] = {button=btn, selectedOverlay=selectedOverlay, rankBox=rankBox, rankBoxStroke=rankBoxStroke, rank=rankLabel, info=infoLabel, rate=rateLabel, mutation=mutationLabel, petData=petData}

btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if manualSelectedTargetUID == petData.uid then
        -- Clicking the already-selected row → deselect AND restore the
        -- mode that was active before the manual pick.
        selectedTargetIndex = nil
        selectedTargetUID   = nil
        manualSelectedTargetUID = nil
        if _G._VanishModeSnapshot then
            local sn = _G._VanishModeSnapshot
            stealNearestEnabled  = sn.nearest  == true
            stealHighestEnabled  = sn.highest  == true
            stealPriorityEnabled = sn.priority == true
            Config.StealNearest  = stealNearestEnabled
            Config.StealHighest  = stealHighestEnabled
            Config.StealPriority = stealPriorityEnabled
            _G.NEAREST_INSTANT_MODE = sn.nearestInstant == true
            _G._VanishModeSnapshot = nil
            syncAutoStealEnabledFromModes()
            SaveConfig()
        end
    else
        -- Snapshot the active mode BEFORE we nuke it, so deselect can
        -- restore. Skip overwriting an existing snapshot (the user
        -- might be switching between manual targets).
        if not _G._VanishModeSnapshot then
            _G._VanishModeSnapshot = {
                nearest         = stealNearestEnabled  == true,
                highest         = stealHighestEnabled  == true,
                priority        = stealPriorityEnabled == true,
                nearestInstant  = _G.NEAREST_INSTANT_MODE == true,
            }
        end
        selectedTargetIndex = i
        selectedTargetUID = petData.uid
        manualSelectedTargetUID = petData.uid
        stealNearestEnabled = false
        stealHighestEnabled = false
        stealPriorityEnabled = false
        Config.StealNearest = false
        Config.StealHighest = false
        Config.StealPriority = false
        syncAutoStealEnabledFromModes()
        _G.NEAREST_INSTANT_MODE = false
        SaveConfig()
    end
    SharedState.ListNeedsRedraw = false
    updateUI(autoStealEnabled, get_all_pets())
end)
                end
            end
            SharedState.ListNeedsRedraw = false
            local visibleRows = #petButtons
            local contentHeight = 0
            if visibleRows > 0 then
                contentHeight = (visibleRows * ROW_HEIGHT) + ((visibleRows - 1) * ROW_PADDING)
            end
            listHolder.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(ROW_BORDER_PAD_X * 2), 0, contentHeight)
            listFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, contentHeight + (ROW_BORDER_PAD_Y * 2))

            do
                local display   = math.min(visibleRows, MAX_VISIBLE_ROWS)
                local newListH  = display > 0 and (display * ROW_HEIGHT + (display - 1) * ROW_PADDING) or 0
                local newFrameH = 56 + newListH + 12
                local needsScroll = visibleRows > MAX_VISIBLE_ROWS
                listFrame.ScrollBarThickness        = needsScroll and 4 or 0
                listFrame.ScrollBarImageTransparency = needsScroll and 0.4 or 1
                listFrame.ScrollingEnabled          = needsScroll
                listFrame.Size  = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, newListH)
                frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, math.floor(265 * mobileScale), 0, math.floor(newFrameH * mobileScale))
            end
        end

    instantStealEnabled = false
    Config.InstantSteal = false

    local findProximityPromptForAnimal = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(animalData)
        if not animalData then return nil end
        local cp = PromptMemoryCache[animalData.uid]
        if cp and cp.Parent then return cp end
        local plot = Workspace.Plots:FindFirstChild(animalData.plot); if not plot then return nil end
        local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums"); if not podiums then return nil end

        local ch = _G.stealthGet(plot.Name)
        if not ch then
            
            local podium = podiums:FindFirstChild(animalData.slot)
            if podium then
                local base = podium:FindFirstChild("Base")
                local spawn = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
                if spawn then
                    local attach = spawn:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
                    if attach then
                        for _, p in ipairs(attach:GetChildren()) do
                            if p:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                                PromptMemoryCache[animalData.uid] = p
                                return p
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return nil
        end
        
        local al = _G.sProp(ch, "AnimalList")
        if not al then return nil end
        
        local brainrotName = animalData.name and animalData.name:lower() or ""
        local targetSlot = animalData.slot

        local foundPodium = nil
        for slot, ad in pairs(al) do
            if type(ad) == "table" and tostring(slot) == targetSlot then
                local aName, aInfo = ad.Index, AnimalsData[ad.Index]
                if aInfo and (aInfo.DisplayName or aName):lower() == brainrotName then
                    foundPodium = podiums:FindFirstChild(tostring(slot))
                    break
                end
            end
        end

        if not foundPodium then
            foundPodium = podiums:FindFirstChild(animalData.slot)
        end
        
        if foundPodium then
            local base = foundPodium:FindFirstChild("Base")
            local spawn = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
            if spawn then
                
                local attach = spawn:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
                if attach then
                    for _, p in ipairs(attach:GetChildren()) do
                        if p:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and p.Enabled and p.ActionText == "Steal" then
                            PromptMemoryCache[animalData.uid] = p
                            return p
                        end
                    end
                end

                local startPos = spawn.Position
                local slotX, slotZ = startPos.X, startPos.Z
                local nearestPrompt = nil
                local minDist = math.huge
                
                for _, desc in pairs(plot:GetDescendants()) do
                    if desc:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and desc.Enabled and desc.ActionText == "Steal" then
                        local part = desc.Parent
                        local promptPos = nil
                        
                        if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                            promptPos = part.Position
                        elseif part and part:IsA("Attachment") and part.Parent and part.Parent:IsA("BasePart") then
                            promptPos = part.Parent.Position
                        end
                        
                        if promptPos then
                            local checkStartY = startPos.Y
                            if brainrotName:find("la secret combinasion") then
                                checkStartY = startPos.Y - 5
                            end
                            local horizontalDist = math.sqrt((promptPos.X - slotX)^2 + (promptPos.Z - slotZ)^2)
                            if horizontalDist < 5 and promptPos.Y > checkStartY then
                                local yDist = promptPos.Y - checkStartY
                                if yDist < minDist then
                                    minDist = yDist
                                    nearestPrompt = desc
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                
                if nearestPrompt then
                    PromptMemoryCache[animalData.uid] = nearestPrompt
                    return nearestPrompt
                end
            end
        end

        return nil
    end)

    local STEAL_DURATION = 1.2

    local function buildStealCallbacks(prompt)
        if InternalStealCache[prompt] then return end
        local data = {holdCallbacks = {}, triggerCallbacks = {}, holdEndCallbacks = {}, ready = true}
        local ok1, conns1 = pcall(getconnections, prompt.PromptButtonHoldBegan)
        if ok1 and type(conns1) == "table" then
            for _, conn in ipairs(conns1) do
                if type(conn.Function) == "function" then
                    table.insert(data.holdCallbacks, conn.Function)
                end
            end
        end
        local ok2, conns2 = pcall(getconnections, prompt.Triggered)
        if ok2 and type(conns2) == "table" then
            for _, conn in ipairs(conns2) do
                if type(conn.Function) == "function" then
                    table.insert(data.triggerCallbacks, conn.Function)
                end
            end
        end
        local ok3, conns3 = pcall(getconnections, prompt.PromptButtonHoldEnded)
        if ok3 and type(conns3) == "table" then
            for _, conn in ipairs(conns3) do
                if type(conn.Function) == "function" then
                    table.insert(data.holdEndCallbacks, conn.Function)
                end
            end
        end
        if (#data.holdCallbacks > 0) or (#data.triggerCallbacks > 0) or (#data.holdEndCallbacks > 0) then
            InternalStealCache[prompt] = data
        end
    end

    local function runCallbackList(list)
        for _, fn in ipairs(list) do
            task.spawn(fn)
        end
    end

    local INSTANT_STEAL_RADIUS = 60
    local INSTANT_STEAL_COOLDOWN = 0.04
    local lastInstantStealTime = 0
    local function isMyPlot_Instant(plotName)
        local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not plots then return false end
        local plot = plots:FindFirstChild(plotName)
        if not plot then return false end
        local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
        if not sign then return false end
        local yb = sign:FindFirstChild("YourBase")
        return yb and yb:IsA("BillboardGui") and yb.Enabled
    end
    local function findNearestPrompt_Instant()
        local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return nil, math.huge, nil end
        local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not plots then return nil, math.huge, nil end
        local bestPrompt, bestDist, bestName = nil, math.huge, nil
        for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do repeat
            if isMyPlot_Instant(plot.Name) then break end
            local plotDist = math.huge
            pcall(function() plotDist = (plot:GetPivot().Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude end)
            if plotDist > INSTANT_STEAL_RADIUS + 40 then break end
            local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
            if not podiums then break end
            for _, pod in ipairs(podiums:GetChildren()) do repeat
                local base = pod:FindFirstChild("Base")
                local spawn = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
                if not spawn then break end
                local dist = (spawn.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                if dist > INSTANT_STEAL_RADIUS or dist >= bestDist then break end
                local att = spawn:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
                if not att then break end
                local prompt = att:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt")
                if prompt and prompt.Parent and prompt.Enabled then
                    bestPrompt = prompt
                    bestDist = dist
                    bestName = pod.Name
                end
            until true end
        until true end
        return bestPrompt, bestDist, bestName
    end

    local function executeInternalStealAsync(prompt, animalUID)
        local data = InternalStealCache[prompt]
        if not data or not data.ready then return false end
        data.ready = false

        task.spawn(function()
            if currentStealTargetUID ~= animalUID then
                if activeProgressTween then activeProgressTween:Cancel() end
                progressBarFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
                currentStealTargetUID = animalUID
            end

            if #data.holdCallbacks > 0 then
                runCallbackList(data.holdCallbacks)
            end

            progressBarFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
            progressBarFill.BackgroundTransparency = 0
            activeProgressTween = TweenService:Create(progressBarFill, TweenInfo.new(STEAL_DURATION, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)})
            activeProgressTween:Play()
            activeProgressTween.Completed:Wait()

            if currentStealTargetUID == animalUID and #data.triggerCallbacks > 0 then
                runCallbackList(data.triggerCallbacks)
            end

            data.ready = true
        end)

        return true
    end

    -- Hashim-style attempt: fire game's hold callbacks (extracted via
    -- getconnections), tween bar 0->1 over HOLD_MAX, wait HOLD_MIN, then
    -- fire trigger callbacks once we're in STEAL_RANGE.
    -- NO fireproximityprompt fallback — if getconnections returns
    -- nothing on a prompt, we abort that attempt cleanly.
    local STEAL_HOLD_MIN = 1.3
    local STEAL_HOLD_MAX = 2.6
    local STEAL_RANGE = 10
    local STEAL_ENTRY_DELAY = 0.3

    local function _getAnimalDataByUID(uid)
        for _, a in ipairs(allAnimalsCache) do
            if a.uid == uid then return a end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local _animalPos = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(animalData)
        if not animalData then return nil end
        local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots"); if not plots then return nil end
        local plot = plots:FindFirstChild(animalData.plot); if not plot then return nil end
        local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums"); if not podiums then return nil end
        local podium = podiums:FindFirstChild(tostring(animalData.slot)); if not podium then return nil end
        return podium:GetPivot().Position
    end)

    local function _distToAnimal(animalData)
        local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return math.huge end
        local pos = _animalPos(animalData)
        if not pos then return math.huge end
        return (hrp.Position - pos).Magnitude
    end

    local _attemptStealLastFire = {}
    local attemptSteal = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(prompt, animalUID)
        if not prompt or not prompt.Parent then return false end

        if currentStealTargetUID ~= animalUID then
            if activeProgressTween then
                activeProgressTween:Cancel()
                activeProgressTween = nil
            end
            progressBarFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
            currentStealTargetUID = animalUID
        end

        local now = os.clock()
        local last = _attemptStealLastFire[prompt]
        if last and (now - last) < 0.5 then return true end
        _attemptStealLastFire[prompt] = now

        buildStealCallbacks(prompt)
        local data = InternalStealCache[prompt]

        if not (data and data.ready and #data.holdCallbacks > 0 and #data.triggerCallbacks > 0) then
            -- No extractable callbacks — abort. We do NOT fall back to
            -- fireproximityprompt (game patched / unreliable).
            return false
        end

        data.ready = false
        local animalData = _getAnimalDataByUID(animalUID)
        task.spawn(function()
            runCallbackList(data.holdCallbacks)

            progressBarFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
            progressBarFill.BackgroundTransparency = 0
            if activeProgressTween then activeProgressTween:Cancel() end
            activeProgressTween = TweenService:Create(progressBarFill,
                TweenInfo.new(STEAL_HOLD_MAX, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear),
                {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)})
            activeProgressTween:Play()

            local startT = tick()
            while tick() - startT < STEAL_HOLD_MIN do
                if currentStealTargetUID ~= animalUID then
                    if activeProgressTween then activeProgressTween:Cancel(); activeProgressTween = nil end
                    progressBarFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
                    data.ready = true
                    return
                end
                task.wait()
            end

            local alreadyInRange = _distToAnimal(animalData) <= STEAL_RANGE
            local fired = false
            while true do
                local elapsed = tick() - startT
                if elapsed > STEAL_HOLD_MAX then break end
                if not prompt.Parent then break end
                if currentStealTargetUID ~= animalUID then break end
                if _distToAnimal(animalData) <= STEAL_RANGE then
                    if not alreadyInRange then task.wait(STEAL_ENTRY_DELAY) end
                    runCallbackList(data.triggerCallbacks)
                    fired = true
                    break
                end
                task.wait()
            end

            if fired then
                if activeProgressTween then activeProgressTween:Cancel(); activeProgressTween = nil end
                progressBarFill.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                task.wait(0.15)
            end
            progressBarFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
            task.wait(0.05)
            data.ready = true
        end)
        return true
    end)

    -- Expose to outside callers (V1 prefires steal during the clone-reset
    -- flow so by the time we land at the brainrot the hold-min has nearly
    -- elapsed, making the trigger near-instant).
    _G.vanishAttemptSteal = attemptSteal
    _G.vanishFindPromptForAnimal = findProximityPromptForAnimal

    local function prebuildStealCallbacks()
        for _, prompt in pairs(PromptMemoryCache) do
            if prompt and prompt.Parent then
                buildStealCallbacks(prompt)
            end
        end
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(2) do
            if autoStealEnabled then
                prebuildStealCallbacks()
            end
        end
    end)

    local lastAnimalData = {}
    local function getAnimalHash(al, ownerName)
        if not al then return "" end; local h=""
        for slot, d in pairs(al) do
            if type(d)=="table" then
                -- Include Machine.Type so the hash changes when a brainrot
                -- enters/leaves a blocking machine (Fuse / Duel / Trade /
                -- Crafting) — otherwise scanSinglePlot would short-circuit
                -- and the cache would never drop the now-unstealable entry.
                local mt = (type(d.Machine) == "table" and d.Machine.Type) or ""
                h = h..tostring(slot)..tostring(d.Index)..tostring(d.Mutation)..tostring(mt)
            end
        end
        -- Include the owner's duel/trade state so transitions into
        -- IsDuelSelecting / IsTrading also trigger a cache refresh and
        -- the whole-plot filter kicks in.
        if ownerName then
            local plr = Players:FindFirstChild(ownerName)
            if plr then
                h = h .. "|"
                    .. tostring(plr:GetAttribute("IsDuelSelecting") or false)
                    .. tostring(plr:GetAttribute("IsTrading")       or false)
            end
        end
        return h
    end

--> ( Live PlotClient via getgc — finds the in-memory PlotClient table that
--    has the most authoritative AnimalList (faster + more reliable than
--    Synchronizer:Get during state flux). Falls back to Synchronizer if
--    getgc isn't available or the live table isn't found. )
-- LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE: getgc(true) walks the entire heap; native dispatch is
-- ~100x faster than virtualized iteration. Called from scanSinglePlot for
-- every plot on every cache rebuild, so this is a HOT-PATH wrap.
local _getLivePlotClient = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(plot)
    if not plot or not getgc then return nil end
    for _, value in ipairs(getgc(true)) do
        if type(value) == "table"
            and rawget(value, "PlotModel") == plot
            and rawget(value, "Channel")
            and rawget(value, "AnimalsModels")
            and rawget(value, "AnimalsPrompts")
        then
            return value
        end
    end
    return nil
end)

local function scanSinglePlot(plot)
    local changed = false

    pcall(function()
        --> ( Try live PlotClient first; fall back to Synchronizer channel. )
        local liveClient = _getLivePlotClient(plot)
        local al = liveClient and rawget(liveClient, "AnimalList")
        local owner = liveClient and (rawget(liveClient, "Owner") or rawget(liveClient, "OwnerName"))

        if typeof(al) ~= "table" or not owner then
            local data = _G.VanishGetSyncData and _G.VanishGetSyncData(plot.Name)
            if not data then return end
            al = al or data.AnimalList
            owner = owner or data.Owner
        end

        --> ( Normalize owner: it may be a Player Instance, the owner table, or just a name string. )
        local ownerName
        if typeof(owner) == "Instance" and owner:IsA("Player") then ownerName = owner.Name
        elseif type(owner) == "table" and owner.Name then ownerName = owner.Name
        elseif type(owner) == "string" then ownerName = owner end

        if not ownerName or not Players:FindFirstChild(ownerName) then
            lastAnimalData[plot.Name] = nil

            for i = #allAnimalsCache, 1, -1 do
                if allAnimalsCache[i].plot == plot.Name then
                    table.remove(allAnimalsCache, i)
                    changed = true
                end
            end

            return
        end

        -- Skip whole plot if its owner is in duel-selection / trading —
        -- the brainrots there are off-limits even if they don't carry an
        -- individual Machine.Type yet. Drop any cached entries from this
        -- plot so ESP and the steal target list stop showing them too.
        if _G.VanishIsDuelBlocking and _G.VanishIsDuelBlocking(owner) then
            lastAnimalData[plot.Name] = nil
            for i = #allAnimalsCache, 1, -1 do
                if allAnimalsCache[i].plot == plot.Name then
                    table.remove(allAnimalsCache, i)
                    changed = true
                end
            end
            return
        end

        if typeof(al) ~= "table" then
            lastAnimalData[plot.Name] = nil

            for i = #allAnimalsCache, 1, -1 do
                if allAnimalsCache[i].plot == plot.Name then
                    table.remove(allAnimalsCache, i)
                    changed = true
                end
            end

            return
        end

        local hash = getAnimalHash(al, ownerName)

        if lastAnimalData[plot.Name] == hash then
            return
        end

        for i = #allAnimalsCache, 1, -1 do
            if allAnimalsCache[i].plot == plot.Name then
                table.remove(allAnimalsCache, i)
            end
        end

        for slot, ad in pairs(al) do
            if type(ad) == "table" then repeat
                -- Skip unstealable: pet is in a Fuse machine.
                if _VanishIsFusing(ad) then break end
                local aName, aInfo = ad.Index, AnimalsData[ad.Index]
                if aInfo then
                    local mut = ad.Mutation or "None"
                    if mut == "Yin Yang" then mut = "YinYang" end
                    local traits = (ad.Traits and #ad.Traits > 0) and table.concat(ad.Traits, ", ") or "None"
                    local gv = AnimalsShared:GetGeneration(aName, ad.Mutation, ad.Traits, nil)
                    local gt = "$" .. NumberUtils:ToString(gv) .. "/s"

                    table.insert(allAnimalsCache, {
                        name = aInfo.DisplayName or aName,
                        Index = aName,
                        genText = gt,
                        genValue = gv,
                        mutation = mut,
                        traits = traits,
                        owner = ownerName,
                        plot = plot.Name,
                        slot = tostring(slot),
                        uid = plot.Name .. "_" .. tostring(slot)
                    })
                end
            until true end
        end

        lastAnimalData[plot.Name] = hash
        changed = true
    end)

    if changed then
        table.sort(allAnimalsCache, function(a, b)
            return a.genValue > b.genValue
        end)

        SharedState.AllAnimalsCache = allAnimalsCache
        SharedState.ListNeedsRedraw = true

        if SharedState.UpdateAutoStealUI then
            SharedState.UpdateAutoStealUI()
        end

    end
end

    local function setupPlotListener(plot)
        scanSinglePlot(plot)

        local pending = false
        local function scheduleScan()
            if pending then return end
            pending = true
            task.delay(0.5, function()
                pending = false
                scanSinglePlot(plot)
            end)
        end
        plot.DescendantAdded:Connect(scheduleScan)
        plot.DescendantRemoving:Connect(scheduleScan)

        pcall(function()
            local ch = Synchronizer:Get(plot.Name)
            if ch then
                if ch.Signals then
                    if ch.Signals.DictionaryInsert and ch.Signals.DictionaryInsert.Connect then
                        ch.Signals.DictionaryInsert:Connect(scheduleScan)
                    end
                    if ch.Signals.DictionaryRemoved and ch.Signals.DictionaryRemoved.Connect then
                        ch.Signals.DictionaryRemoved:Connect(scheduleScan)
                    end
                    if ch.Signals.Changed and ch.Signals.Changed.Connect then
                        ch.Signals.Changed:Connect(scheduleScan)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    local plots = Workspace:WaitForChild("Plots", 8)
    if plots then
        for _, p in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do setupPlotListener(p) end
        plots.ChildAdded:Connect(function(p) task.wait(0.5); setupPlotListener(p) end)
        plots.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(p)
            lastAnimalData[p.Name]=nil
            for i=#allAnimalsCache,1,-1 do if allAnimalsCache[i].plot==p.Name then table.remove(allAnimalsCache,i) end end
            SharedState.ListNeedsRedraw=true
        end)
    end

    -- Refresh the relevant plot when any player toggles IsDuelSelecting
    -- or IsTrading — neither of those changes triggers a DescendantAdded
    -- or Synchronizer signal, so without this hook the cache would hold
    -- stale entries until the next animal-list change.
    local function _hookPlayerBlockingAttrs(plr)
        if not plr or plr == LocalPlayer then return end
        local function _refreshOwnedPlot()
            if not plots then return end
            for _, p in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
                local data = _G.VanishGetSyncData and _G.VanishGetSyncData(p.Name)
                local ownr = data and data.Owner
                local match = false
                if typeof(ownr) == "Instance" and ownr == plr then match = true
                elseif type(ownr) == "table" and ownr.UserId == plr.UserId then match = true
                elseif type(ownr) == "string" and ownr == plr.Name then match = true end
                if match then
                    lastAnimalData[p.Name] = nil  -- bust hash so scan won't short-circuit
                    scanSinglePlot(p)
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        plr:GetAttributeChangedSignal("IsDuelSelecting"):Connect(_refreshOwnedPlot)
        plr:GetAttributeChangedSignal("IsTrading"):Connect(_refreshOwnedPlot)
    end
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do _hookPlayerBlockingAttrs(plr) end
    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(_hookPlayerBlockingAttrs)

    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(0.5)
            if autoStealEnabled then
                local pets = get_all_pets()
                if #pets > 0 then
                    local function applySelection(newIndex)
                        if newIndex and newIndex >= 1 and newIndex <= #pets and selectedTargetIndex ~= newIndex then
                            selectedTargetIndex = newIndex
                            selectedTargetUID = pets[newIndex].uid
                            SharedState.ListNeedsRedraw = false
                            updateUI(autoStealEnabled, pets)
                        end
                    end

if stealPriorityEnabled then
    local foundPrioIndex = nil
    for _, pName in ipairs(PRIORITY_LIST) do
        local searchName = pName:lower()
        for i, p in ipairs(pets) do
            if p.petName and p.petName:lower() == searchName then
                foundPrioIndex = i
                break
            end
        end
        if foundPrioIndex then break end
    end
    if foundPrioIndex then
        applySelection(foundPrioIndex)
    else
        applySelection(1)
    end

elseif stealNearestEnabled then

    if not instantStealEnabled then
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp then
            local bestIndex = nil
            local bestDist = math.huge
            for i, p in ipairs(pets) do
                local targetPart = p.animalData and findAdorneeGlobal(p.animalData)
                if targetPart and targetPart:IsA("BasePart") then
                    local d = (hrp.Position - targetPart.Position).Magnitude
                    if d < bestDist then
                        bestDist = d
                        bestIndex = i
                    end
                end
            end
            if bestIndex then
                applySelection(bestIndex)
            else
                applySelection(1)
            end
        else
            applySelection(1)
        end
    end

elseif stealHighestEnabled then
    applySelection(1)
end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- ============================================================
    -- HASHIM-STYLE AUTO STEAL LOOP
    -- Always picks closest non-mine brainrot within PRIME_RANGE (80
    -- studs). Mode toggles still influence target choice when active:
    --   Priority -> first match in PRIORITY_LIST, then closest
    --   Nearest  -> closest within PRIME_RANGE
    --   Highest  -> pets[1] (sorted desc by genValue)
    --   None on  -> closest (hashim default)
    -- ============================================================
    local _autoStealLastTick = 0
    local PRIME_RANGE = 80

    local _hashimPickClosest = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(pets)
        local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return nil end
        local best, bestDist = nil, math.huge
        for _, p in ipairs(pets) do
            if not isMyBaseAnimal(p.animalData) then
                local pos = _animalPos(p.animalData)
                if pos then
                    local d = (hrp.Position - pos).Magnitude
                    if d <= PRIME_RANGE and d < bestDist then
                        bestDist = d
                        best = p
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return best
    end)

    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        if not _G.VanishStartupReady then return end   -- gated for 10s after join-snipe
        if not autoStealEnabled and not manualSelectedTargetUID then return end
        local _nowT = os.clock()
        if _nowT - _autoStealLastTick < 0.1 then return end  -- throttled 20Hz -> 10Hz to halve Luraph load
        _autoStealLastTick = _nowT

        local pets = get_all_pets()
        if #pets == 0 then return end

        local tp
        -- Manual selection takes absolute priority over the modes.
        if manualSelectedTargetUID then
            for _, p in ipairs(pets) do
                if p.uid == manualSelectedTargetUID then tp = p; break end
            end
        end
        if not tp then
            if stealPriorityEnabled then
                for _, pName in ipairs(PRIORITY_LIST or {}) do
                    local searchName = pName:lower()
                    for _, p in ipairs(pets) do
                        if p.petName and p.petName:lower() == searchName then
                            tp = p; break
                        end
                    end
                    if tp then break end
                end
                -- If nothing on the priority list is present, fall back to
                -- HIGHEST (pets[1], since list is sorted desc by genValue),
                -- NOT nearest. Priority mode = "this list or the biggest".
                tp = tp or pets[1]
            elseif stealNearestEnabled then
                tp = _hashimPickClosest(pets)
            elseif stealHighestEnabled then
                tp = pets[1]
            else
                tp = _hashimPickClosest(pets)
            end
        end

        if not tp or isMyBaseAnimal(tp.animalData) then return end

        local pr = PromptMemoryCache[tp.uid]
        if not pr or not pr.Parent then
            pr = findProximityPromptForAnimal(tp.animalData)
        end
        if pr then
            attemptSteal(pr, tp.uid)
        end
    end))

    task.spawn(function() while task.wait(0.5) do updateUI(autoStealEnabled, get_all_pets()) end end)
    task.delay(1, function() SharedState.ListNeedsRedraw=true; updateUI(autoStealEnabled, get_all_pets()) end)
    task.spawn(function() while true do SharedState.AllAnimalsCache=allAnimalsCache; task.wait(0.5) end end)

    local beamFolder = Instance.new("Folder", Workspace)
    beamFolder.Name = "VanishTracers"
    local currentBeam = nil
    local currentAtt0 = nil
    local currentAtt1 = nil

    local updateTracer = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        if not autoStealEnabled or not Config.TracerEnabled or Config.StealNearest then
            if currentBeam then currentBeam:Destroy() currentBeam=nil end
            if currentAtt0 then currentAtt0:Destroy() currentAtt0=nil end
            if currentAtt1 then currentAtt1:Destroy() currentAtt1=nil end
            return
        end

        local pets = get_all_pets()
        if #pets == 0 then
            if currentBeam then currentBeam.Enabled = false end
            return
        end
        if selectedTargetIndex > #pets then selectedTargetIndex = #pets end
        if selectedTargetIndex < 1 then selectedTargetIndex = 1 end

        local best = pets[selectedTargetIndex] or pets[1]
        local targetPart = findAdorneeGlobal(best.animalData)
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if hrp and targetPart then
            if not currentAtt0 or currentAtt0.Parent ~= hrp then
                if currentAtt0 then currentAtt0:Destroy() end
                currentAtt0 = Instance.new("Attachment", hrp)
            end
            if not currentAtt1 or currentAtt1.Parent ~= targetPart then
                if currentAtt1 then currentAtt1:Destroy() end
                currentAtt1 = Instance.new("Attachment", targetPart)
            end

            if not currentBeam then
                currentBeam = Instance.new("Beam", beamFolder)
                currentBeam.FaceCamera = true
                currentBeam.Width0 = 0.8
                currentBeam.Width1 = 0.8
                currentBeam.TextureMode = Enum.TextureMode.Static
                currentBeam.TextureSpeed = 3
            end

            currentBeam.Attachment0 = currentAtt0
            currentBeam.Attachment1 = currentAtt1
            currentBeam.Enabled = true

            local MUT_COLORS_TRACE = {
                Cursed=Color3.fromRGB(200,0,0), Gold=Color3.fromRGB(255,215,0),
                Diamond=Color3.fromRGB(0,255,255), YinYang=Color3.fromRGB(220,220,220),
                Rainbow=Color3.fromRGB(255,100,200), Lava=Color3.fromRGB(255,100,20),
                Candy=Color3.fromRGB(255,105,180), Divine=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
            }
            local col = (best and best.mutation and MUT_COLORS_TRACE[best.mutation]) or Theme.Accent1
            currentBeam.Color = ColorSequence.new(col)
        else
            if currentBeam then currentBeam.Enabled = false end
        end
    end)

    local _tracerLastTick = 0
    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        if not _G.VanishStartupReady then return end
        local _nowT = os.clock()
        if _nowT - _tracerLastTick < 0.1 then return end
        _tracerLastTick = _nowT
        updateTracer()
    end))
end)

local BASES_LOW = {
    [1] = Vector3.new(-460, -6, 219), [5] = Vector3.new(-355, -6, 217),
    [2] = Vector3.new(-460, -6, 111), [6] = Vector3.new(-355, -6, 113),
    [3] = Vector3.new(-460, -6, 5),   [7] = Vector3.new(-355, -6, 5),
    [4] = Vector3.new(-460, -6, -100),[8] = Vector3.new(-355, -6, -100) 
}

local BASES_HIGH = {
    [1] = Vector3.new(-476.474853515625, 20.732906341552734, 220.94090270996094), [5] = Vector3.new(-342.5367126464844, 17.69801902770996, 221.44737243652344),
    [2] = Vector3.new(-476.5684814453125, 20.70664405822754, 113.77315521240234), [6] = Vector3.new(-342.8604736328125, 17.669641494750977, 113.41409301757812),
    [3] = Vector3.new(-476.8675842285156, 20.74148178100586, 6.178487777709961),  [7] = Vector3.new(-342.42108154296875, 17.687667846679688, 6.249461650848389),
    [4] = Vector3.new(-476.6324768066406, 20.744949340820312, -101.07275390625), [8] = Vector3.new(-342.7937927246094, 17.748071670532227, -99.73458862304688)
}

local CLONE_POSITIONS_FLOOR = {
    Vector3.new(-476, -4, 221), Vector3.new(-476, -4, 114),
    Vector3.new(-476, -4, 7),   Vector3.new(-476, -4, -100),
    Vector3.new(-342, -4, -100),Vector3.new(-342, -4, 6),
    Vector3.new(-342, -4, 114), Vector3.new(-342, -4, 220)
}

local FACE_TARGETS = {
    Vector3.new(-519, -3, 221), Vector3.new(-519, -3, 114),
    Vector3.new(-518, -3, 7),   Vector3.new(-519, -3, -100),
    Vector3.new(-301, -3, -100),Vector3.new(-301, -3, 7),
    Vector3.new(-302, -3, 114), Vector3.new(-300, -3, 220)
}

local TeleportData = {
    bodyController = nil,
}
local bodyController = TeleportData.bodyController
local floatActive = State.floatActive

RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
    if State.floatActive and TeleportData.bodyController and LocalPlayer.Character then
        local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp then TeleportData.bodyController.Position = Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X, TeleportData.bodyController.Position.Y, TeleportData.bodyController.Position.Z) end
    end
end))

local function getClosestBaseIdx(pos)
    local closest, dist = 1, math.huge
    for i, basePos in pairs(BASES_LOW) do
        local d = (Vector2.new(pos.X, pos.Z) - Vector2.new(basePos.X, basePos.Z)).Magnitude
        if d < dist then dist = d; closest = i end
    end
    return closest
end

local isTpMoving = State.isTpMoving

_G._isTargetPlotUnlocked = function(plotName)
    local ok, res = pcall(function()
        local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not plots then return false end
        local targetPlot = plots:FindFirstChild(plotName)
        if not targetPlot then return false end
        local unlockFolder = targetPlot:FindFirstChild("Unlock")
        if not unlockFolder then return true end
        local unlockItems = {}
        for _, item in pairs(unlockFolder:GetChildren()) do
            local pos = nil
            if item:IsA("Model") then pcall(function() pos = item:GetPivot().Position end)
            elseif item:IsA("BasePart") then pos = item.Position end
            if pos then table.insert(unlockItems, {Object = item, Height = pos.Y}) end
        end
        table.sort(unlockItems, function(a, b) return a.Height < b.Height end)
        if #unlockItems == 0 then return true end
        local floor1Door = unlockItems[1].Object
        for _, desc in ipairs(floor1Door:GetDescendants()) do
            if desc:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and desc.Enabled then return false end
        end
        for _, child in ipairs(floor1Door:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and child.Enabled then return false end
        end
        return true
    end)
    return ok and res or false
end

-- ============================================================
-- CLONE OWNERSHIP TELEPORT
-- Activates the Quantum Cloner, claims ownership of the clone via
-- TP-into-clone + firetouchinterest + bat trick + anchor, then directly
-- moves the clone to the brainrot (or under it with a support platform
-- for upper floors). No base navigation, no clone-reset chain — the
-- swap teleports the player straight to the brainrot.
-- ============================================================
local function _pickRunAutoSnipeTarget()
    -- Manual UI selection wins over every mode. SelectedPetData is set
    -- whenever the user clicks a row in the steal-target list AND no
    -- mode flag is active (the click handler clears them).
    if SharedState.SelectedPetData
       and not (Config.StealNearest or Config.StealHighest or Config.StealPriority) then
        local d = SharedState.SelectedPetData.animalData
        if d then return d end
    end
    if Config.StealNearest then
        local p = pickNearestNonSelf and pickNearestNonSelf()
        if p then return p end
        return SharedState.SelectedPetData and SharedState.SelectedPetData.animalData
    elseif Config.StealHighest then
        local p = pickHighestNonSelf and pickHighestNonSelf()
        if p then return p end
        return SharedState.SelectedPetData and SharedState.SelectedPetData.animalData
    end
    local p = pickHighestNonSelf and pickHighestNonSelf()
    if p then return p end
    return SharedState.SelectedPetData and SharedState.SelectedPetData.animalData
end


_G._VanishWaitSettled = _G._VanishWaitSettled or function()
    local lp = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    local rs = game:GetService("RunService")
    local last, still
    for _ = 1, 30 do
        local h = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not h then return end
        if last and (h.Position - last).Magnitude < 1.2 then
            still = (still or 0) + 1
            if still >= 4 then return end
        else
            still = 0
        end
        last = h.Position
        rs.Heartbeat:Wait()
    end
end

local function runAutoSnipe()
    if Config.TpMode == "Side" and _G.VanishStartSideTP then
        return _G.VanishStartSideTP()
    end
    if State.isTpMoving then return end
    -- Don't start a new steal while the player is still mid-carry.
    -- Firing a TP during a carry moves the player away and drops the
    -- brainrot in mid-air, leaving real floating brainrots on the map.
    if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then return end

    if _G.VanishDesync and _G.VanishDesync.isActive and _G.VanishDesync.isActive() then
        pcall(_G.VanishDesync.suspend)
    end
    if _G.VanishDeltaDesync and _G.VanishDeltaDesync.isActive and _G.VanishDeltaDesync.isActive() then
        pcall(_G.VanishDeltaDesync.suspend)
    end

    if State.carpetSpeedEnabled then
        setCarpetSpeed(false)
        if _carpetStatusLabel then
            _carpetStatusLabel.Text = "OFF"
            _carpetStatusLabel.TextColor3 = Theme.Error
        end
    end

    local targetPetData = nil

    -- ============================================================
    -- FRESH SCAN: rescan ALL plots inline before picking target.
    -- AllAnimalsCache can be stale/incomplete on join. This guarantees
    -- we pick from the latest data the Synchronizer knows about.
    -- ============================================================
    local cache = {}
    do
        local _RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local _Packages = _RS:FindFirstChild("Packages")
        local _Datas    = _RS:FindFirstChild("Datas")
        local _Shared   = _RS:FindFirstChild("Shared")
        local _Utils    = _RS:FindFirstChild("Utils")
        local _Sync, _AData, _AShared, _NU
        pcall(function() _Sync   = _Packages and require(_Packages:FindFirstChild("Synchronizer")) end)
        pcall(function() _AData  = _Datas    and require(_Datas:FindFirstChild("Animals")) end)
        pcall(function() _AShared = _Shared  and require(_Shared:FindFirstChild("Animals")) end)
        pcall(function() _NU     = _Utils    and require(_Utils:FindFirstChild("NumberUtils")) end)

        local plotsFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if plotsFolder and _Sync and _AData and _AShared then
            for _, plot in ipairs(plotsFolder:GetChildren()) do
                pcall(function()
                    local data = _G.VanishGetSyncData and _G.VanishGetSyncData(plot.Name)
                    if not data then return end
                    local owner = data.Owner
                    local ownerName
                    if typeof(owner) == "Instance" and owner:IsA("Player") then ownerName = owner.Name
                    elseif type(owner) == "table" and owner.Name then ownerName = owner.Name
                    elseif type(owner) == "string" then ownerName = owner end
                    if not ownerName or ownerName == LocalPlayer.Name or not Players:FindFirstChild(ownerName) then return end
                    if _G.VanishIsDuelBlocking and _G.VanishIsDuelBlocking(ownerName) then return end
                    local al = data.AnimalList
                    if type(al) ~= "table" then return end
                    for slot, ad in pairs(al) do
                        if type(ad) == "table" and ad.Index then repeat
                            if _G.VanishIsFusing and _G.VanishIsFusing(ad) then break end
                            local info = _AData[ad.Index]
                            if info then
                                local gv = 0
                                pcall(function()
                                    gv = _AShared:GetGeneration(ad.Index, ad.Mutation, ad.Traits, nil) or 0
                                end)
                                if type(gv) == "number" and gv >= 1 then
                                    local genText
                                    if _NU then
                                        pcall(function() genText = "$" .. _NU:ToString(gv) .. "/s" end)
                                    end
                                    table.insert(cache, {
                                        name = info.DisplayName or ad.Index,
                                        Index = ad.Index,
                                        genText = genText or ("$" .. tostring(math.floor(gv)) .. "/s"),
                                        genValue = gv,
                                        mutation = ad.Mutation or "None",
                                        owner = ownerName,
                                        plot = plot.Name,
                                        slot = tostring(slot),
                                        uid = plot.Name .. "_" .. tostring(slot),
                                    })
                                end
                            end
                        until true end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
        table.sort(cache, function(a, b) return a.genValue > b.genValue end)
        -- Fallback to existing cache if fresh scan came up empty (e.g.
        -- modules not loaded yet on very first tick after script start)
        if #cache == 0 and SharedState and type(SharedState.AllAnimalsCache) == "table" then
            cache = SharedState.AllAnimalsCache
        else
            if SharedState then SharedState.AllAnimalsCache = cache end
        end
    end

    local function pickHighestNonSelf()
        if not cache or #cache == 0 then return nil end
        return cache[1] -- already sorted desc by genValue
    end
    if Config.AutoTPPriority then
        local bestEntry = nil
        if cache and type(cache) == "table" then
            for _, pName in ipairs(PRIORITY_LIST) do
                local searchName = pName:lower()
                local bestForName, bestVal = nil, -math.huge
                for _, a in ipairs(cache) do
                    if a and a.name and a.name:lower() == searchName and a.owner ~= LocalPlayer.Name
                        and type(a.genValue) == "number" and a.genValue > bestVal then
                        bestForName = a; bestVal = a.genValue
                    end
                end
                if bestForName then bestEntry = bestForName; break end
            end
            if not bestEntry then
                bestEntry = pickHighestNonSelf()
            end
        end
        if bestEntry then
            targetPetData = bestEntry
        else
            if not SharedState.SelectedPetData then return end
            targetPetData = SharedState.SelectedPetData.animalData
        end
    else
        targetPetData = pickHighestNonSelf()
        if not targetPetData then
            if not SharedState.SelectedPetData then return end
            targetPetData = SharedState.SelectedPetData.animalData
        end
    end
    if not targetPetData then return end

    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

    if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end

    State.isTpMoving = true
    isTpMoving = State.isTpMoving
    -- Signal to the startup gate that the join-TP is firing — gate
    -- starts the 5s post-fire timer from this point.
    _G.VanishAutoSnipeFired = true

    local targetPart = findAdorneeGlobal(targetPetData)
    if not targetPart then
        State.isTpMoving = false
        isTpMoving = State.isTpMoving
        return
    end

    local exactPos = targetPart.Position
    local carpetName = Config.TpSettings.Tool
    local carpet = LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild(carpetName) or char:FindFirstChild(carpetName)

    local _spd = tonumber(Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.Speed) or 1
    if _spd < 1 then _spd = 1 elseif _spd > 3 then _spd = 3 end
    local _stepWait    = ({0.05, 0.075, 0.10})[_spd]
    local _faceWait    = ({0.10, 0.16,  0.22})[_spd]
    local _preCloneW   = ({0.55, 0.70,  0.85})[_spd]
    local _postCloneW  = ({0.15, 0.22,  0.30})[_spd]

    -- Read TeleportMethod directly (authoritative). TpV2NoAP is legacy.
    -- Anything that isn't "Tween Teleport" uses the AP flow.
    local _useV2 = (Config.TeleportMethod == "Tween Teleport")

    -- Ragdoll-TP removed entirely. (AP path no longer fires self-ragdoll;
    -- Tween path never did, so this change can't regress Tween TP.)

    if carpet then hum:EquipTool(carpet) end
    task.wait(0.01)

    -- Pickup turn-on: fires the moment HRP is within 60 studs of the
    -- brainrot. Wait deadline is 10s as a safety. HOLD_MIN starts ticking
    -- so the steal trigger fires the instant we land at the brainrot.
    task.spawn(function()
        local pickupDeadline = os.clock() + 10
        while os.clock() < pickupDeadline do
            local _ch = LocalPlayer.Character
            local _hrp = _ch and _ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if _hrp and targetPart and targetPart.Parent then
                if (_hrp.Position - targetPart.Position).Magnitude <= 60 then
                    break
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.05)
        end
        if _G.vanishAttemptSteal and _G.vanishFindPromptForAnimal then
            local pr = _G.vanishFindPromptForAnimal(targetPetData)
            if pr then
                pcall(_G.vanishAttemptSteal, pr, targetPetData.uid)
            end
        end
    end)
    local isSecondFloor = exactPos.Y > 10
    local plotIndex = getClosestBaseIdx(exactPos)
    local targetBasePos = isSecondFloor and BASES_HIGH[plotIndex] or BASES_LOW[plotIndex]

    local minHeight = 50
    local targetHeight = math.max(targetBasePos.Y, minHeight)

    if _useV2 then
        -- V2: pathfinding ground velocity travel — uses PathfindingService
        -- to route around collidable walls/fences. Tighter AgentRadius +
        -- finer WaypointSpacing produce shorter routes that hug obstacles
        -- instead of taking wide detours. A per-frame line-of-sight skip
        -- "string-pulls" the path: each tick we jump to the furthest
        -- waypoint we can see, cutting corners that the raw A* keeps.
        pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end)
        task.wait(0.04)
        local _v2Speed   = (Config.TpSettings.Tool == "Grapple Hook")
            and math.clamp(tonumber(Config.TpGrappleSpeed) or 200, 180, 230)
            or  math.clamp(tonumber(Config.TpV2Speed) or 150, 150, 180)
        local _v2Arrival = 4
        local _v2Timeout = 8

        local PathfindingService = game:GetService("PathfindingService")
        local path = PathfindingService:CreatePath({
            AgentRadius     = 1.2,   -- tighter — paths hug walls instead of bowing out
            AgentHeight     = 5,
            AgentCanJump    = false,
            AgentCanClimb   = false,
            WaypointSpacing = 4,     -- finer — smoother curves, no chunky zigzag
            Costs = {
                Water       = 50,
                DangerZone  = 100,
                Plastic     = 1,
                SmoothPlastic = 1,
            },
        })
        local waypoints
        local pfOk = pcall(function()
            path:ComputeAsync(hrp.Position, targetBasePos)
        end)
        if pfOk and path.Status == Enum.PathStatus.Success then
            waypoints = path:GetWaypoints()
            if #waypoints == 0 then waypoints = nil end
        end
        if not waypoints then
            -- Fallback: single direct waypoint when pathfinding fails
            waypoints = { { Position = targetBasePos } }
        end

        -- Raycast params for the line-of-sight skip (ignore self + clones).
        local _losParams = RaycastParams.new()
        _losParams.FilterType   = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        _losParams.IgnoreWater  = true
        do
            local ignore = { char }
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if p ~= LocalPlayer and p.Character then table.insert(ignore, p.Character) end
            end
            local cloneInst = Workspace:FindFirstChild(tostring(LocalPlayer.UserId) .. "_Clone")
            if cloneInst then table.insert(ignore, cloneInst) end
            _losParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignore
        end

        -- Returns true if the path from `fromPos` to `toPos` is clear
        -- (no hit on the segment, treating Y as flat).
        local function _losClear(fromPos, toPos)
            local seg = Vector3.new(toPos.X - fromPos.X, 0, toPos.Z - fromPos.Z)
            local d = seg.Magnitude
            if d < 0.1 then return true end
            local hit = Workspace:Raycast(fromPos + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), seg, _losParams)
            return hit == nil
        end

        local _v2T0    = tick()
        local _v2Done  = false
        local _v2WpIdx = 1
        local _v2WpT0  = tick()
        local _v2WpTimeout = 1.2
        local _v2Conn
        _v2Conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            if _v2Done then _v2Conn:Disconnect(); return end
            if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then _v2Done = true; _v2Conn:Disconnect(); return end
            if tick() - _v2T0 > _v2Timeout then
                pcall(function() hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end)
                _v2Done = true; _v2Conn:Disconnect(); return
            end

            -- STRING-PULL: scan ahead — if a further waypoint is visible
            -- via raycast, skip directly to it (cuts the cornered path).
            local cur = hrp.Position
            local lookMax = math.min(#waypoints, _v2WpIdx + 8)
            for i = lookMax, _v2WpIdx + 1, -1 do
                local fwp = waypoints[i]
                if fwp and _losClear(cur, fwp.Position) then
                    _v2WpIdx = i
                    _v2WpT0 = tick()
                    break
                end
            end

            local wp = waypoints[_v2WpIdx]
            if not wp then
                pcall(function() hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end)
                _v2Done = true; _v2Conn:Disconnect(); return
            end

            local isFinal = (_v2WpIdx == #waypoints)
            local wpPos = wp.Position
            local diff
            if isFinal then
                -- Final waypoint: track full 3D so we land at base height
                diff = wpPos - cur
            else
                -- Mid waypoints: travel flat at current Y to avoid clipping
                diff = Vector3.new(wpPos.X - cur.X, 0, wpPos.Z - cur.Z)
            end
            local dist = diff.Magnitude
            local arrThresh = isFinal and _v2Arrival or 3
            if dist <= arrThresh or tick() - _v2WpT0 > _v2WpTimeout then
                _v2WpIdx = _v2WpIdx + 1
                _v2WpT0 = tick()
                return
            end
            local dir = diff.Unit
            if Config.TpSettings.Tool == "Grapple Hook" and _G.VanishFireGrapple then _G.VanishFireGrapple() end
            pcall(function() hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = dir * _v2Speed end)
        end))
        while not _v2Done do task.wait(0.05) end
        pcall(function() hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end)

        -- ─── Proximity gate ───────────────────────────────────────────
        -- Path loop may finish far from the target (per-waypoint timeout,
        -- pathfinder got blocked, etc.). DO NOT snap to the cloning spot
        -- unless the player is actually close — otherwise it just looks
        -- like the script "TPed to the cloning place" mid-flight.
        --
        -- If still far: do a final 2s straight-line velocity push toward
        -- the target. If even then we can't get close, skip the snap and
        -- bail — the wall raycast will fail and no clone will be wasted.
        local _V2_CLEAN_SNAP_MAX   = 8   -- snap-cleanup is fine inside this
        local _V2_FINAL_PUSH_TIME  = 2.0 -- max seconds for the recovery push
        local _V2_FINAL_PUSH_STOP  = 4   -- arrival threshold during push

        local function _v2DistToTarget()
            if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return math.huge end
            return (hrp.Position - targetBasePos).Magnitude
        end

        if _v2DistToTarget() > _V2_CLEAN_SNAP_MAX then
            local _pT0 = tick()
            while tick() - _pT0 < _V2_FINAL_PUSH_TIME do
                if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then break end
                if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") then break end
                local d = _v2DistToTarget()
                if d <= _V2_FINAL_PUSH_STOP then break end
                local diff = targetBasePos - hrp.Position
                local mag  = diff.Magnitude
                if mag < 0.1 then break end
                if Config.TpSettings.Tool == "Grapple Hook" and _G.VanishFireGrapple then _G.VanishFireGrapple() end
                pcall(function()
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = (diff / mag) * _v2Speed
                end)
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            end
            pcall(function() hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end)
        end

        -- Only do the cleanup snap if we're actually close. Skipping the
        -- snap when far prevents the "TPed to cloning place from across
        -- the map" behavior the user reported.
        if _v2DistToTarget() <= _V2_CLEAN_SNAP_MAX then
            pcall(function() hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetBasePos) end)
        else
            -- Bail out of the TP entirely — caller's _atWall check would
            -- fail and no clone would be placed, so abort cleanly here.
            State.isTpMoving = false
            isTpMoving = State.isTpMoving
            return
        end
        task.wait(_stepWait)
    else
        pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end)
        task.wait(0.07)
        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position.X, targetHeight, hrp.Position.Z)

        if (hrp.Position - targetBasePos).Magnitude > 3 then
            pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end)
            task.wait(0.07)
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetBasePos)
            task.wait(_stepWait)
        end
    end

    if not isSecondFloor then
        local bestSpot = CLONE_POSITIONS_FLOOR[1]
        local minDst = math.huge
        for _, v in ipairs(CLONE_POSITIONS_FLOOR) do
            local d = (targetPart.Position - v).Magnitude
            if d < minDst then minDst = d; bestSpot = v end
        end
        if (hrp.Position - bestSpot).Magnitude > 3 then
            pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end)
            task.wait(0.07)
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(bestSpot)
            task.wait(_stepWait)
        end
    end

    local bestFace = FACE_TARGETS[1]
    local minFaceDist = math.huge
    for _, v in ipairs(FACE_TARGETS) do
        local d = (hrp.Position - v).Magnitude
        if d < minFaceDist then
            minFaceDist = d
            bestFace = v
        end
    end

    task.wait(_faceWait)
    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(bestFace.X, hrp.Position.Y, bestFace.Z))
    if isSecondFloor or not _G._isTargetPlotUnlocked(targetPetData.plot) then

        do
            local rp = RaycastParams.new()
            rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
            rp.IgnoreWater = true
            rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = { char }
            local origin = hrp.Position
            local dir = hrp.CFrame.LookVector * 30
            local hit = Workspace:Raycast(origin, dir, rp)
            if hit and hit.Position then
                local approachVec = (hit.Position - origin)
                local approachDist = approachVec.Magnitude
                if approachDist > 0.9 then
                    --> ( Floor 1 lands a little closer to the wall (-0.45 vs
                    --    original -0.6); upper floors stay at -1. )
                    local backoff = isSecondFloor and 1 or 0.45
                    local landFlat = origin + approachVec.Unit * (approachDist - backoff)
