    local function makePage(name, displayName, hasSubTabs)
        local page = Instance.new("Frame")
        page.Name = name
        page.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        page.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        page.Visible = false
        page.Parent = content

        local card = Instance.new("CanvasGroup")
        card.Name = "Card"
        card.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        card.BorderSizePixel = 0
        card.GroupTransparency = 0
        card.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        card.Parent = page

        if hasSubTabs then
            -- Sub-tab pages don't get a scroll frame here; makeSubTabPage populates card instead.
            return page, card
        end

        local sc = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
        sc.Name = "Scroll"
        sc.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -12)
        sc.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 2)
        sc.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        sc.BorderSizePixel = 0
        sc.ScrollBarThickness = 4
        sc.ScrollBarImageColor3 = VANISH.ACCENT2
        sc.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        sc.Parent = card

        local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
        pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8)
        pad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8)
        pad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4)
        pad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 12)
        pad.Parent = sc

        local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        lay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
        lay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        lay.Parent = sc

        lay:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
            sc.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, lay.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 18)
        end)

        do
            local row = Instance.new("Frame")
            row.Name = "PageTitleRow"
            row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, s(20))
            row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            row.Parent = sc

            local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            lbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 150, 1, 0)
            lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, s(4), 0, 0)
            lbl.Text = tostring(displayName or name)
            lbl.TextColor3 = VANISH.TEXT
            lbl.TextSize = s(13)
            lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            lbl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
            lbl.Parent = row

            local line = Instance.new("Frame")
            line.Size = UDim2.new(1, -s(160), 0, 1)
            line.Position = UDim2.new(0, s(158), 0.5, 0)
            line.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
            line.BackgroundColor3 = VANISH.ACCENT2
            line.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
            line.BorderSizePixel = 0
            line.Parent = row
            VanishCorner(line, 999)
        end

        return page, card, sc, lay
    end

    -- Creates a horizontal sub-tab bar + independent scroll frames inside a page card.
    -- subTabDefs = { {key="...", label="..."}, ... }
    -- Returns: scrolls table (key → ScrollingFrame), setActive function
    local function makeSubTabPage(parentCard, subTabDefs)
        local STAB_H   = s(28)
        local STAB_GAP = s(4)
        local n = #subTabDefs

        -- ── Sub-tab button bar ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        local bar = Instance.new("Frame")
        bar.Name = "SubTabBar"
        bar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, STAB_H)
        bar.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 4)
        bar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
        bar.Parent = parentCard

        local barLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", bar)
        barLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
        barLayout.SortOrder  = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        barLayout.Padding    = UDim.new(0, STAB_GAP)

        -- ── Content area below the bar (CanvasGroup enables cross-fade) ─────────
        local contentY = STAB_H + 8
        local contentGroup = Instance.new("CanvasGroup")
        contentGroup.Name = "SubContent"
        contentGroup.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -contentY)
        contentGroup.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, contentY)
        contentGroup.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        contentGroup.BorderSizePixel = 0
        contentGroup.GroupTransparency = 0
        contentGroup.Parent = parentCard

        local scrolls    = {}
        local btnRefs    = {}
        local curKey     = nil
        local isFirstTab = true  -- skip animation on initial load

        local function setActive(key)
            -- ── Button highlight animation ───────────────────────────────────
            for k, refs in pairs(btnRefs) do
                local sel = (k == key)
                TweenService:Create(refs.btn, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                    BackgroundColor3       = sel and VANISH.ACCENT or VANISH.SURF2,
                    BackgroundTransparency = sel and 0.72 or 0.55,
                }):Play()
                TweenService:Create(refs.lbl, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                    TextColor3 = sel and VANISH.TEXT or VANISH.DIM,
                }):Play()
                refs.lbl.Font = sel and Enum.Font.GothamBold or Enum.Font.GothamMedium
            end

            -- ── Content cross-fade ───────────────────────────────────────────
            if isFirstTab then
                isFirstTab = false
                curKey = key
                for k, sc in pairs(scrolls) do sc.Visible = (k == key) end
            else
                curKey = key
                TweenService:Create(contentGroup, TweenInfo.new(0.08, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {GroupTransparency = 1}):Play()
                task.delay(0.09, function()
                    for k, sc in pairs(scrolls) do sc.Visible = (k == key) end
                    TweenService:Create(contentGroup, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {GroupTransparency = 0}):Play()
                end)
            end
        end

        -- Equal-width buttons that fill the bar
        local bw   = n > 0 and (1 / n) or 1
        local boff = n > 1 and -math.ceil(STAB_GAP * (n - 1) / n) or 0

        for i, def in ipairs(subTabDefs) do
            local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
            btn.Name = def.key .. "SubTab"
            btn.AutoButtonColor = false
            btn.Size = UDim2.new(bw, boff, 1, 0)
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = VANISH.SURF2
            btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55
            btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
            btn.Text = ""
            btn.LayoutOrder = i
            btn.Parent = bar
            VanishCorner(btn, s(6))

            local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", btn)
            lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            lbl.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            lbl.Text = def.label
            lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
            lbl.TextSize = s(12)
            lbl.TextColor3 = VANISH.DIM
            lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
            lbl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center

            -- Scroll frame for this sub-tab's content
            local sc = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
            sc.Name = def.key .. "Scroll"
            sc.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            sc.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            sc.BorderSizePixel = 0
            sc.ScrollBarThickness = 4
            sc.ScrollBarImageColor3 = VANISH.ACCENT2
            sc.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
            sc.Visible = false
            sc.Parent = contentGroup

            local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding", sc)
            pad.PaddingLeft   = UDim.new(0, 8)
            pad.PaddingRight  = UDim.new(0, 8)
            pad.PaddingTop    = UDim.new(0, 6)
            pad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 12)

            local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout", sc)
            lay.Padding   = UDim.new(0, 6)
            lay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
            lay:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
                sc.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, lay.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 18)
            end)

            scrolls[def.key] = sc
            btnRefs[def.key] = { btn = btn, lbl = lbl }

            btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setActive(def.key) end)
        end

        if #subTabDefs > 0 then setActive(subTabDefs[1].key) end
        return scrolls, setActive
    end

    local Pages = {}
    PageCards = {}
    _G.VanishPageScrolls = _G.VanishPageScrolls or {}

    -- Helper: build a sub-tab page and register all its scrolls
    -- Registry so search navigation can switch sub-tabs directly by key.
    _G.VanishSubTabSwitch = {}

    local function setupSubTabs(pageName, displayName, tabDefs)
        local page, card = makePage(pageName, displayName, true)
        Pages[pageName]     = page
        PageCards[pageName] = card
        local scrolls, setActiveFn = makeSubTabPage(card, tabDefs)
        for k, sc in pairs(scrolls) do
            _G.VanishPageScrolls[k] = sc
            -- Store a direct switcher for each sub-tab key
            local subKey = k
            _G.VanishSubTabSwitch[subKey] = function()
                setActiveFn(subKey)
            end
        end
        return scrolls, setActiveFn
    end

    -- Main — 4 sub-tabs
    setupSubTabs("Main", nil, {
        { key = "Main_Teleport",   label = "Teleport"   },
        { key = "Main_AutoSteal",  label = "Auto Steal" },
        { key = "Main_Automation", label = "Automation" },
        { key = "Main_Visuals",    label = "Visuals"    },
    })

    -- Player — flat list
    do
        local page, card, sc = makePage("Player", "Player")
        Pages.Player     = page
        PageCards.Player = card
        _G.VanishPageScrolls.Player = sc
    end

    -- Misc — 2 sub-tabs
    setupSubTabs("Misc", nil, {
        { key = "Misc_General",  label = "General"        },
        { key = "Misc_AntiFeat", label = "Anti Features"  },
    })

    -- Admin — 4 sub-tabs
    setupSubTabs("Admin", nil, {
        { key = "Admin_ClickAP", label = "Click to AP" },
        { key = "Admin_Atlas",   label = "Atlas"       },
        { key = "Admin_Kawaifu", label = "Kawaifu"     },
        { key = "Admin_Moby",    label = "Moby"        },
    })

    -- Priority — flat
    do
        local page, card, sc = makePage("Priority")
        Pages.Priority     = page
        PageCards.Priority = card
        _G.VanishPageScrolls.Priority = sc
    end

    -- Keybinds — 3 sub-tabs
    setupSubTabs("Keybinds", nil, {

        { key = "Keybinds_Abilities", label = "Abilities" },

    -- Settings — 3 sub-tabs
    local settingsScrolls = setupSubTabs("Settings", nil, {
        { key = "Settings_UI",      label = "UI"      },
        { key = "Settings_Camera",  label = "Camera"  },
        { key = "Settings_Animals", label = "Animals" },
    })

    -- sList / sLayout: fallback parent used by CreateRow when _G.VanishActiveScroll is nil.
    -- Since SetVanishUIPage is always called before any CreateRow, this is never actually hit,
    -- but it must be a valid Instance.
    sList   = settingsScrolls["Settings_UI"]
    sLayout = sList:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout")
    _G.VanishPageScrolls.Settings = sList
    _G.VanishActiveScroll = sList

    local TABS = {
        { key = "Main",     label = "Main",     icon = "rbxassetid://10723415903" },
        { key = "Player",   label = "Player",   icon = "rbxassetid://10747384394" },
        { key = "Misc",     label = "Misc",     icon = "rbxassetid://10709790474" },
        { key = "Admin",    label = "Admin",    icon = "rbxassetid://10734897398" },
        { key = "Priority", label = "Priority", icon = "rbxassetid://10733765400" },
        { key = "Keybinds", label = "Keybinds", icon = "rbxassetid://10723416952" },
        { key = "Settings", label = "Settings", icon = "rbxassetid://10734897398" },
    }

    local tabButtons = {}
    local currentKey = nil

    local function makeSideTab(def, order)
        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        btn.Name = def.key .. "Tab"
        btn.AutoButtonColor = false
        btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32)
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 170, 255)
        btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        btn.Text = ""
        btn.LayoutOrder = order
        btn.Parent = tabHolder
        VanishCorner(btn, 6)

        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        label.Name = "Label"
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0)
        label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0)
        label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        label.Text = def.label
        label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 155, 165)
        label.TextSize = 14
        label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        label.Parent = btn

        btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            if currentKey ~= def.key then
                TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 32, 40), BackgroundTransparency = 0.5}):Play()
                TweenService:Create(label, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)}):Play()
            end
        end)

        btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            if currentKey ~= def.key then
                TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
                TweenService:Create(label, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 155, 165)}):Play()
            end
        end)

        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            showTab(def.key)
        end)

        tabButtons[def.key] = {
            Button = btn,
            Label = label,
        }
    end

    showTab = function(key)
        currentKey = key

        for name, page in pairs(Pages) do
            local isSelected = (name == key)
            if isSelected then
                page.Visible = true
                local card = PageCards[name]
                if card then
                    card.GroupTransparency = 1
                    TweenService:Create(card, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {GroupTransparency = 0}):Play()
                end
            else
                page.Visible = false
            end
        end

        for name, refs in pairs(tabButtons) do
            local selected = (name == key)
            TweenService:Create(refs.Button, TweenInfo.new(0.20, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {
                BackgroundTransparency = selected and 0.78 or 1,
                BackgroundColor3 = selected and VANISH.ACCENT or Color3.fromRGB(28, 32, 40),
            }):Play()
            refs.Label.Font = selected and Enum.Font.GothamBold or Enum.Font.GothamMedium
            TweenService:Create(refs.Label, TweenInfo.new(0.20, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {
                TextColor3 = selected and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(150, 155, 165),
            }):Play()
        end

        local pages = _G.VanishPageScrolls
        if pages and pages[key] then
            _G.VanishActiveScroll = pages[key]
        else
            _G.VanishActiveScroll = sList
        end

        if key == "Priority" and _G.ReloadEmbeddedPriorityUi then
            _G.ReloadEmbeddedPriorityUi()
        end
    end

    -- Expose so applyAccentColor can re-style the active tab instantly
    _G.VanishRefreshCurrentTab = function()
        if currentKey then showTab(currentKey) end
    end

    for i, def in ipairs(TABS) do
        makeSideTab(def, i)
    end

    task.defer(function()
        task.wait()
        showTab("Main")
    end)

    function _G.SetVanishUIPage(name)
        local pages = _G.VanishPageScrolls
        if pages and pages[name] then
            _G.VanishActiveScroll = pages[name]
            showTab(name)
        else
            _G.VanishActiveScroll = sList
        end
    end

    local function CreateToggleSwitch(parent, initialState, callback)
        local sw = Instance.new("Frame")
        sw.Size = UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 22)
        sw.Position = UDim2.new(1, -56, 0.5, -11)
        sw.BackgroundColor3 = initialState and VANISH.ACCENT or Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)
        sw.BorderSizePixel = 0
        sw.Parent = parent
        do local c = Instance.new("UICorner"); c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0); c.Parent = sw end

        local dot = Instance.new("Frame")
        dot.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20)
        dot.Position = initialState and UDim2.new(1, -21, 0, 1) or UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, 1)
        dot.BackgroundColor3 = initialState and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
        dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dot.Parent = sw
        do local c = Instance.new("UICorner"); c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0); c.Parent = dot end

        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        btn.Text = ""
        btn.AutoButtonColor = false
        btn.Parent = sw

        local isOn = initialState == true

        local function applyVisual(state)
            if state then
                VanishTween(sw, 0.12, {BackgroundColor3 = VANISH.ACCENT})
                VanishTween(dot, 0.12, {Position = UDim2.new(1, -21, 0, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)})
            else
                VanishTween(sw, 0.12, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)})
                VanishTween(dot, 0.12, {Position = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)})
            end
        end

        local function SetState(state)
            isOn = state == true
            applyVisual(isOn)
        end

        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            callback(not isOn, function(state)
                isOn = state == true
                applyVisual(isOn)
            end)
        end)

        return {Set = SetState, Container = sw}
    end

    local function CreateRow(text, height)
        local row = Instance.new("Frame")
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height or s(40))
        row.BackgroundColor3 = VANISH.SURF
        row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
        row.BorderSizePixel = 0
        row.Parent = (_G.VanishActiveScroll or sList)
        VanishCorner(row, s(8))
        VanishPad(row, s(12), s(8), s(12), s(8))

        local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(s(60) + s(14)), 1, 0)
        lbl.Text = text
        lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        lbl.TextSize = s(13)
        lbl.TextColor3 = VANISH.TEXT
        lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        lbl.Parent = row

        return row
    end

    local function CreatePageHeader(text)
        local row = Instance.new("Frame")
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, s(30))
        row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        row.Parent = (_G.VanishActiveScroll or sList)

        local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -s(8), 1, 0)
        lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, s(4), 0, 0)
        lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lbl.Text = text
        lbl.TextColor3 = VANISH.TEXT
        lbl.TextSize = s(12)
        lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        lbl.Parent = row

        return row
    end

    local function CreateSectionHeader(text)
        local row = Instance.new("Frame")
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, s(26))
        row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        row.Parent = (_G.VanishActiveScroll or sList)

        local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -s(8), 1, 0)
        lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, s(2), 0, 0)
        lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lbl.Text = text
        lbl.TextColor3 = VANISH.ACCENT
        lbl.TextSize = s(14)
        lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        lbl.Parent = row

        return row
    end

    local function CreateSubHeader(text)
        local row = Instance.new("Frame")
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, s(18))
        row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        row.Parent = (_G.VanishActiveScroll or sList)

        local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -s(14), 1, 0)
        lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, s(10), 0, 0)
        lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lbl.Text = text
        lbl.TextColor3 = Theme.TextSecondary
        lbl.TextSize = s(11)
        lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        lbl.Parent = row

        return row
    end

    -- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- SEARCH BAR — index every CreateRow + CreateToggleSwitch, then search
    -- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    local _searchIndex       = {}   -- array of index entries
    local _searchPendingEntry = nil  -- last CreateRow entry (links with its toggle)

    -- Human-readable name for each page/sub-tab key
    local _pageDisplayNames = {
        Main_Teleport      = "Teleport",
        Main_AutoSteal     = "Auto Steal",
        Main_Automation    = "Automation",
        Main_Visuals       = "Visuals",
        Player             = "Player",
        Misc_General       = "General",
        Misc_AntiFeat      = "Anti Features",
        Admin_ClickAP      = "Click to AP",
        Admin_Atlas        = "Atlas",
        Admin_Kawaifu      = "Kawaifu",
        Admin_Moby         = "Moby",
        Priority           = "Priority",
        Keybinds_Actions   = "Actions",
        Keybinds_Abilities = "Abilities",
        Keybinds_Menu      = "Menu",
        Settings_UI        = "UI",
        Settings_Camera    = "Camera",
        Settings_Animals   = "Animals",
    }

    -- Wrap CreateToggleSwitch to track state + link to pending search entry
    local _origCreateToggleSwitch = CreateToggleSwitch
    CreateToggleSwitch = function(parent, initialState, outerCallback)
        local stateBox = { v = initialState == true }
        -- Wrap callback so stateBox stays current when the real toggle is clicked
        local trackedCallback = function(ns, set)
            stateBox.v = ns == true
            outerCallback(ns, set)
        end
        local sw = _origCreateToggleSwitch(parent, initialState, trackedCallback)
        if _searchPendingEntry then
            _searchPendingEntry.toggleRef      = sw            -- {Set, Container}
            _searchPendingEntry.toggleCallback = outerCallback -- original (Config updater)
            _searchPendingEntry.stateBox       = stateBox
            _searchPendingEntry = nil
        end
        return sw
    end

    -- Wrap CreateRow to add every feature row to the search index
    local _origCreateRow = CreateRow
    CreateRow = function(text, height)
        local row = _origCreateRow(text, height)
        -- Figure out which sub-tab page this row belongs to
        local pageKey = "Unknown"
        for k, sc in pairs(_G.VanishPageScrolls) do
            if _G.VanishActiveScroll == sc then pageKey = k; break end
        end
        local entry = { label = text, pageKey = pageKey, rowRef = row }
        table.insert(_searchIndex, entry)
        _searchPendingEntry = entry  -- CreateToggleSwitch (if called next) picks this up
        return row
    end

    -- ── Core search (Option 1: click-to-navigate results panel) ─────────────
    local _searchActive = false

    local function _clearResultRows()
        for _, c in ipairs(searchResultsScroll:GetChildren()) do
            if not c:IsA("UIListLayout") and not c:IsA("UIPadding") then
                c:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end

    local function _closeSearch()
        searchInput.Text       = ""
        searchClearBtn.Visible = false
        _searchActive          = false
        searchResultsFrame.Visible = false
        _clearResultRows()
    end

    -- Navigate to any page key, including sub-tabs (e.g. "Main_Teleport").
    -- Uses _G.VanishSubTabSwitch (populated by setupSubTabs) to switch directly.
    local _origShowTab  -- assigned below after the showTab override
    local function _navigateToPage(pageKey)
        local parentKey = pageKey:match("^([^_]+)") or pageKey
        _origShowTab(parentKey)
        if parentKey ~= pageKey then
            -- Switch to the correct sub-tab using the stored setActiveFn closure
            local switcher = _G.VanishSubTabSwitch and _G.VanishSubTabSwitch[pageKey]
            if switcher then
                task.defer(function()
                    task.wait()
                    switcher()
                end)
            end
        end
    end

    -- Build one result row: feature name + tab badge + live toggle (if the feature has one).
    -- Clicking the row (outside the toggle) navigates to the tab and flashes the row.
    local function _makeResultRow(entry)
        local hasToggle = entry.toggleRef and entry.stateBox ~= nil

        local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
        row.Size                   = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, s(38))
        row.BackgroundColor3       = VANISH.SURF
        row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
        row.BorderSizePixel        = 0
        row.Text                   = ""
        row.AutoButtonColor        = false
        row.ZIndex                 = 52
        row.Parent                 = searchResultsScroll
        VanishCorner(row, s(6))

        -- Tab badge (right side; shift left if toggle present)
        local badgePad = hasToggle and s(62) or s(8)
        local badge = Instance.new("Frame", row)
        badge.AutomaticSize          = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
        badge.Size                   = UDim2.fromOffset(0, s(18))
        badge.Position               = UDim2.new(1, -badgePad, 0.5, 0)
        badge.AnchorPoint            = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
        badge.BackgroundColor3       = VANISH.ACCENT
        badge.BackgroundTransparency = 0.72
        badge.BorderSizePixel        = 0
        badge.ZIndex                 = 53
        VanishCorner(badge, 999)
        do
            local bp = Instance.new("UIPadding", badge)
            bp.PaddingLeft  = UDim.new(0, 6)
            bp.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6)
            local bl = Instance.new("TextLabel", badge)
            bl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            bl.Size                   = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
            bl.AutomaticSize          = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
            bl.Text                   = _pageDisplayNames[entry.pageKey] or entry.pageKey
            bl.Font                   = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
            bl.TextSize               = s(10)
            bl.TextColor3             = VANISH.ACCENT
            bl.ZIndex                 = 54
        end

        -- Feature name
        local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
        lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lbl.Size                   = UDim2.new(1, -(badgePad + s(68)), 1, 0)
        lbl.Position               = UDim2.fromOffset(s(10), 0)
        lbl.Text                   = entry.label
        lbl.Font                   = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        lbl.TextSize               = s(13)
        lbl.TextColor3             = VANISH.TEXT
        lbl.TextXAlignment         = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        lbl.ZIndex                 = 53

        -- Live toggle switch (syncs with the real toggle on the page)
        if hasToggle then
            _origCreateToggleSwitch(row, entry.stateBox.v, function(ns, set)
                set(ns)
                entry.stateBox.v = ns
                pcall(entry.toggleRef.Set, ns)
                if entry.toggleCallback then
                    entry.toggleCallback(ns, function() end)
                end
            end)
        end

        -- Hover effect on the row background
        row.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            VanishTween(row, 0.1, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.25})
        end)
        row.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            VanishTween(row, 0.1, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.5})
        end)

        -- Click row → close search, navigate to tab, scroll to + flash the row
        row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local e = entry
            _closeSearch()
            _navigateToPage(e.pageKey)
            task.defer(function()
                task.wait(0.05)
                local scrollFrame = _G.VanishPageScrolls[e.pageKey]
                local rowRef      = e.rowRef
                if scrollFrame and rowRef and rowRef.Parent then
                    local rowAbsY    = rowRef.AbsolutePosition.Y
                    local scrollAbsY = scrollFrame.AbsolutePosition.Y
                    local offset     = rowAbsY - scrollAbsY + scrollFrame.CanvasPosition.Y
                    scrollFrame.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, offset - 40))
                    local origColor = rowRef.BackgroundColor3
                    local origAlpha = rowRef.BackgroundTransparency
                    VanishTween(rowRef, 0.12, {BackgroundColor3 = VANISH.ACCENT, BackgroundTransparency = 0.45})
                    task.delay(0.55, function()
                        VanishTween(rowRef, 0.25, {BackgroundColor3 = origColor, BackgroundTransparency = origAlpha})
                    end)
                end
            end)
        end)
    end

    local function _runSearch(rawQuery)
        _clearResultRows()
        local query = rawQuery:lower():match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""

        if query == "" then
            searchClearBtn.Visible     = false
            _searchActive              = false
            searchResultsFrame.Visible = false
            return
        end

        _searchActive              = true
        searchClearBtn.Visible     = true
        searchResultsFrame.Visible = true

        local matches = {}
        for _, entry in ipairs(_searchIndex) do
            if entry.label:lower():find(query, 1, true) then
                table.insert(matches, entry)
            end
        end

        if #matches == 0 then
            local noResult = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            noResult.Size                   = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, s(44))
            noResult.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            noResult.Text                   = 'No features found for "' .. rawQuery .. '"'
            noResult.TextColor3             = VANISH.DIM
            noResult.Font                   = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
            noResult.TextSize               = s(13)
            noResult.TextXAlignment         = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
            noResult.TextYAlignment         = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
            noResult.ZIndex                 = 52
            noResult.Parent                 = searchResultsScroll
        else
            for _, entry in ipairs(matches) do
                _makeResultRow(entry)
            end
        end
    end

    -- Wire up TextBox + clear button
    searchInput:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        _runSearch(searchInput.Text)
    end)

    searchClearBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        searchInput.Text = ""
        searchInput:CaptureFocus()
    end)

    -- When user manually switches tab, close the results panel
    _origShowTab = showTab
    showTab = function(key)
        if _searchActive then
            _closeSearch()
        end
        _origShowTab(key)
    end
    -- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    -- (TP V2 toggle removed — replaced by the "Teleport Method" dropdown
    --  added below the Teleport Tool dropdown.)

    -- Min Gen for Auto TP — sits right under Auto Teleport, hidden when toggle is off
    rMinGen = CreateRow("Min Gen for Auto TP (M/s)")
    rMinGen.Visible = Config.TpSettings.TpOnLoad == true
    local minGenBox = Instance.new("TextBox", rMinGen)
    minGenBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 24)
    minGenBox.Position = UDim2.new(1, -110, 0.5, -12)
    minGenBox.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SurfaceHighlight
    minGenBox.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    minGenBox.TextSize = 11
    minGenBox.TextColor3 = Theme.TextPrimary
    minGenBox.PlaceholderText = "0"
    minGenBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    Instance.new("UICorner", minGenBox).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
    local function _fmtMinGen(v)
        v = tonumber(v) or 0
        return string.format("%g M", v)
    end
    do
        local raw = tostring(Config.TpSettings.MinGenForTp or "")
        local n = tonumber((raw:gsub("[^%d%.]", ""))) or 0
        local suf = raw:lower():match("[kmb]")
        if suf == "k" then n = n / 1000
        elseif suf == "b" then n = n * 1000 end
        Config.TpSettings.MinGenForTp = n
    end
    _G.TPMinValue = tonumber(Config.TpSettings.MinGenForTp) or 0
    minGenBox.Text = _fmtMinGen(Config.TpSettings.MinGenForTp)
    minGenBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local raw = tostring(minGenBox.Text or ""):gsub("[^%d%.]", "")
        local n = tonumber(raw) or 0
        Config.TpSettings.MinGenForTp = n
        _G.TPMinValue = n
        SaveConfig()
        minGenBox.Text = _fmtMinGen(n)
    end)

    local tpDropOptions = {"Flying Carpet", "Cupid's Wings", "Santa's Sleigh", "Witch's Broom", "Grapple Hook"}
    local function getTpDropLabel()
        local t = Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.Tool
        return (type(t) == "string" and #t > 0) and t or "Flying Carpet"
    end

    -- Accordion container: lives in the scroll list and expands downward, pushing rows below it
    local tpOptH    = s(36)
    local tpOptGap  = s(3)

    local tpExpandH = tpRowH + #tpDropOptions * (tpOptH + tpOptGap)

    tpContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, tpCollH)
    tpContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    tpContainer.BorderSizePixel = 0
    tpContainer.ClipsDescendants = true
    tpContainer.Parent = (_G.VanishActiveScroll or sList)

    local tpContLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", tpContainer)
    tpContLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    tpContLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, tpOptGap)

    -- Header row: always visible, styled to match other rows
    local tpSelRow = Instance.new("Frame")
    tpSelRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, tpRowH)
    tpSelRow.BackgroundColor3 = VANISH.SURF
    tpSelRow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
    tpSelRow.BorderSizePixel = 0
    tpSelRow.LayoutOrder = 1
    tpSelRow.Parent = tpContainer
    VanishCorner(tpSelRow, s(8))
    VanishPad(tpSelRow, s(12), s(8), s(12), s(8))

    local tpSelLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", tpSelRow)
    tpSelLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    tpSelLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -s(150), 1, 0)
    tpSelLabel.Text = "Teleport Tool"
    tpSelLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    tpSelLabel.TextSize = s(13)
    tpSelLabel.TextColor3 = VANISH.TEXT
    tpSelLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

    -- Pill showing the selected tool + chevron — clicking this opens/closes
    local tpSelPill = Instance.new("TextButton")
    tpSelPill.Size = UDim2.new(0, s(162), 0, s(30))
    tpSelPill.Position = UDim2.new(1, -s(168), 0.5, -s(15))
    tpSelPill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(24, 27, 42)
    tpSelPill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    tpSelPill.AutoButtonColor = false

    tpSelPill.Parent = tpSelRow

    tpPillStroke.Color = VANISH.ACCENT

    tpPillStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border

    tpSelText.Size = UDim2.new(1, -s(34), 1, 0)
    tpSelText.Position = UDim2.new(0, s(10), 0, 0)
    tpSelText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    tpSelText.Text = getTpDropLabel()
    tpSelText.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    tpSelText.TextSize = s(13)
    tpSelText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 228, 255)
    tpSelText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    tpSelText.ZIndex = 2

    -- Divider between text and arrow
    local tpPillDiv = Instance.new("Frame", tpSelPill)
    tpPillDiv.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, s(16))
    tpPillDiv.Position = UDim2.new(1, -s(30), 0.5, -s(8))
    tpPillDiv.BackgroundColor3 = VANISH.ACCENT
    tpPillDiv.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55

    local tpDdIsOpen = false

    local function closeTpDropdown()
        if not tpDdIsOpen then return end
        tpDdIsOpen = false
        VanishTween(tpContainer, 0.2, {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, tpCollH)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        VanishTween(tpChevron, 0.18, {Rotation = 0})
    end

    local tpOptBtns = {}

    for i, optName in ipairs(tpDropOptions) do
        local optRow = Instance.new("TextButton")
        optRow.LayoutOrder = i + 1
        optRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, tpOptH)
        optRow.BackgroundColor3 = getTpDropLabel() == optName
            and Color3.fromRGB(36, 52, 92)
            or Color3.fromRGB(22, 24, 37)
        optRow.BorderSizePixel = 0
        optRow.AutoButtonColor = false
        optRow.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        optRow.TextSize = s(12)
        optRow.TextColor3 = getTpDropLabel() == optName
            and Color3.fromRGB(235, 240, 255)
            or VANISH.TEXT
        optRow.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        optRow.Text = "    " .. optName
        optRow.Parent = tpContainer
        VanishCorner(optRow, s(6))
        tpOptBtns[optName] = optRow

        optRow.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Config.TpSettings.Tool = optName
            SaveConfig()
            if _G.VanishRefreshSpeedSlider then _G.VanishRefreshSpeedSlider() end
            tpSelText.Text = optName
            for n, ob in pairs(tpOptBtns) do
                local sel = n == optName
                VanishTween(ob, 0.12, {BackgroundColor3 = sel and Color3.fromRGB(36, 52, 92) or Color3.fromRGB(22, 24, 37)})
                ob.TextColor3 = sel and Color3.fromRGB(235, 240, 255) or VANISH.TEXT
            end
            closeTpDropdown()
        end)

        optRow.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            if getTpDropLabel() ~= optName then
                VanishTween(optRow, 0.1, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(32, 44, 72)})
            end
        end)
        optRow.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            local sel = getTpDropLabel() == optName
            VanishTween(optRow, 0.1, {BackgroundColor3 = sel and Color3.fromRGB(36, 52, 92) or Color3.fromRGB(22, 24, 37)})
        end)
    end

    tpSelPill.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if tpDdIsOpen then
            closeTpDropdown()
        else
            tpDdIsOpen = true
            VanishTween(tpContainer, 0.25, {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, tpExpandH)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
            VanishTween(tpChevron, 0.2, {Rotation = 180})
        end
    end)

    -- TELEPORT METHOD dropdown removed — only one method (Tween
    -- Teleport) remained after Clone Ownership TP was patched.
    -- Config keys hardcoded so downstream _useV2 logic stays sane.
    Config.TeleportMethod = "Tween Teleport"
    Config.TpV2NoAP       = true

    -- ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    -- INPUT METHOD DROPDOWNS (Clone + AP)
    -- Style matches the Teleport Tool / Teleport Method dropdowns above.
    -- Lets the user pick how Quantum Cloner button + Admin Panel button
    -- clicks are dispatched (Net-free):
    --   "FireSignal"          → firesignal() on the GuiButton's events
    --   "VirtualInputManager" → Instance.new("VirtualInputManager") +
    --                            SendMouseButtonEvent at the button's pos
    -- ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    local _methodOpts = {"Remote", "FireSignal", "VirtualInputManager"}

    local function _makeMethodDropdown(labelText, configKey, defaultVal, optsList)
        local _opts = optsList or _methodOpts
        if type(Config[configKey]) ~= "string" or not table.find(_opts, Config[configKey]) then
            Config[configKey] = defaultVal
        end
        local function _getLabel() return Config[configKey] or defaultVal end

        local _optH    = s(36)
        local _optGap  = s(3)
        local _rowH    = s(40)
        local _collH   = _rowH
        local _expandH = _rowH + #_opts * (_optH + _optGap)

        local _container = Instance.new("Frame")
        _container.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, _collH)
        _container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        _container.BorderSizePixel = 0
        _container.ClipsDescendants = true
        _container.Parent = (_G.VanishActiveScroll or sList)

        local _layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", _container)
        _layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        _layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, _optGap)

        local _selRow = Instance.new("Frame")
        _selRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, _rowH)
        _selRow.BackgroundColor3 = VANISH.SURF
        _selRow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
        _selRow.BorderSizePixel = 0
        _selRow.LayoutOrder = 1
        _selRow.Parent = _container

        _selLabel.TextSize = s(13)

        _selLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

        _selPill.Position = UDim2.new(1, -s(226), 0.5, -s(15))
        _selPill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(24, 27, 42)
        _selPill.BorderSizePixel = 0
        _selPill.AutoButtonColor = false
        _selPill.Text = ""
        _selPill.Parent = _selRow
        VanishCorner(_selPill, s(7))
        local _stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", _selPill)
        _stroke.Color = VANISH.ACCENT
        _stroke.Thickness = 1.2
        _stroke.Transparency = 0.25
        _stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border

        local _selText = Instance.new("TextLabel", _selPill)
        _selText.Size = UDim2.new(1, -s(34), 1, 0)
        _selText.Position = UDim2.new(0, s(10), 0, 0)
        _selText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        _selText.Text = _getLabel()
        _selText.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        _selText.TextSize = s(12)

        _selText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

        _div.BackgroundColor3 = VANISH.ACCENT
        _div.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55
        _div.BorderSizePixel = 0
        _div.ZIndex = 2

        _chevron.Position = UDim2.new(1, -s(30), 0, 0)
        _chevron.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        _chevron.BorderSizePixel = 0
        _chevron.ZIndex = 3

        local _cL = Instance.new("Frame", _chevron)
        _cL.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(s(8), s(2))
        _cL.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        _cL.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -s(3), 0.5, 0)
        _cL.BackgroundColor3 = VANISH.ACCENT
        _cL.BorderSizePixel = 0
        _cL.Rotation = 35
        _cL.ZIndex = 3
        Instance.new("UICorner", _cL).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

        local _cR = Instance.new("Frame", _chevron)
        _cR.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(s(8), s(2))
        _cR.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        _cR.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, s(3), 0.5, 0)
        _cR.BackgroundColor3 = VANISH.ACCENT
        _cR.BorderSizePixel = 0
        _cR.Rotation = -35
        _cR.ZIndex = 3
        Instance.new("UICorner", _cR).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

        local _isOpen = false
        local function _close()
            if not _isOpen then return end
            _isOpen = false
            VanishTween(_container, 0.2, {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, _collH)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
            VanishTween(_chevron, 0.18, {Rotation = 0})
        end

        local _optBtns = {}
        for i, optName in ipairs(_opts) do
            local _opt = Instance.new("TextButton")
            _opt.LayoutOrder = i + 1
            _opt.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, _optH)
            _opt.BackgroundColor3 = _getLabel() == optName
                and Color3.fromRGB(36, 52, 92)
                or Color3.fromRGB(22, 24, 37)
            _opt.BorderSizePixel = 0
            _opt.AutoButtonColor = false
            _opt.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
            _opt.TextSize = s(12)
            _opt.TextColor3 = _getLabel() == optName
                and Color3.fromRGB(235, 240, 255)
                or VANISH.TEXT
            _opt.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            _opt.Text = "    " .. optName
            _opt.Parent = _container
            VanishCorner(_opt, s(6))
            _optBtns[optName] = _opt

            _opt.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                Config[configKey] = optName
                SaveConfig()
                _selText.Text = optName
                for n, ob in pairs(_optBtns) do
                    local sel = n == optName
                    VanishTween(ob, 0.12, {BackgroundColor3 = sel and Color3.fromRGB(36, 52, 92) or Color3.fromRGB(22, 24, 37)})
                    ob.TextColor3 = sel and Color3.fromRGB(235, 240, 255) or VANISH.TEXT
                end
                _close()
            end)

            _opt.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                if _getLabel() ~= optName then
                    VanishTween(_opt, 0.1, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(32, 44, 72)})
                end
            end)
            _opt.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                local sel = _getLabel() == optName
                VanishTween(_opt, 0.1, {BackgroundColor3 = sel and Color3.fromRGB(36, 52, 92) or Color3.fromRGB(22, 24, 37)})
            end)
        end

        _selPill.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if _isOpen then
                _close()
            else
                _isOpen = true
                VanishTween(_container, 0.25, {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, _expandH)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
                VanishTween(_chevron, 0.2, {Rotation = 180})
            end
        end)
    end

    local rSpeed = CreateRow("Teleport Speed")
    local SPEED_COUNT = 3
    local speedCont = Instance.new("Frame", rSpeed)
    speedCont.Size = UDim2.new(0, 108, 0, 26)
    speedCont.Position = UDim2.new(1, -118, 0.5, -13)
    speedCont.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 20, 28)
    speedCont.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
    speedCont.BorderSizePixel = 0
    speedCont.ClipsDescendants = true
    Instance.new("UICorner", speedCont).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    local speedContStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", speedCont)
    speedContStroke.Color = VANISH.ACCENT
    speedContStroke.Thickness = 1
    speedContStroke.Transparency = 0.75
    speedContStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border

    local speedAccent = VANISH.ACCENT
    local speedTextOff = Color3.fromRGB(150, 155, 170)
    local speedTextOn = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    local speedSlideInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
    local speedTextInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)

    local selectedDelayOption = math.clamp(tonumber(Config.TpSettings.Speed) or 1, 1, SPEED_COUNT)
    Config.TpSettings.Speed = selectedDelayOption

    local segWidth = 1 / SPEED_COUNT
    local speedIndicator = Instance.new("Frame", speedCont)
    speedIndicator.BackgroundColor3 = speedAccent
    speedIndicator.BorderSizePixel = 0
    speedIndicator.Size = UDim2.new(segWidth, -4, 1, -4)
    speedIndicator.Position = UDim2.new((selectedDelayOption - 1) * segWidth, 2, 0, 2)
    speedIndicator.ZIndex = 1
    Instance.new("UICorner", speedIndicator).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

    local speedBtns = {}
    for i = 1, SPEED_COUNT do
        local b = Instance.new("TextButton", speedCont)
        b.Size = UDim2.new(segWidth, 0, 1, 0)
        b.Position = UDim2.new((i - 1) * segWidth, 0, 0, 0)
        b.AutoButtonColor = false
        b.BorderSizePixel = 0
        b.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        b.Text = tostring(i)
        b.TextColor3 = (selectedDelayOption == i) and speedTextOn or speedTextOff
        b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        b.TextSize = 13
        b.ZIndex = 2

        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if Config.TpSettings.Speed == i then return end
            Config.TpSettings.Speed = i
            SaveConfig()
            TweenService:Create(speedIndicator, speedSlideInfo, {
                Position = UDim2.new((i - 1) * segWidth, 2, 0, 2),
            }):Play()
            for idx, btn in ipairs(speedBtns) do
                TweenService:Create(btn, speedTextInfo, {
                    TextColor3 = (idx == i) and speedTextOn or speedTextOff,
                }):Play()
            end
        end)
        table.insert(speedBtns, b)
    end

    local function CreateConditionalSlider(opts)
        local function _rv(x) return type(x) == "function" and x() or x end
        local row = CreateRow(opts.label)
        local rowFullSize = row.Size
        -- Mobile: thicker bar (18px) + larger knob (20px) so a finger can
        -- actually grab them. Desktop keeps the slim 5/12 look.
        local _sliderH    = IS_MOBILE and 18 or 5
        local _sliderHalf = _sliderH * 0.5
        local _knobSize   = IS_MOBILE and 20 or 12
        local sliderBg = Instance.new("Frame", row)
        sliderBg.Size = UDim2.new(0, 140, 0, _sliderH)
        sliderBg.Position = UDim2.new(1, -200, 0.5, -_sliderHalf)
        sliderBg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 32, 38)
        sliderBg.BorderSizePixel = 0
        Instance.new("UICorner", sliderBg).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        local fill = Instance.new("Frame", sliderBg)
        fill.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent1
        fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
        fill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
        Instance.new("UICorner", fill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        local knob = Instance.new("Frame", sliderBg)
        knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, _knobSize, 0, _knobSize)
        knob.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.TextPrimary
        knob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        knob.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
        knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
        Instance.new("UICorner", knob).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        local knobStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", knob)
        knobStroke.Color = Theme.Accent1
        knobStroke.Thickness = 1.5
        knobStroke.Transparency = 0.2
        local valLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
        valLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 20)
        valLbl.Position = UDim2.new(1, -55, 0.5, -10)
        valLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        valLbl.TextColor3 = Theme.TextPrimary
        valLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        valLbl.TextSize = 12

        local function snap(v)
            local mn, mx, st = _rv(opts.min), _rv(opts.max), _rv(opts.step)
            local steps = math.floor((v - mn) / st + 0.5)
            return math.clamp(mn + steps * st, mn, mx)
        end
        local function setValue(v, silent)
            v = snap(v)
            opts.set(v)
            if not silent then SaveConfig() end
            valLbl.Text = string.format(opts.fmt, v)
            local mn, mx = _rv(opts.min), _rv(opts.max)
            local pct = (mx > mn) and (v - mn) / (mx - mn) or 0
            fill.Size = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 1, 0)
            knob.Position = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 0.5, 0)
        end
        setValue(opts.get(), true)

        local dragging = false
        sliderBg.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then dragging = true end
        end)
        UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then dragging = false end
        end)
        UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if dragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                local x = i.Position.X
                local r = sliderBg.AbsolutePosition.X
                local w = sliderBg.AbsoluteSize.X
                local p = math.clamp((x - r) / w, 0, 1)
                setValue(_rv(opts.min) + p * (_rv(opts.max) - _rv(opts.min)))
            end
        end)

        if opts.exposeRefresh then opts.exposeRefresh(function() setValue(opts.get(), true) end) end
        local function setVisible(show)
            row.Visible = show
            row.Size = show and rowFullSize or UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        end
        return setVisible
    end

    local setJoinDelayVisible = CreateConditionalSlider({
        label = "Teleport Delay on Join",
        min = 0.1, max = 2, step = 0.1, fmt = "%.1fs",
        get = function() return tonumber(Config.TpSettings.TpJoinDelay) or 0.3 end,
        set = function(v) Config.TpSettings.TpJoinDelay = v end,
    })

        label = "Teleport Speed",
        min = function() return (Config.TpSettings.Tool == "Grapple Hook") and 180 or 150 end,
        max = function() return (Config.TpSettings.Tool == "Grapple Hook") and 230 or 180 end,
        step = 5, fmt = "%d",
        get = function()
            if Config.TpSettings.Tool == "Grapple Hook" then return tonumber(Config.TpGrappleSpeed) or 200 end
            return tonumber(Config.TpV2Speed) or 150
        end,
        set = function(v)
            if Config.TpSettings.Tool == "Grapple Hook" then Config.TpGrappleSpeed = v else Config.TpV2Speed = v end
        end,
        exposeRefresh = function(fn) _G.VanishRefreshSpeedSlider = fn end,
    })
    _G.VanishTpV2SetSliderVisible = setTpV2SpeedVisible

        local SKY_OPTIONS = {"Off", "Moonlight", "Space", "Dark", "Classic"}
        if type(Config.SkyPreset) ~= "string" or not table.find(SKY_OPTIONS, Config.SkyPreset) then
            Config.SkyPreset = "Off"
        end

        local skyRow = CreateRow("Sky Preset")

        -- Pill button showing current preset — left-click cycles forward, right-click cycles back
        local pill = Instance.new("TextButton", skyRow)
        pill.Size        = UDim2.new(0, 120, 0, 24)
        pill.Position    = UDim2.new(1, -126, 0.5, -12)
        pill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(24, 27, 42)
        pill.BorderSizePixel  = 0
        pill.AutoButtonColor  = false
        pill.Font         = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
        pill.TextSize     = 13
        pill.TextColor3   = Color3.fromRGB(200, 210, 255)
        pill.Text         = "◀  " .. Config.SkyPreset .. "  ▶"
        Instance.new("UICorner", pill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        local pillStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", pill)
        pillStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(80, 100, 200)
        pillStroke.Thickness = 1.2
        pillStroke.Transparency = 0.3

        local function applyPreset(preset)
            Config.SkyPreset = preset
            SaveConfig()
            pill.Text = "◀  " .. preset .. "  ▶"
            if _G.VanishSkyPreset then _G.VanishSkyPreset.set(preset) end
        end

        pill.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local idx = table.find(SKY_OPTIONS, Config.SkyPreset) or 1
            applyPreset(SKY_OPTIONS[(idx % #SKY_OPTIONS) + 1])
        end)
        pill.MouseButton2Click:Connect(function()
            local idx = table.find(SKY_OPTIONS, Config.SkyPreset) or 1
            applyPreset(SKY_OPTIONS[((idx - 2) % #SKY_OPTIONS) + 1])
        end)
    end

    local highestToggleRef = {}
    local priorityToggleRef = {}
    autoTPPriorityToggleRef = autoTPPriorityToggleRef or {setFn = nil}

    local rDefaultNearest = CreateRow("Default To Nearest")
    local nearestToggleSwitch = CreateToggleSwitch(rDefaultNearest, Config.DefaultToNearest, function(ns, set)
        if ns then
            Config.DefaultToNearest = true
            Config.DefaultToHighest = false
            Config.DefaultToPriority = false
            set(true)
            if highestToggleRef.setFn then highestToggleRef.setFn(false) end
            if priorityToggleRef.setFn then priorityToggleRef.setFn(false) end
            
            Config.AutoTPPriority = true
            if autoTPPriorityToggleRef and autoTPPriorityToggleRef.setFn then
                autoTPPriorityToggleRef.setFn(true)
            end
        else
            local otherDefaults = Config.DefaultToHighest or Config.DefaultToPriority
            if not otherDefaults then
                set(true)
                return
            end
            Config.DefaultToNearest = false
            set(false)
        end
        SaveConfig()
    end)
    nearestToggleRef.setFn = nearestToggleSwitch.Set

    local rDefaultHighest = CreateRow("Default To Highest")
    local highestToggleSwitch = CreateToggleSwitch(rDefaultHighest, Config.DefaultToHighest, function(ns, set)
        if ns then

            if autoTPPriorityToggleRef and autoTPPriorityToggleRef.setFn then
                autoTPPriorityToggleRef.setFn(false)
            end
        else
            local otherDefaults = Config.DefaultToNearest or Config.DefaultToPriority
            if not otherDefaults then
                set(true)
                return
            end
            Config.DefaultToHighest = false
            set(false)
        end
        SaveConfig()
    end)

    local minBox = Instance.new("TextBox", rAutoBuyMin)
    minBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 24)
    minBox.Position = UDim2.new(1, -110, 0.5, -12)
    minBox.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SurfaceHighlight

    minBox.TextColor3 = Theme.TextPrimary
    minBox.PlaceholderText = "0"
    minBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    Instance.new("UICorner", minBox).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
    local function _fmtMin(v)
        v = tonumber(v) or 0
        return string.format("%g M", v)
    end
    minBox.Text = _fmtMin(Config.AutoSnipeRoadMinM)
    minBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local raw = tostring(minBox.Text or ""):gsub("[^%d%.]", "")
        local n = tonumber(raw) or 0
        Config.AutoSnipeRoadMinM = n
        _G.AutoSnipeRoadMinM = n
        SaveConfig()
        minBox.Text = _fmtMin(n)
    end)

        set(ns); Config.CompactAutoSteal = ns; SaveConfig()
        local asUI = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("AutoStealUI")
        if asUI and asUI:FindFirstChild("Frame") then
            local frame = asUI.Frame
            local mobileScale = IS_MOBILE and 0.6 or 1
            local targetHeight = ns and (5 * 44 + 135) or (630 * mobileScale)

            local tween = TweenService:Create(
                frame,
                TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut),
                {Size = UDim2.new(frame.Size.X.Scale, frame.Size.X.Offset, 0, targetHeight)}
            )
            tween:Play()

            tween.Completed:Connect(function()
                SharedState.ListNeedsRedraw = true
                if SharedState.UpdateAutoStealUI then
                    SharedState.UpdateAutoStealUI()
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

                Config.ProximityAPKeybind=NormalizeKeyName(inp.KeyCode.Name, Config.ProximityAPKeybind or "P"); Config.SavedKeybinds.ProximityAPKeybind = Config.ProximityAPKeybind; bProximityAPKeybind.Text=PrettyKeyName(Config.ProximityAPKeybind)
                bProximityAPKeybind.TextColor3=Theme.TextPrimary; SaveConfig(); con:Disconnect()
            end
        end)
    end)

    end
