    task.wait()
    do
    task.wait()
    _G.SetVanishUIPage("Settings_Camera")
    local rFOV = CreateRow("FOV")
    local fovSliderBg = Instance.new("Frame", rFOV)
    -- Mobile: thicker bar + bigger knob so a finger can grab them.
    local _fovH    = IS_MOBILE and 18 or 5
    local _fovHalf = _fovH * 0.5
    local _fovKnob = IS_MOBILE and 20 or 12
    fovSliderBg.Size = UDim2.new(0, 140, 0, _fovH)
    fovSliderBg.Position = UDim2.new(1, -200, 0.5, -_fovHalf)
    fovSliderBg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 32, 38)
    Instance.new("UICorner", fovSliderBg).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    local fovFill = Instance.new("Frame", fovSliderBg)
    fovFill.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent1
    fovFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
    Instance.new("UICorner", fovFill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    local fovKnob = Instance.new("Frame", fovSliderBg)
    fovKnob.Size = UDim2.new(0, _fovKnob, 0, _fovKnob)
    fovKnob.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.TextPrimary
    fovKnob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    fovKnob.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
    Instance.new("UICorner", fovKnob).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    local fovKnobStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", fovKnob)
    fovKnobStroke.Color = Theme.Accent1
    fovKnobStroke.Thickness = 1.5
    fovKnobStroke.Transparency = 0.2
    local fovValLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", rFOV)
    fovValLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 20)
    fovValLbl.Position = UDim2.new(1, -50, 0.5, -10)
    fovValLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    fovValLbl.Text = string.format("%.1f", Config.FOV)
    fovValLbl.TextColor3 = Theme.TextPrimary
    fovValLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    fovValLbl.TextSize = 13

    local function updateFOVSlider(val)
        val = math.clamp(val, 30, 180)
        Config.FOV = val
        SaveConfig()
        fovValLbl.Text = string.format("%.1f", val)
        local pct = (val - 30) / 150
        fovFill.Size = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 1, 0)
        fovKnob.Position = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 0.5, 0)
        if Workspace.CurrentCamera then
            Workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = val
        end
    end
    updateFOVSlider(Config.FOV)

    local fovDragging = false
    fovSliderBg.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
        if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then fovDragging = true end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
        if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then fovDragging = false end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
        if fovDragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local x = i.Position.X
            local r = fovSliderBg.AbsolutePosition.X
            local w = fovSliderBg.AbsoluteSize.X
            local p = (x - r) / w
            updateFOVSlider(30 + (p * 150))
        end
    end)

    local rFOVReset = CreateRow("Reset FOV")
    local bFOVReset = Instance.new("TextButton", rFOVReset)
    bFOVReset.Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 24)
    bFOVReset.Position = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0.5, -12)
    bFOVReset.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SurfaceHighlight
    bFOVReset.Text = "Reset"
    bFOVReset.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    bFOVReset.TextColor3 = Theme.TextPrimary
    bFOVReset.TextSize = 12
    Instance.new("UICorner", bFOVReset).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
    bFOVReset.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        updateFOVSlider(70)
    end)

    end
    task.wait()
    do
    task.wait()
    _G.SetVanishUIPage("Keybinds_Menu")
    Config.MenuKey = NormalizeKeyName((Config.SavedKeybinds and Config.SavedKeybinds.MenuKey) or Config.MenuKey, "LeftControl")
    if not IS_MOBILE then
        local rMenu = CreateRow("Menu Toggle Key")
        local bMenu = Instance.new("TextButton", rMenu)
        bMenu.Size=UDim2.new(0,80,0,24); bMenu.Position=UDim2.new(1,-90,0.5,-12)
        bMenu.BackgroundColor3=Theme.SurfaceHighlight; bMenu.Text=PrettyKeyName(Config.MenuKey or "LeftControl")
        bMenu.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; bMenu.TextColor3=Theme.TextPrimary; bMenu.TextSize=12
        Instance.new("UICorner",bMenu).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,4)
        bMenu.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            bMenu.Text="..."; bMenu.TextColor3=Theme.Accent1
            local con; con=UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
                if inp.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
                    Config.MenuKey=NormalizeKeyName(inp.KeyCode.Name, Config.MenuKey or "LeftControl"); Config.SavedKeybinds.MenuKey = Config.MenuKey; bMenu.Text=PrettyKeyName(Config.MenuKey)
                    bMenu.TextColor3=Theme.TextPrimary; SaveConfig(); con:Disconnect()
                end
            end)
        end)
    else
        task.wait()
        _G.SetVanishUIPage("Keybinds_Menu")
        CreateRow("Menu Toggle: Touch Icon")
    end

    end
    task.wait()
    do
    task.wait()
    _G.SetVanishUIPage("Settings_Animals")

    local rHideStealPics = CreateRow("Hide Animal Pictures (Steal Target)")
    CreateToggleSwitch(rHideStealPics, Config.HideStealTargetPictures, function(ns, set)
        set(ns); Config.HideStealTargetPictures = ns; SaveConfig()
        _G.HideStealTargetPictures = ns
    end)
    _G.HideStealTargetPictures = Config.HideStealTargetPictures == true

    local rHidePrioPics = CreateRow("Hide Animal Pictures (Priority)")
    CreateToggleSwitch(rHidePrioPics, Config.HidePriorityPictures, function(ns, set)
        set(ns); Config.HidePriorityPictures = ns; SaveConfig()
        _G.HidePriorityPictures = ns
    end)
    _G.HidePriorityPictures = Config.HidePriorityPictures == true


    end
    task.wait()
    do
    task.wait()
    _G.SetVanishUIPage("Settings_UI")
    CreateSectionHeader("UI CONTROLS")
    local rLock = CreateRow("Lock UI Dragging")
    CreateToggleSwitch(rLock, Config.UILocked, function(ns, set)
        set(ns); Config.UILocked = ns; SaveConfig()
    end)

    if IS_MOBILE then
        local scaleUI = {}
        scaleUI.row = CreateRow("Mobile GUI Scale")
        scaleUI.bg = Instance.new("Frame", scaleUI.row)
        -- Mobile slider: 18px tall for thumb-grab. This is THE slider mobile
        -- users hit most often (controls their own UI scale), so it gets the
        -- chunky treatment regardless of platform.
        scaleUI.bg.Size = UDim2.new(0, 140, 0, 18)
        scaleUI.bg.Position = UDim2.new(1, -200, 0.5, -9)
        scaleUI.bg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 32, 38)
        Instance.new("UICorner", scaleUI.bg).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        scaleUI.fill = Instance.new("Frame", scaleUI.bg)
        scaleUI.fill.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent1
        scaleUI.fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
        scaleUI.fill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
        Instance.new("UICorner", scaleUI.fill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        scaleUI.knob = Instance.new("Frame", scaleUI.bg)
        scaleUI.knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 20)
        scaleUI.knob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        scaleUI.knob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        scaleUI.knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
        Instance.new("UICorner", scaleUI.knob).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        scaleUI.stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", scaleUI.knob)
        scaleUI.stroke.Color = Theme.Accent1
        scaleUI.stroke.Thickness = 2
        scaleUI.readout = Instance.new("TextLabel", scaleUI.row)
        scaleUI.readout.Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 20)
        scaleUI.readout.Position = UDim2.new(1, -145, 0.5, -10)
        scaleUI.readout.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        scaleUI.readout.Text = string.format("%.1f", Config.MobileGuiScale or 0.4)
        scaleUI.readout.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        scaleUI.readout.TextSize = 11
        scaleUI.readout.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent1
        scaleUI.readout.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
        scaleUI.dragging = false
        scaleUI.min = 0.1
        scaleUI.max = 1.0
        scaleUI.scaleToT = function(s)
            return math.clamp((s - scaleUI.min) / (scaleUI.max - scaleUI.min), 0, 1)
        end
        scaleUI.updateScaleSlider = function(val)
            local c = math.clamp(val, scaleUI.min, scaleUI.max)
            Config.MobileGuiScale = c
            SaveConfig()
            scaleUI.fill.Size = UDim2.new(scaleUI.scaleToT(c), 0, 1, 0)
            scaleUI.knob.Position = UDim2.new(0, scaleUI.scaleToT(c) * scaleUI.bg.AbsoluteSize.X, 0.5, 0)
            scaleUI.readout.Text = string.format("%.2f", c)
            if SharedState.RefreshMobileScale then SharedState.RefreshMobileScale() end
        end
        task.defer(function() scaleUI.updateScaleSlider(Config.MobileGuiScale or 0.4) end)
        scaleUI.onScaleInput = function(pos)
            scaleUI.updateScaleSlider(scaleUI.min + math.clamp((pos.X - scaleUI.bg.AbsolutePosition.X) / scaleUI.bg.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1) * (scaleUI.max - scaleUI.min))
        end
        scaleUI.bg.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
            if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                scaleUI.dragging = true
                scaleUI.onScaleInput(inp.Position)
            end
        end)
        scaleUI.knob.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
            if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                scaleUI.dragging = true
            end
        end)
        UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(inp)
            if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                scaleUI.dragging = false
            end
        end)
        UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
            if scaleUI.dragging and (inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                scaleUI.onScaleInput(inp.Position)
            end
        end)
    end

    local rReset = CreateRow("Reset UI Positions")
    local bReset = Instance.new("TextButton", rReset)
    bReset.Size=UDim2.new(0,80,0,24); bReset.Position=UDim2.new(1,-90,0.5,-12)
    bReset.BackgroundColor3=Theme.Error; bReset.Text="RESET"
    bReset.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; bReset.TextColor3=Theme.TextPrimary; bReset.TextSize=12
    Instance.new("UICorner",bReset).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,4)
    bReset.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Config.Positions = DeepCopy(DefaultConfig.Positions)
        SaveConfig()
        sFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
            DefaultConfig.Positions.Settings.X,
            DefaultConfig.Positions.Settings.OffsetX or 0,
            DefaultConfig.Positions.Settings.Y,
            DefaultConfig.Positions.Settings.OffsetY or 0
        )
        if PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("AutoStealUI") and PlayerGui.AutoStealUI:FindFirstChild("Frame") then
            PlayerGui.AutoStealUI.Frame.Position = UDim2.new(
                DefaultConfig.Positions.AutoSteal.X,
                DefaultConfig.Positions.AutoSteal.OffsetX or 0,
                DefaultConfig.Positions.AutoSteal.Y,
                DefaultConfig.Positions.AutoSteal.OffsetY or 0
            )
        end
        if PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("VanishAdminPanel") and PlayerGui.VanishAdminPanel:FindFirstChild("Frame") then
            PlayerGui.VanishAdminPanel.Frame.Position = UDim2.new(
                DefaultConfig.Positions.AdminPanel.X,
                DefaultConfig.Positions.AdminPanel.OffsetX or 0,
                DefaultConfig.Positions.AdminPanel.Y,
                DefaultConfig.Positions.AdminPanel.OffsetY or 0
            )
        end
        if PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("VanishInvisPanel") and PlayerGui.VanishInvisPanel:FindFirstChild("Frame") then
            PlayerGui.VanishInvisPanel.Frame.Position = UDim2.new(
                DefaultConfig.Positions.InvisPanel.X,
                DefaultConfig.Positions.InvisPanel.OffsetX or 0,
                DefaultConfig.Positions.InvisPanel.Y,
                DefaultConfig.Positions.InvisPanel.OffsetY or 0
            )
        end
        if PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("AutoStealTargetControls") and PlayerGui.AutoStealTargetControls:FindFirstChild("TargetControlsFrame") then
            PlayerGui.AutoStealTargetControls.TargetControlsFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
                DefaultConfig.Positions.TargetControls.X,
                DefaultConfig.Positions.TargetControls.OffsetX or 0,
                DefaultConfig.Positions.TargetControls.Y,
                DefaultConfig.Positions.TargetControls.OffsetY or 0
            )
        end
    end)

    end

    -- UI Accent Color section (in UI tab) ----------------------------------
    do
    task.wait()
    _G.SetVanishUIPage("Settings_UI")
    CreateSectionHeader("ACCENT COLOR")

    -- Every accent shade ever used (catches elements built before VANISH.ACCENT was set)
    local _knownAccents = {
        Color3.fromRGB(60,  130, 255),  -- original Theme.Accent1 used by external UIs
        Color3.fromRGB(0,   170, 255),  -- default VANISH.ACCENT
        VANISH.ACCENT,                  -- current (may differ if saved config was loaded)
    }
    -- Dark variants: accent lerped 18% toward black (ACCENT2, used in separator lines / gradients)
    local _knownDarkAccents = {}
    -- Light variants: accent lerped ~47% toward white (used in glow gradient mid-keypoints)
    local _knownLightAccents = {
        Color3.fromRGB(120, 220, 255),  -- lighter mid-point used in glow gradients
    }
    local _DARK_F  = 0.18
    local _LIGHT_F = 0.47
    local function _refreshAccentVariants()
        _knownDarkAccents  = {}
        _knownLightAccents = { Color3.fromRGB(120, 220, 255) }
        for _, ac in ipairs(_knownAccents) do
            table.insert(_knownDarkAccents,  ac:Lerp(Color3.new(0,0,0), _DARK_F))
            table.insert(_knownLightAccents, ac:Lerp(Color3.new(1,1,1), _LIGHT_F))
        end
    end
    _refreshAccentVariants()

    -- Recolour every accent-tinted element across all Vanish UIs
    local function applyAccentColor(newColor, sliderRoot)
        local oldColor = VANISH.ACCENT
        table.insert(_knownAccents, oldColor)   -- remember this shade too
        _refreshAccentVariants()                -- keep dark/light tables in sync
        VANISH.ACCENT  = newColor
        VANISH.ACCENT2 = newColor:Lerp(Color3.new(0, 0, 0), _DARK_F)
        VANISH.GLOW    = newColor
        Theme.Accent1  = newColor
        Theme.Accent2  = VANISH.ACCENT2
        Config.UIAccentColor = { R = newColor.R, G = newColor.G, B = newColor.B }
        SaveConfig()

        local newDark  = VANISH.ACCENT2                             -- darker shade for ACCENT2 slots
        local newLight = newColor:Lerp(Color3.new(1,1,1), _LIGHT_F) -- lighter shade for glow mid-points

        -- Fuzzy match against ALL known old accent shades
        local TOL = 0.012
        local function isOld(c)
            for _, oc in ipairs(_knownAccents) do
                if math.abs(c.R - oc.R) < TOL
                and math.abs(c.G - oc.G) < TOL
                and math.abs(c.B - oc.B) < TOL then
                    return true
                end
            end
            return false
        end
        local function isOldDark(c)
            for _, dc in ipairs(_knownDarkAccents) do
                if math.abs(c.R - dc.R) < TOL
                and math.abs(c.G - dc.G) < TOL
                and math.abs(c.B - dc.B) < TOL then
                    return true
                end
            end
            return false
        end
        local function isOldLight(c)
            local TOL2 = 0.06   -- wider tolerance: light variants have more float spread
            for _, lc in ipairs(_knownLightAccents) do
                if math.abs(c.R - lc.R) < TOL2
                and math.abs(c.G - lc.G) < TOL2
                and math.abs(c.B - lc.B) < TOL2 then
                    return true
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        -- All Vanish-owned GUI roots
        local roots = { sFrame }
        for _, n in ipairs({
            "VanishAdminPanel", "AutoStealUI", "VanishInvisPanel",
            "AutoStealTargetControls", "VanishSettingsToggle", "VanishMobileControls",
            "VanishStatusHUD"
        }) do
            local g = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild(n)
            if g then table.insert(roots, g) end
        end

        for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
            for _, obj in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do repeat
                if sliderRoot and obj:IsDescendantOf(sliderRoot) then break end
                if obj:IsA("UIStroke") then
                    if isOld(obj.Color) then
                        obj.Color = newColor
                    elseif isOldDark(obj.Color) then
                        obj.Color = newDark
                    end
                elseif obj:IsA("Frame") or obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("TextLabel") then
                    if isOld(obj.BackgroundColor3) then
                        obj.BackgroundColor3 = newColor
                    elseif isOldDark(obj.BackgroundColor3) then
                        obj.BackgroundColor3 = newDark
                    end
                    if obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") then
                        if isOld(obj.TextColor3) then
                            obj.TextColor3 = newColor
                        elseif isOldDark(obj.TextColor3) then
                            obj.TextColor3 = newDark
                        end
                    end
                elseif obj:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
                    if isOld(obj.ScrollBarImageColor3) then
                        obj.ScrollBarImageColor3 = newColor
                    elseif isOldDark(obj.ScrollBarImageColor3) then
                        obj.ScrollBarImageColor3 = newDark
                    end
                elseif obj:IsA("UIGradient") then
                    local kps, changed = obj.Color.Keypoints, false
                    local newKps = {}
                    for _, kp in ipairs(kps) do
                        if isOld(kp.Value) then
                            table.insert(newKps, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(kp.Time, newColor))
                            changed = true
                        elseif isOldDark(kp.Value) then
                            table.insert(newKps, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(kp.Time, newDark))
                            changed = true
                        elseif isOldLight(kp.Value) then
                            table.insert(newKps, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(kp.Time, newLight))
                            changed = true
                        else
                            table.insert(newKps, kp)
                        end
                    end
                    if changed then obj.Color = ColorSequence.new(newKps) end
                end
            until true end
        end

        -- Re-apply tab highlight so selected tab uses the new accent immediately
        if _G.VanishRefreshCurrentTab then _G.VanishRefreshCurrentTab() end
        -- Recolour all live player ESP name labels immediately
        if _G.VanishRefreshPlayerESP then _G.VanishRefreshPlayerESP() end
    end

    -- Rainbow hue slider row
    local rHue = CreateRow("Accent Color")

    -- Slider bar
    local hueBar = Instance.new("Frame", rHue)
    hueBar.Name = "VanishHueBar"
    hueBar.Size = UDim2.new(0, s(200), 0, s(12))
    hueBar.Position = UDim2.new(1, -s(230), 0.5, -s(6))
    hueBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    hueBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    Instance.new("UICorner", hueBar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

    local hueGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient", hueBar)
    hueGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0/6, Color3.fromHSV(0/6, 1, 1)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1/6, Color3.fromHSV(1/6, 1, 1)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(2/6, Color3.fromHSV(2/6, 1, 1)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(3/6, Color3.fromHSV(3/6, 1, 1)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(4/6, Color3.fromHSV(4/6, 1, 1)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(5/6, Color3.fromHSV(5/6, 1, 1)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,   Color3.fromHSV(0,   1, 1)),
    })

    -- Preview swatch showing current colour
    local huePreview = Instance.new("Frame", rHue)
    huePreview.Name = "VanishHuePreview"
    huePreview.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(s(22), s(22))
    huePreview.Position = UDim2.new(1, -s(22), 0.5, -s(11))
    huePreview.BackgroundColor3 = VANISH.ACCENT
    huePreview.BorderSizePixel = 0
    Instance.new("UICorner", huePreview).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    local previewStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", huePreview)
    previewStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    previewStroke.Thickness = 1.5
    previewStroke.Transparency = 0.5
    previewStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border

    -- Draggable knob on the bar
    local hueKnob = Instance.new("Frame", hueBar)
    hueKnob.Name = "VanishHueKnob"
    hueKnob.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(s(16), s(16))
    hueKnob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    hueKnob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    hueKnob.BorderSizePixel = 0
    hueKnob.ZIndex = 4
    Instance.new("UICorner", hueKnob).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    local knobStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", hueKnob)
    knobStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
    knobStroke.Thickness = 1.5
    knobStroke.Transparency = 0.2

    -- Position knob at the saved hue on load
    local function hueFromColor(c)
        local h, _, _ = Color3.toHSV(c)
        return h
    end
    local initHue = hueFromColor(VANISH.ACCENT)
    hueKnob.Position = UDim2.new(initHue, 0, 0.5, 0)

    -- Drag logic
    -- Preview updates every frame; full element scan fires ONCE on release
    local hueDragging = false
    local lastHue = hueFromColor(VANISH.ACCENT)

    local function previewHue(hue)
        hue = math.clamp(hue, 0, 1)
        lastHue = hue
        hueKnob.Position = UDim2.new(hue, 0, 0.5, 0)
        huePreview.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(hue, 1, 1)
    end

    local function commitHue()
        local col = Color3.fromHSV(lastHue, 1, 1)
        huePreview.BackgroundColor3 = col
        applyAccentColor(col, rHue)
    end

    local function hueFromInput(inputX)
        local barX = hueBar.AbsolutePosition.X
        local barW = hueBar.AbsoluteSize.X
        return (inputX - barX) / barW
    end

    hueBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
        if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            hueDragging = true
            previewHue(hueFromInput(inp.Position.X))
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
        if hueDragging and (inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
        or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            previewHue(hueFromInput(inp.Position.X))
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(inp)
        if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            if hueDragging then
                hueDragging = false
                commitHue()   -- apply to all UIs exactly once on release
            end
        end
    end)
    end
    -- ----------------------------------------------------------------------

    -- Bottom-right settings toggle button ---------------------------------
    if not IS_MOBILE then
            do  -- GUI stagger: each ScreenGui starts ~0.45s after the previous
                _G.VanishGuiStaggerSlot = (_G.VanishGuiStaggerSlot or 0) + 1
                task.wait(0.45 * (_G.VanishGuiStaggerSlot - 1))
            end
        local toggleGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        toggleGui.Name = "VanishSettingsToggle"
        toggleGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        toggleGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        toggleGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        toggleGui.DisplayOrder = 998
        toggleGui.Parent = PlayerGui

        local toggleBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        toggleBtn.Name = "SettingsToggleBtn"
        toggleBtn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1)
        toggleBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(46, 46)
        toggleBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -16)
        toggleBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 20, 28)
        toggleBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        toggleBtn.Text = "⚙"
        toggleBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        toggleBtn.TextSize = 22
        toggleBtn.TextColor3 = VANISH.ACCENT
        toggleBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        toggleBtn.ZIndex = 10
        toggleBtn.Parent = toggleGui
        Instance.new("UICorner", toggleBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)

        local tbStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", toggleBtn)
        tbStroke.Color = VANISH.ACCENT
        tbStroke.Thickness = 1.5
        tbStroke.Transparency = 0.35
        tbStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border

        toggleBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(toggleBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {
                BackgroundColor3 = VANISH.SURF2,
                TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
            }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(tbStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {
                Transparency = 0,
            }):Play()
        end)
        toggleBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(toggleBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 20, 28),
                TextColor3 = VANISH.ACCENT,
            }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(tbStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {
                Transparency = 0.35,
            }):Play()
        end)
        toggleBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if settingsGui and settingsGui.Enabled then
                closeVanishUI()
            else
                openVanishUI()
            end
        end)
    end
    -- ----------------------------------------------------------------------

end

-- Was `local function` — promoted to global to free a register slot
-- (main chunk hit the 200-locals cap right here). All references are
-- below in the same file and use bare name, so no call-site change needed.
function updateSettingsCanvasSize()
    local contentHeight = sLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y
    sList.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, math.max(contentHeight + 20, sList.AbsoluteSize.Y))
end

sLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(updateSettingsCanvasSize)
task.defer(updateSettingsCanvasSize)

if IS_MOBILE then
    sList.ScrollBarThickness = 6
    sList.ScrollingEnabled = true
    sList.ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Always
end

if not IS_MOBILE then
    UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
        if gp then return end
        if input.KeyCode == (Enum.KeyCode[NormalizeKeyName(Config.MenuKey, "LeftControl")] or Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) then
            if settingsGui.Enabled then closeVanishUI() else openVanishUI() end
            if SharedState.RefreshMobileScale then
                SharedState.RefreshMobileScale()
            end
        end
        if NormalizeKeyName(Config.KickKey, "") ~= "" and input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode[NormalizeKeyName(Config.KickKey, "")] then
            kickPlayer()
        end
        if NormalizeKeyName(Config.RagdollSelfKey, "") ~= "" and input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode[NormalizeKeyName(Config.RagdollSelfKey, "")] then
            local ragdollOnCooldown = false
            if type(isOnCooldown) == "function" then
                local okCooldown, resultCooldown = pcall(isOnCooldown, "ragdoll")
                if okCooldown then
                    ragdollOnCooldown = resultCooldown and true or false
                end
            end

            if not ragdollOnCooldown then
                local didRun = false
                if type(runAdminCommand) == "function" then
                    local okRun, resultRun = pcall(runAdminCommand, LocalPlayer, "ragdoll")
                    didRun = okRun and resultRun and true or false
                end

                if didRun then
                    if type(activeCooldowns) == "table" then
                        activeCooldowns["ragdoll"] = tick()
                    end
                    if type(setGlobalVisualCooldown) == "function" then
                        pcall(setGlobalVisualCooldown, "ragdoll")
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if NormalizeKeyName(Config.ProximityAPKeybind, "P") and input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode[NormalizeKeyName(Config.ProximityAPKeybind, "P")] then
            ProximityAPActive = not ProximityAPActive
            if SharedState.ProximityAPButton then
                updateProxButton()
            end
            if _G.VanishUpdateProxViz then pcall(_G.VanishUpdateProxViz) end
        end
        if input.KeyCode == (Enum.KeyCode[NormalizeKeyName(Config.ClickToAPKeybind, "L")] or Enum.KeyCode.L) then
            Config.ClickToAP = not Config.ClickToAP
            SaveConfig()
        end
    end)
end

local function parseMinGen(v)
    if type(v) == "number" then
        if v <= 0 then return 0 end
        return v * 1e6
    end
    if type(v) ~= "string" then return 0 end
    local str = v:gsub("%s", ""):lower()
    if str == "" then return 0 end
    local num, suffix = str:match("^([%d%.]+)([kmb]?)$")
    if not num then return 0 end
    num = tonumber(num)
    if not num or num < 0 then return 0 end
    if suffix == "k" then return num * 1e3
    elseif suffix == "b" then return num * 1e9
    end
    return num * 1e6
end

if Config.TpSettings.TpOnLoad
   and not _G._priorityAutoTPDone
   and not _G._autoTPTurboPending
then
    task.spawn(function()
        local minGen = parseMinGen(Config.TpSettings.MinGenForTp)
        local minLabel = tostring(Config.TpSettings.MinGenForTp or "")

        local function isEligibleAutoLoadTarget(pet)
            if not pet then return false end
            if not pet.name or not pet.uid then return false end
            if pet.owner == LocalPlayer.Name then return false end
            if minGen > 0 and (tonumber(pet.genValue) or 0) < minGen then
                return false
            end
            return true
        end

        local function getPriorityTargetFromCache_MinGen()
            local cache = SharedState and SharedState.AllAnimalsCache
            if not cache or #cache == 0 then
                return nil
            end

            for i = 1, #PRIORITY_LIST do
                local wanted = PRIORITY_LIST[i]:lower()

                for _, pet in ipairs(cache) do
                    if isEligibleAutoLoadTarget(pet)
                        and pet.name:lower() == wanted then
                        return pet
                    end
                end
            end

            return nil
        end

        local function getFallbackTargetFromCache_MinGen()
            local cache = SharedState and SharedState.AllAnimalsCache
            if not cache or #cache == 0 then
                return nil
            end

            for _, pet in ipairs(cache) do
                if isEligibleAutoLoadTarget(pet) then
                    return pet
                end
            end

            return nil
        end

        local function chooseAutoLoadTarget()
            return getPriorityTargetFromCache_MinGen() or getFallbackTargetFromCache_MinGen()
        end

        local chosen = nil
        local sawAnyCache = false
        local deadline = os.clock() + 6.0

        repeat
            local cache = SharedState and SharedState.AllAnimalsCache
            if cache and #cache > 0 then
                sawAnyCache = true
            end

            chosen = chooseAutoLoadTarget()
            if chosen then
                break
            end

            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
        until os.clock() >= deadline

        if not chosen then
            if minGen > 0 then
            elseif sawAnyCache then
            else
            end
            return
        end

        SharedState.SelectedPetData = {
            petName = chosen.name,
            name = chosen.name,
            mpsText = chosen.genText,
            mpsValue = chosen.genValue,
            genText = chosen.genText,
            genValue = chosen.genValue,
            owner = chosen.owner,
            plot = chosen.plot,
            slot = chosen.slot,
            uid = chosen.uid,
            mutation = chosen.mutation,
            animalData = chosen
        }

        local _joinDelay = tonumber(Config.TpSettings.TpJoinDelay) or 0.3
        if _joinDelay > 0 then task.wait(_joinDelay) end

        if _G.tpToBestBrainrot then
            pcall(_G.tpToBestBrainrot)
        else
            waitSecondsHeartbeat(0.10)
            runAutoSnipe()
        end
    end)
end

LocalPlayer:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
    local isStealing = LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing")
    local wasStealing = not isStealing

    if isStealing then
        if Config.AutoUnlockOnSteal then
            autoUnlockCurrentFloor()
        end
    end
end)

-- ============================================================
-- AUTO BACK ON HIT — fires when the steal gets CANCELED.
-- Trigger: Stealing attribute transitions true → false (got hit mid-
-- steal, attribute fell off). Behavior: tween-TP back to the brainrot
-- every ~0.5s for up to 8 seconds, OR until Stealing comes back true
-- (the steal restarted on its own via the auto-steal scanner). After
-- 8s with no successful restart, the loop gives up.
-- ============================================================
do
    local stealAnimalData = nil

    local function _refreshAnimalData()
        local idx = LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("StealingIndex")
        if not idx then stealAnimalData = nil; return end
        local cache = SharedState and SharedState.AllAnimalsCache
        if not cache then stealAnimalData = nil; return end
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp  = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local best, bestDist = nil, math.huge
        for _, entry in ipairs(cache) do
            if entry.Index == idx then
                if hrp then
                    local ok, part = pcall(findAdorneeGlobal, entry)
                    if ok and part then
                        local d = (hrp.Position - part.Position).Magnitude
                        if d < bestDist then bestDist = d; best = entry end
                    else
                        best = best or entry
                    end
                else
                    best = entry; break
                end
            end
        end
        stealAnimalData = best
    end

    -- _lastStealData is preserved across the Stealing=false transition
    -- so the back-loop knows where to return after a hit cancels the steal.
    local _lastStealData        = nil
    local _backToBrainrotActive = false

    -- Returns true when LocalPlayer is currently STANDING IN the enemy
    -- plot (within its XZ bounding box, ±2 studs padding). Used so the
    -- back-loop only fires when the hit just knocked us inside the base,
    -- not when we got launched all the way home.
    local function _isInPlot(plotName)
        if not plotName then return false end
        local plotsFolder = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        local plot = plotsFolder and plotsFolder:FindFirstChild(plotName)
        if not plot or not plot:IsA("Model") then return false end
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp  = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return false end
        local ok, cf, sz = pcall(function() return plot:GetBoundingBox() end)
        if not ok or not cf then return false end
        local rel  = cf:PointToObjectSpace(hrp.Position)
        local half = sz * 0.5
        return math.abs(rel.X) <= half.X + 2
           and math.abs(rel.Z) <= half.Z + 2
    end

    -- Equip the user-selected flying tool (Flying Carpet, Cupid's Wings,
    -- Santa's Sleigh, Witch's Broom — whichever is set in Config.TpSettings.Tool).
    local function _equipFlyingTool()
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hum  = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum then return end
        local toolName = (Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.Tool) or "Flying Carpet"
        local tool = (LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack") and LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild(toolName))
                   or char:FindFirstChild(toolName)
        if not tool then return end
        if tool.Parent ~= char then
            pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(tool) end)
        end
    end

    local function _doTweenBackTo(brainrotPart)
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp  = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp or not brainrotPart or not brainrotPart.Parent then return end
        local dest = brainrotPart.Position
        local dist = (dest - hrp.Position).Magnitude
        if dist < 1 then return end
        local duration = math.clamp(dist / 110, 0.15, 0.7)
        pcall(function()
            TweenService:Create(hrp,
                TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                {CFrame = CFrame.new(dest)}
            ):Play()
        end)
    end

    local function _startAutoTpBackLoop()
        if _backToBrainrotActive then return end
        if not Config.AutoBack then return end
        if not _lastStealData then return end
        if not _isInPlot(_lastStealData.plot) then return end
        _backToBrainrotActive = true
        task.spawn(function()
            local deadline = tick() + 8

            _equipFlyingTool()

            local ok, brainrotPart = pcall(findAdorneeGlobal, _lastStealData)
            local floatState  -- { hum, prevPS, prevMassless, plat }

            if ok and brainrotPart and brainrotPart:IsA("BasePart") then
                local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                local hrp  = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local hum  = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hrp and hum then
                    local itemPos = brainrotPart.Position
                    -- Upper-floor brainrot (above the player's current Y):
                    -- aim BELOW it so the player ends up underneath and floats
                    -- up into it. Third-floor brainrots (high Y) get a 1.5x
                    -- larger drop (4.5 vs 3 studs) so we settle properly under
                    -- them instead of landing inside/above. Ground/flat
                    -- brainrots aim directly at the part.
                    local isUpper      = (itemPos.Y - hrp.Position.Y) > 4
                    local isThirdFloor = itemPos.Y > 18
                    local belowOffset  = isThirdFloor and (3 * 1.5) or 3
                    local destPos = isUpper
                        and Vector3.new(itemPos.X, itemPos.Y - belowOffset, itemPos.Z)
                        or  itemPos

                    -- ── Float setup (only when we'll be hanging below
                    --    the brainrot — otherwise gravity is fine). ──
                    if isUpper then
                        local prevPS = hum.PlatformStand
                        local prevMassless = {}
                        for _, p in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                            if p:IsA("BasePart") then
                                prevMassless[p] = p.Massless
                                pcall(function() p.Massless = true end)
                            end
                        end
                        pcall(function() hum.PlatformStand = true end)

                        local plat = Instance.new("Part")
                        plat.Name         = "VanishAutoBackPlatform"
                        plat.Size         = Vector3.new(8, 1, 8)
                        plat.Anchored     = true
                        plat.CanCollide   = true
                        plat.CanTouch     = false
                        plat.CanQuery     = false
                        plat.Transparency = 1
                        plat.CastShadow   = false
                        plat.Locked       = true
                        plat.Massless     = true
                        plat.CFrame       = CFrame.new(destPos - Vector3.new(0, 3.35, 0))
                        plat.Parent       = Workspace

                        floatState = {
                            hum = hum, prevPS = prevPS,
                            prevMassless = prevMassless, plat = plat,
                        }
                    end

                    -- ── Single tween to destination ──
                    local dist = (destPos - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                    if dist >= 1 then
                        local duration = math.clamp(dist / 110, 0.15, 0.7)
                        pcall(function()
                            TweenService:Create(hrp,
                                TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                                {CFrame = CFrame.new(destPos)}
                            ):Play()
                        end)
                    end

                    -- ── Verify arrival window (>= the slower tween's max) ──
                    local verifyDeadline = tick() + 0.8
                    while tick() < verifyDeadline do
                        if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then break end
                        local _hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if _hrp and brainrotPart.Parent
                           and (_hrp.Position - destPos).Magnitude < 5 then break end
                        task.wait(0.05)
                    end

                    -- ── SERVER-REPLICATION FLUSH ──────────────────────────
                    -- The CFrame tween moves us client-side, but after a hit
                    -- the knockback can leave the server owning our HRP / stuck
                    -- at the hit position. Zero the knockback velocity, clear
                    -- the ragdoll state (reclaims client control), and hard-set
                    -- the CFrame for several frames so the real position
                    -- actually replicates — otherwise the steal fires while the
                    -- server still thinks we're back where we got hit.
                    for _ = 1, 8 do
                        if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then break end
                        local _hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if not _hrp or not _hrp.Parent then break end
                        pcall(function() _hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity  = Vector3.zero end)
                        pcall(function() _hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero end)
                        pcall(function() _hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(destPos) end)
                        RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                    end

                end
            end

            -- ── Wait for Stealing to come back true (or 8s timeout).
            -- The normal auto-steal scanner handles the actual prompt
            -- firing now that the server has our true position.
            while tick() < deadline do
                if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then break end
                if not Config.AutoBack then break end
                task.wait(0.1)
            end

            -- ── Cleanup float state on exit (whether success or timeout) ──
            if floatState then
                if floatState.hum and floatState.hum.Parent then
                    pcall(function() floatState.hum.PlatformStand = floatState.prevPS or false end)
                end
                for p, was in pairs(floatState.prevMassless) do
                    if p and p.Parent then
                        pcall(function() p.Massless = was end)
                    end
                end
                if floatState.plat and floatState.plat.Parent then
                    pcall(function() floatState.plat:Destroy() end)
                end
            end

            _backToBrainrotActive = false
        end)
    end

    LocalPlayer:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
        if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then
            _refreshAnimalData()
            _lastStealData = stealAnimalData  -- snapshot for the back-loop
        else
            stealAnimalData = nil
            -- Auto Back to Brainrot DISABLED for now. To re-enable, restore
            -- the trigger below (0.3s delay → re-check → _startAutoTpBackLoop).
            --[[
            if Config.AutoBack and _lastStealData then
                task.delay(0.3, function()
                    if Config.AutoBack and _lastStealData
                       and not LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") then
                        _startAutoTpBackLoop()
                    end
                end)
            end
            ]]
        end
    end)
    LocalPlayer:GetAttributeChangedSignal("StealingIndex"):Connect(function()
        if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then
            _refreshAnimalData()
            _lastStealData = stealAnimalData
        end
    end)

    LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        stealAnimalData = nil
    end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    local stealSpeedEnabled = false
    local STEAL_SPEED = Config.StealSpeed or 25.5
    local stealConn = nil

    local function _stealTeardown()
        -- Clean up any stale LV/attachments left from old impl
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp then
            for _, c in ipairs(hrp:GetChildren()) do
                if c.Name == "VanishStealLV" or c.Name == "VanishStealAttach" then
                    pcall(function() c:Destroy() end)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function doDisable()
        stealSpeedEnabled = false
        if stealConn then stealConn:Disconnect(); stealConn = nil end
        _stealTeardown()
    end

    local function doEnable()
        stealSpeedEnabled = true
        if stealConn then stealConn:Disconnect(); stealConn = nil end
        _stealTeardown()
        stealConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character; if not char then return end
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not hum or not hrp then return end

            local md = hum.MoveDirection
            local curY = hrp.Velocity.Y
            if md.Magnitude > 0 then
                hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(md.X * STEAL_SPEED, curY, md.Z * STEAL_SPEED)
            end
        end))
    end

    SharedState.GetStealSpeed = function()
        return STEAL_SPEED
    end

    SharedState.SetStealSpeed = function(v, silent)
        local n = tonumber(v)
        if not n then return STEAL_SPEED end
        STEAL_SPEED = math.clamp(n, 5, 32)
        Config.StealSpeed = STEAL_SPEED
        if not silent then
            SaveConfig()
        end
        if SharedState._ssUpdateBtn then
            SharedState._ssUpdateBtn()
        end
        return STEAL_SPEED
    end

    SharedState.DisableStealSpeed = function()
        doDisable()
        if SharedState._ssUpdateBtn then SharedState._ssUpdateBtn() end
    end

    SharedState._ssUpdateBtn = function() end

    SharedState.StealSpeedToggleFunc = function()
        if stealSpeedEnabled then doDisable() else doEnable() end
        if SharedState._ssUpdateBtn then SharedState._ssUpdateBtn() end
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        local lastHadSteal = nil
        while true do repeat
            task.wait(0.3)
            if not Config.AutoStealSpeed then lastHadSteal = nil; break end
            local hasSteal = (LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true)
            if lastHadSteal == hasSteal then break end
            lastHadSteal = hasSteal
            if hasSteal and not stealSpeedEnabled then
                doEnable(); if SharedState._ssUpdateBtn then SharedState._ssUpdateBtn() end
            elseif not hasSteal and stealSpeedEnabled then
                doDisable(); if SharedState._ssUpdateBtn then SharedState._ssUpdateBtn() end
            end
        until true end
    end)
end)

task.spawn(function()
    -- Defer 2s — BrainrotESP iterates Workspace + CoreGui descendants
    -- for cleanup THEN builds billboards for every brainrot in the cache.
    -- Outer LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE not possible (inner Heartbeat:Connect wraps
    -- can't be nested). Inner wraps cover the hot paths.
    task.wait(2)
    local brainrotESPEnabled = Config.BrainrotESP

    local function cleanupLegacyBrainrotESP()
        for _, inst in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
            if inst.Name == "VanishBrainrotESP" then
                pcall(function() inst:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
        for _, inst in ipairs(game:GetService("CoreGui"):GetChildren()) do
            if inst:IsA("Highlight") and inst.Name == "VanishBrainrotESP_Highlight" then
                pcall(function() inst:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
        for _, inst in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if inst:IsA("BillboardGui") and string.sub(inst.Name, 1, 12) == "BrainrotESP_" then
                pcall(function() inst:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
    end

    cleanupLegacyBrainrotESP()

    local brainrotESPFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
    brainrotESPFolder.Name = "VanishBrainrotESP"
    brainrotESPFolder.Parent = Workspace
    local brainrotBillboards = {}
    local hiddenOverheads = {}
    local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
    local UID_ATTR = "_BR_UID"
    local uidCounter = 0
    local myPlot = nil
    local myPlotBounds = nil
    local myDisplayName = LocalPlayer.DisplayName
    local SCAN_INTERVAL = 0.35
    local PLOT_PADDING = 6
    local MIN_M_PER_SEC = 35

    local _espAnimalsData, _espAnimalsShared, _espNumberUtils, _espAnimalController
    pcall(function()
        local Datas = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Datas", 5)
        _espAnimalsData = Datas and require(Datas:WaitForChild("Animals", 5))
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared", 5)
        _espAnimalsShared = Shared and require(Shared:WaitForChild("Animals", 5))
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local Utils = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Utils", 5)
        _espNumberUtils = Utils and require(Utils:WaitForChild("NumberUtils", 5))
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local Controllers = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Controllers", 5)
        _espAnimalController = Controllers and require(Controllers:WaitForChild("AnimalController", 5))
    end)

    local RARITY_COLORS = {
        ["Common"]        = Color3.fromRGB(190, 195, 210),

    local MUT_COLORS = {
        Cursed = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50),
        Gold = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0),
        Diamond = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255),
        YinYang = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220),

    local function getStableUid(part)
        if not part then return nil end
        local existing = part:GetAttribute(UID_ATTR)
        if existing then
            return tostring(existing)
        end
        uidCounter = uidCounter + 1
        local newId = "br_" .. tostring(uidCounter)
        part:SetAttribute(UID_ATTR, newId)
        return newId
    end

    local function parseGenToM(genText)
        genText = tostring(genText or "")
        if genText == "" then return nil end
        genText = genText:gsub("%s+", "")

        local num, suf = genText:match("%$?([%d%.]+)([KkMmBb]?)/s")
        if not num then return nil end

        local n = tonumber(num)
        if not n then return nil end

        suf = (suf or ""):upper()

        if suf == "B" then
            return n * 1000, true
        elseif suf == "M" then
            return n, false
        elseif suf == "K" then
            return n / 1000, false
        else
            return n / 1000000, false
        end
    end

    local function formatGenText(valueM)
        local m = valueM or 0
        if m >= 1000 then
            local bVal = m / 1000
            if math.floor(bVal) == bVal then
                return string.format("$%dB/s", bVal)
            else
                return string.format("$%.1fB/s", bVal)
            end
        elseif m >= 1 then
            if math.floor(m) == m then
                return string.format("$%dM/s", m)
            else
                return string.format("$%.1fM/s", m)
            end
        elseif m >= 0.001 then
            local kVal = m * 1000
            if math.floor(kVal) == kVal then
                return string.format("$%dK/s", kVal)
            else
                return string.format("$%.1fK/s", kVal)
            end
        else
            local normalVal = math.floor(m * 1000000 + 0.5)
            return string.format("$%d/s", normalVal)
        end
    end

    local function findMyPlot()
        local plotsFolder = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not plotsFolder then return nil end

        for _, plot in ipairs(plotsFolder:GetChildren()) do
            local plotSign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
            if plotSign then
                local surfaceGui = plotSign:FindFirstChild("SurfaceGui")
                if surfaceGui then
                    local frame = surfaceGui:FindFirstChild("Frame")
                    if frame then
                        local textLabel = frame:FindFirstChild("TextLabel")
                        if textLabel and textLabel:IsA("TextLabel") then
                            local txt = tostring(textLabel.Text or "")
                            if txt:find(myDisplayName, 1, true) and txt:find("'s Base", 1, true) then
                                return plot
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        return nil
    end

    local function computePlotBounds(plot)
        if not plot then return nil end

        local minX, minY, minZ = math.huge, math.huge, math.huge
        local maxX, maxY, maxZ = -math.huge, -math.huge, -math.huge
        local found = false

        for _, obj in ipairs(plot:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                found = true
                local pos = obj.Position
                local half = obj.Size / 2

                local x1, x2 = pos.X - half.X, pos.X + half.X
                local y1, y2 = pos.Y - half.Y, pos.Y + half.Y
                local z1, z2 = pos.Z - half.Z, pos.Z + half.Z

                if x1 < minX then minX = x1 end
                if y1 < minY then minY = y1 end
                if z1 < minZ then minZ = z1 end

                if x2 > maxX then maxX = x2 end
                if y2 > maxY then maxY = y2 end
                if z2 > maxZ then maxZ = z2 end
            end
        end

        if not found then return nil end

        return {
            minX = minX, maxX = maxX,
            minY = minY, maxY = maxY,
            minZ = minZ, maxZ = maxZ
        }
    end

    local function pointInsideBounds(pos, bounds, padding)
        if not bounds then return false end
        padding = padding or 0

        return pos.X >= (bounds.minX - padding) and pos.X <= (bounds.maxX + padding)
            and pos.Y >= (bounds.minY - padding) and pos.Y <= (bounds.maxY + padding)
            and pos.Z >= (bounds.minZ - padding) and pos.Z <= (bounds.maxZ + padding)
    end

    local function refreshMyPlot()
        myPlot = findMyPlot()
        myPlotBounds = computePlotBounds(myPlot)
    end

    local function isInsideMyPlotPosition(pos)
        if not myPlotBounds then return false end
        return pointInsideBounds(pos, myPlotBounds, PLOT_PADDING)
    end

    local function isDescendantOfMyPlot(obj)
        return (myPlot and obj and obj:IsDescendantOf(myPlot)) or false
    end

    local function inMapRect(pos)
        if pos.X < -535.882996 or pos.X > -285.808075 then return false end
        if pos.Z < -140.226715 or pos.Z > 258.971161 then return false end
        return true
    end

    local function readOverheadLabels(overheadGui)
        local displayName, generation

        for _, d in ipairs(overheadGui:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("TextLabel") then
                if d.Name == "DisplayName" then
                    local t = d.Text
                    if t and t ~= "" then
                        displayName = t
                    end
                elseif d.Name == "Generation" then
                    local t = d.Text
                    if t and t ~= "" then
                        generation = t
                    end
                end

                if displayName and generation then
                    break
                end
            end
        end

        return displayName, generation
    end

    local function getMutationColor(mut)
        return (mut and mut ~= "None" and mut ~= "N/A" and MUT_COLORS[mut]) or Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 150)
    end

    local function _addGlowStroke(label, color, thickness, transparency)
        local s = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        s.Name = "Glow"
        s.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Contextual
        s.Color = color
        s.Thickness = thickness or 1.4
        s.Transparency = transparency or 0.25
        s.Parent = label
        return s
    end

    local function _getESPAccentColor(data)
        local mut = data and data.mutation
        if mut and mut ~= "None" and mut ~= "" then
            local MC = {
                Gold        = Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 0),
                Diamond     = Color3.fromRGB(100, 220, 255),
                Cyber       = Color3.fromRGB(50, 180, 255),
                Rainbow     = Color3.fromRGB(200, 100, 255),
                Cursed      = Color3.fromRGB(220, 40, 40),
                Candy       = Color3.fromRGB(255, 105, 180),
                Lava        = Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 30),
                Radioactive = Color3.fromRGB(100, 255, 0),
                YinYang     = Color3.fromRGB(210, 210, 210),
                Divine      = Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 140),
                Galaxy      = Color3.fromRGB(160, 80, 255),
                Bloodrot    = Color3.fromRGB(160, 0, 0),
            }
            if MC[mut] then return MC[mut] end
        end
        local gv = tonumber(data and data.genValue) or 0
        if gv >= 100e6 then return Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 40) end
        if gv >= 10e6  then return Color3.fromRGB(0, 180, 255)  end
        if gv >= 1e6   then return Color3.fromRGB(0, 220, 120)  end
        return Color3.fromRGB(90, 130, 110)
    end

    local function _buildBrainrotCard(uid, isConveyor)
        local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        bb.Name = "BrainrotESP_" .. tostring(uid)
        bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 210, 0, 46)
        bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 7, 0)
        bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
        bb.LightInfluence = 0
        bb.MaxDistance = isConveyor and 100000 or 2500
        bb.ResetOnSpawn = false

        local card = Instance.new("Frame", bb)
        card.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 10, 14)
        card.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
        card.BorderSizePixel = 0
        Instance.new("UICorner", card).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)

        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", card)
        stroke.Name = "CardStroke"
        stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 220, 120)
        stroke.Thickness = 2.0
        stroke.Transparency = 0.2
        stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border

        -- (Accent bar removed per request — the thin vertical "dash" line on
        --  the left of the ESP card. The recolor code below is nil-guarded.)

        -- Row 1: Name (left 55%) | Highest M/S (starts at 57%, left-aligned)
        local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
        nameLabel.Name = "Name"
        nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.55, -14, 0, 17)
        nameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 5)
        nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        nameLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        nameLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
        nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        nameLabel.TextSize = 12
        nameLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        nameLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        nameLabel.ClipsDescendants = true
        nameLabel.Text = ""

        local bestTag = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
        bestTag.Name = "BestTag"
        bestTag.Size = UDim2.new(0.38, -8, 0, 17)
        bestTag.Position = UDim2.new(0.62, 0, 0, 5)
        bestTag.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        bestTag.Text = "Highest M/S"
        bestTag.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        bestTag.TextSize = 10
        bestTag.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 60)
        bestTag.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
        bestTag.Visible = false

        -- Row 2: M/S (left) — distance moves here when no extras
        local genLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
        genLabel.Name = "Gen"
        genLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.58, -14, 0, 15)
        genLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 24)
        genLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        genLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 220, 130)
        genLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
        genLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        genLabel.TextSize = 11
        genLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        genLabel.Text = ""

        -- Distance label — repositioned dynamically
        local distLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
        distLabel.Name = "Dist"
        distLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.4, -8, 0, 15)
        distLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0, 24)
        distLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        distLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(155, 155, 180)
        distLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.6
        distLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        distLabel.TextSize = 10
        distLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
        distLabel.Text = ""

        -- Mutation label — shown/hidden + repositioned dynamically
        local mutLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
        mutLabel.Name = "Mutation"
        mutLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 12)
        mutLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 42)
        mutLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        mutLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0)
        mutLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
        mutLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        mutLabel.TextSize = 10
        mutLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        mutLabel.Text = ""
        mutLabel.Visible = false

        -- Traits label — shown/hidden + repositioned dynamically
        local traitsLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
        traitsLabel.Name = "Traits"
        traitsLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.52, -14, 0, 12)
        traitsLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 42)

        traitsLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 175, 60)
        traitsLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.6
        traitsLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
