            local liveWantedName = liveKind == "kawaifu" and "UserTag_KAWAIFU" or liveKind == "atlas" and "UserTag_ATLAS" or "UserTag_MOBY"
            local liveOffset = computeTagOffset(liveRow)

            if liveExisting and liveExisting.Name == liveWantedName then
                liveExisting.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(liveOffset, 8)
            else
                if liveExisting then
                    liveExisting:Destroy()
                end
                createUserTag(liveRow, liveKind, liveOffset)
            end
        end)
    end

    local function createActionButton(parent, info, order, targetPlr)
        local btn = rpMake("TextButton", {
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(RP_COLORS.ButtonSize, RP_COLORS.ButtonSize),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            AutoButtonColor = false,
            Text = "",
            LayoutOrder = order,
            Parent = parent
        })

        local box = rpMake("Frame", {
            Name = "Box",
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(RP_COLORS.BoxSize, RP_COLORS.BoxSize),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Parent = btn
        })
        rpCorner(box, 6)

        local fill = rpMake("Frame", {
            Name = "Fill",
            BackgroundColor3 = RP_COLORS.ButtonBg,
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.08,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
            Parent = box
        })
        rpCorner(fill, 6)

        local stroke = rpStroke(box, RP_COLORS.ButtonStroke, 1.1, 1)
        stroke.Name = "Stroke"

        local iconImg = rpMake("TextLabel", {
            Name = "Icon",
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(RP_COLORS.BoxSize - 8, RP_COLORS.BoxSize - 8),
            Text = info.label or "",
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
            TextSize = 16,
            TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
            TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
            TextScaled = false,
            Parent = btn
        })

        btn:SetAttribute("Locked", false)
        btn:SetAttribute("ActionName", info.action)
        btn:SetAttribute("TargetPlayerName", targetPlr and targetPlr.Name or "")
        rpSetButtonVisual(btn, "normal")

        btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            if btn:GetAttribute("Locked") then return end
            rpSetButtonVisual(btn, "hover")
        end)

        btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            if btn:GetAttribute("Locked") then return end
            rpSetButtonVisual(btn, "normal")
        end)

        table.insert(actionButtonsRef[info.action], btn)

        local locked = rpIsOnCooldown(info.action)
        local blocked = rpIsAdminPanelBlockedForPlayer(targetPlr)
        local disabled = locked or blocked
        btn.Active = not disabled
        btn:SetAttribute("Locked", disabled)
        if disabled then
            rpSetButtonVisual(btn, "locked")
        end

        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if rpIsAdminPanelBlockedForPlayer(targetPlr) then
                rpRefreshActionButtonsForPlayer(targetPlr)
                return
            end

            if rpIsOnCooldown(info.action) then
                return
            end

            local targetName = (targetPlr and targetPlr.Name) or "target"

            rpStartCooldown(info.action)
            local ok = rpRunAdminCommand(targetPlr, info.action)

            if ok == false then
            else
            end
        end)

        return btn
    end

    local function createPlayerRow(plr)
        if not plr or plr == LocalPlayer then return end
        if rows[plr.UserId] and rows[plr.UserId].Parent then return end

        local count = 0
        for _, child in ipairs(rpui.listHolder:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Frame") and child.Name:match("^Row_") then
                count = count + 1
            end
        end
        local isAlt = (count % 2 == 0)
        local rowColor = isAlt and RP_COLORS.RowA or RP_COLORS.RowB

        local row = rpMake("Frame", {
            Name = ("Row_%d"):format(plr.UserId),
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 22, 28),
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.4,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, RP_COLORS.RowHeight),
            ZIndex = 5,
            Parent = rpui.listHolder
        })
        rowCreateCounter = rowCreateCounter + 1
        row:SetAttribute("CreateIndex", rowCreateCounter)
        row:SetAttribute("PlayerName", plr.Name)
        rpCorner(row, RP_COLORS.RowCorner)
        row.ClipsDescendants = true
        rows[plr.UserId] = row

        row.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(row, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 32, 40), BackgroundTransparency = 0.2}):Play()
        end)
        row.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(row, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 22, 28), BackgroundTransparency = 0.4}):Play()
        end)

        -- Click anywhere on the row body (NOT the emoji action buttons)
        -- → fire all admin commands one by one on this player. Same
        -- behavior as Click-to-AP's full-spam path. Bounds check below
        -- explicitly excludes the Actions frame so clicks on the emoji
        -- buttons don't double-trigger.
        row.Active = true
        row.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp, gp)
            if gp then return end
            if inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
               and inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
            if not plr or not plr.Parent or plr == LocalPlayer then return end

            -- Exclude clicks that landed inside the Actions area (emoji
            -- buttons handle their own clicks and we don't want to
            -- double-fire).
            local actions = row:FindFirstChild("Actions")
            if actions then
                local mp = inp.Position
                local ap, asz = actions.AbsolutePosition, actions.AbsoluteSize
                if mp.X >= ap.X and mp.X <= ap.X + asz.X
                   and mp.Y >= ap.Y and mp.Y <= ap.Y + asz.Y then
                    return
                end
            end

            -- Honor the same blocked-user toggles Click-to-AP uses.
            if rpIsAdminPanelBlockedForPlayer
               and rpIsAdminPanelBlockedForPlayer(plr) then return end

            if apTriggerAll then apTriggerAll(plr) end
        end)

        local avatarHolder = rpMake("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(9, 10, 14),
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 34),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 10),
            Parent = row
        })
        rpCorner(avatarHolder, 8)
        rpStroke(avatarHolder, RP_COLORS.AccentSoft, 1, 0.72)

        local avatar = rpMake("ImageLabel", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
            Image = "rbxthumb://type=AvatarHeadShot&id=" .. plr.UserId .. "&w=150&h=150",
            ZIndex = 10,
            Parent = avatarHolder
        })
        rpCorner(avatar, 8)

        local textWidth = -(RP_COLORS.ActionsWidth + RP_COLORS.SafeGap)

        local nameLabel = rpMake("TextLabel", {
            Name = "DisplayNameLabel",
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(50, 6),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, textWidth, 0, 20),
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            Text = plr.DisplayName,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
            TextSize = RP_COLORS.NameSize,
            TextColor3 = RP_COLORS.Text,
            ZIndex = 10,
            Parent = row
        })

        updateUserTag(plr)

        rpMake("TextLabel", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(50, 23),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, textWidth, 0, 16),
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            Text = "@" .. plr.Name,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
            TextSize = RP_COLORS.UserSize,
            TextColor3 = RP_COLORS.SubText,
            ZIndex = 10,
            Parent = row
        })

        local statusTag = rpMake("TextLabel", {
            Name = "Status",
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(50, 37),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, textWidth, 0, 14),
            Visible = false,
            Text = "",
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
            TextSize = RP_COLORS.UserSize,
            TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 200, 255),
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            ZIndex = 10,
            Parent = row
        })

        local stealGlow = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stealGlow.Color = Color3.fromRGB(70, 150, 255)
        stealGlow.Thickness = 1
        stealGlow.Transparency = 0.4
        stealGlow.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Contextual
        stealGlow.Parent = statusTag

        task.spawn(function()
            local pulseInfo = TweenInfo.new(1.0, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut)
            while statusTag.Parent do
                if statusTag.Visible then
                    TweenService:Create(stealGlow, pulseInfo, {Transparency = 0.85}):Play()
                    task.wait(1.0)
                    if not statusTag.Parent then break end
                    TweenService:Create(stealGlow, pulseInfo, {Transparency = 0.25}):Play()
                    task.wait(1.0)
                else
                    task.wait(0.2)
                end
            end
        end)

        stealLabels[plr.UserId] = {
            Tag = statusTag,
            BrainrotLabel = statusTag,
        }

        local actions = rpMake("Frame", {
            Name = "Actions",
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(RP_COLORS.ActionsWidth, RP_COLORS.ButtonSize),
            ZIndex = 12,
            Parent = row
        })

        local actionList = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        actionList.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
        actionList.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        actionList.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
        actionList.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
        actionList.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
        actionList.Parent = actions

        for i, b in ipairs(RP_BUTTONS) do
            createActionButton(actions, b, i, plr)
        end

        local bottomLine = rpMake("Frame", {
            Name = "Line",
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Visible = false,
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
            Parent = row
        })

        row.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then return end
            if rpIsAdminPanelBlockedForPlayer(plr) then
                rpRefreshActionButtonsForPlayer(plr)
                return
            end

            local orderedActions = {"ragdoll", "jail", "rocket", "balloon", "jumpscare", "inverse", "tiny", "morph"}
            local hasAnyAvailable = false
            for _, action in ipairs(orderedActions) do
                if not rpIsOnCooldown(action) then
                    hasAnyAvailable = true
                    break
                end
            end

            if hasAnyAvailable then
                for _, action in ipairs(orderedActions) do
                    if not rpIsOnCooldown(action) then
                        rpStartCooldown(action)
                        if rpIsAdminPanelBlockedForPlayer(plr) then
                            rpRefreshActionButtonsForPlayer(plr)
                            break
                        end
                        rpRunAdminCommand(plr, action)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        local cons = {}
        table.insert(cons, plr:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
            updateStatus(plr)
        end))
        table.insert(cons, plr:GetAttributeChangedSignal("StealingIndex"):Connect(function()
            updateStatus(plr)
        end))
        table.insert(cons, plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
            task.defer(function()
                updateUserTag(plr)
                local ok = pcall(function()
                    char.DescendantAdded:Connect(function()
                        task.defer(function()
                            updateUserTag(plr)
                        end)
                    end)
                    char.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function()
                        task.defer(function()
                            updateUserTag(plr)
                        end)
                    end)
                end)
            end)
        end))
        if plr.Character then
            table.insert(cons, plr.Character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function()
                task.defer(function()
                    updateUserTag(plr)
                end)
            end))
            table.insert(cons, plr.Character.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function()
                task.defer(function()
                    updateUserTag(plr)
                end)
            end))
        end
        attrCons[plr.UserId] = cons

        updateStatus(plr)
        updateUserTag(plr)
        rpRefreshActionButtonsForPlayer(plr)
        sortRows()
    end

    local function removePlayerRow(plr)
        local row = rows[plr.UserId]
        if row then
            row:Destroy()
            rows[plr.UserId] = nil
        end
        stealLabels[plr.UserId] = nil
        local cons = attrCons[plr.UserId]
        if cons then
            for _, c in ipairs(cons) do
                pcall(function()
                    c:Disconnect()
                end)
            end
            attrCons[plr.UserId] = nil
        end
    end

    rpui.refreshBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
                createPlayerRow(plr)
                updateStatus(plr)
            end
        end
        sortRows()
    end)

    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
            createPlayerRow(plr)
        end
    end

    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
        task.defer(function()
            createPlayerRow(plr)
        end)
    end)
    Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(removePlayerRow)

    task.spawn(function()
        while rpui.adminGui.Parent do
            updateProxViz()
            for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
                    updateUserTag(plr)
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.25)
        end
    end)

    if Config.HideAdminPanel then
        rpui.adminGui.Enabled = false
    end
end

__initAdminPanelLiteralReplacement()

task.spawn(function()
    -- Defer 2s — proximity scanner that requires PlotController. Two
    -- chained WaitForChild calls with 20s + 10s timeouts each, then a
    -- 0.15s poll loop. None of this matters in the first 2s after join.
    task.wait(2)
    local NEAR_DIST = 40
    local REARM_DIST = 60
    local POLL_RATE = 0.15

    local pc = nil
    pcall(function()
        local Controllers = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Controllers", 20)
        if Controllers then
            local mod = Controllers:WaitForChild("PlotController", 10)
            if mod then pc = require(mod) end
        end
    end)

    local function getPlot()
        if pc and pc.GetMyPlot then
            local ok, data = pcall(pc.GetMyPlot)
            if ok and data then
                if data.PlotModel and data.PlotModel.Parent then return data.PlotModel end
                if data.Name then
                    local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
                    return plots and plots:FindFirstChild(data.Name)
                end
            end
        end
        local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not plots then return nil end
        local Packages = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Packages")
        local syncMod = Packages and Packages:FindFirstChild("Synchronizer")
        if syncMod then
            local ok, Synchronizer = pcall(require, syncMod)
            if ok and Synchronizer then
                for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
                    local ok2, ch = pcall(function() return _G.stealthGet(plot.Name) end)
                    if ok2 and ch then
                        local owner = _G.sProp(ch, "Owner")
                        if owner and owner.Name == LocalPlayer.Name then
                            return plot
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function getPlotAnchor(plot)
        if not plot then return nil end
        local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
        if sign then
            local part = sign:IsA("BasePart") and sign or sign:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
            if part then return part.Position end
        end
        for _, c in ipairs(plot:GetDescendants()) do
            if c:IsA("BasePart") then return c.Position end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function scanSellSlots(plot)
        if not plot then return {} end
        local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
        if not podiums then return {} end
        local list = {}
        for _, podium in ipairs(podiums:GetChildren()) do
            local num = tonumber(podium.Name)
            if num then
                for _, d in ipairs(podium:GetDescendants()) do
                    if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and d.Enabled then
                        local at = (d.ActionText or ""):lower()
                        if at:find("sell") or at:find("remove") or at:find("retrieve") then
                            table.insert(list, {num = num, prompt = d})
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        table.sort(list, function(a, b) return a.num < b.num end)
        return list
    end

    local function doSell(prompt)
        if not prompt or not prompt.Parent or not prompt.Enabled then return false end
        local ok = pcall(function()
            if fireproximityprompt then fireproximityprompt(prompt) end
            pcall(function() prompt:InputHoldBegin() end)
            pcall(function() prompt:InputHoldEnd() end)
        end)
        return ok
    end

    local armed = true
    while true do repeat
        task.wait(POLL_RATE)
        if not Config.AutoSellRandomBrainrot then
            armed = true
            break
        end

        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then break end

        local plot = getPlot()
        if not plot then break end

        local anchor = getPlotAnchor(plot)
        if not anchor then break end

        local dist = (hrp.Position - anchor).Magnitude

        if armed and dist <= NEAR_DIST then
            local isStealing = LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing")
            if not isStealing then break end
            local slots = scanSellSlots(plot)
            if #slots > 0 then
                local target = slots[math.random(1, #slots)]
                doSell(target.prompt)
                armed = false
            end
        elseif (not armed) and dist > REARM_DIST then
            armed = true
        end
    until true end
end)


task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(15)
    _G._firstTPDone = true
end)

task.spawn(function()
    local _ip_Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local _ip_LP = _ip_Players.LocalPlayer

    local _ip_RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local _ip_SyncMod, _ip_AnimalsMod
    pcall(function() _ip_SyncMod = require(_ip_RS:WaitForChild("Packages"):WaitForChild("Synchronizer")) end)
    pcall(function() _ip_AnimalsMod = require(_ip_RS:WaitForChild("Datas"):WaitForChild("Animals")) end)
    local function _ip_getSync() return _ip_SyncMod end
    local function _ip_getAnimalsData() return _ip_AnimalsMod end

    local _incomingSlotGUIs = {}
    local _vanishIncomingByPlayer = {}
    local _vanishLastSeenSlots = {}
    local _vanishBaselined = {}
    local _vanishRecentStealersByPlot = {}
    local _ipTooLName = "Flying Carpet"

    local _VANISH_MUT_COLORS = {
        Cursed = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50),
        Gold = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0),
        Diamond = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255),
        YinYang = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220),
        Rainbow = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 200),
        Lava = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 20),
        Candy = Color3.fromRGB(255, 105, 180),
        Bloodrot = Color3.fromRGB(139, 0, 0),
        Radioactive = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0),
        Divine = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 150),
        Galaxy = Color3.fromRGB(155, 50, 255),
        Cyber = Color3.fromRGB(50, 180, 255),
    }

    local function _vanishIncomingClearAll()
        for slotPart, gui in pairs(_incomingSlotGUIs) do
            pcall(function() if gui and gui.Parent then gui:Destroy() end end)
            _incomingSlotGUIs[slotPart] = nil
        end
        for p, _ in pairs(_vanishIncomingByPlayer) do
            _vanishIncomingByPlayer[p] = nil
        end
    end
    _G._vanishIncomingClearAll = _vanishIncomingClearAll

    local function _vanishIncomingShow(thiefPlot, slotNumber, animalName, mutation, genText)
        if not thiefPlot or not thiefPlot.Parent then return end
        local podiums = thiefPlot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
        if not podiums then return end
        local podium = podiums:FindFirstChild(tostring(slotNumber))
        if not podium then return end
        local base = podium:FindFirstChild("Base")
        local spawn = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
        if not spawn or not spawn:IsA("BasePart") then return end

        if _incomingSlotGUIs[spawn] then
            pcall(function() _incomingSlotGUIs[spawn]:Destroy() end)
            _incomingSlotGUIs[spawn] = nil
        end

        local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        bb.Name = "VanishIncomingSlot"
        bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 170, 0, 80)
        bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 11, 0)
        bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
        bb.Adornee = spawn
        bb.Parent = spawn
        _incomingSlotGUIs[spawn] = bb

        local card = Instance.new("Frame")
        card.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 10, 14)
        card.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
        card.BorderSizePixel = 0
        card.Parent = bb
        Instance.new("UICorner", card).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)

        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", card)
        stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60)
        stroke.Thickness = 1.5
        stroke.Transparency = 0.2
        stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border

        local accent = Instance.new("Frame", card)
        accent.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, -10)
        accent.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 5)
        accent.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60)
        accent.BorderSizePixel = 0
        Instance.new("UICorner", accent).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

        local incomingLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
        incomingLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 78, 0, 14)
        incomingLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 4)
        incomingLbl.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 40, 40)
        incomingLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
        incomingLbl.BorderSizePixel = 0
        incomingLbl.Text = "⚠ INCOMING"
        incomingLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        incomingLbl.TextSize = 9
        incomingLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        incomingLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        Instance.new("UICorner", incomingLbl).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)

        local nameLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
        nameLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 18)
        nameLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 21)
        nameLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        nameLbl.Text = animalName or "Unknown"
        nameLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        nameLbl.TextSize = 13
        nameLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        nameLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        nameLbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd

        local genLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
        genLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 15)
        genLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 41)
        genLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        genLbl.Text = genText or ""
        genLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        genLbl.TextSize = 12
        genLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 220, 130)
        genLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        genLbl.Visible = genText ~= nil and genText ~= ""

        local mutKey = mutation and tostring(mutation) or ""
        if mutKey == "Yin Yang" then mutKey = "YinYang" end
        local mutColor = (mutKey ~= "" and mutKey ~= "None" and _VANISH_MUT_COLORS[mutKey]) or Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180)
        local infoText = (mutKey ~= "" and mutKey ~= "None") and (mutation .. "  ·  SLOT #" .. tostring(slotNumber)) or ("SLOT #" .. tostring(slotNumber))

        local infoLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
        infoLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 14)
        infoLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 60)
        infoLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        infoLbl.Text = infoText
        infoLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        infoLbl.TextSize = 10
        infoLbl.TextColor3 = mutColor
        infoLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        infoLbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    end

    local function _vanishIncomingHide(thiefPlot, slotNumber)
        if not thiefPlot then return end
        local podiums = thiefPlot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
        if not podiums then return end
        local podium = podiums:FindFirstChild(tostring(slotNumber))
        local base = podium and podium:FindFirstChild("Base")
        local spawn = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
        if spawn and _incomingSlotGUIs[spawn] then
            pcall(function() _incomingSlotGUIs[spawn]:Destroy() end)
            _incomingSlotGUIs[spawn] = nil
        end
    end

    local function _vanishFirstEmptySlot(thiefPlot)
        if not thiefPlot then return nil end
        local data = _G.VanishGetSyncData and _G.VanishGetSyncData(thiefPlot.Name)
        if not data then return nil end
        local al = data.AnimalList or {}
        local podiums = thiefPlot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
        if not podiums then return nil end
        for i = 1, 26 do
            if podiums:FindFirstChild(tostring(i)) then
                local entry = al[i] or al[tostring(i)]
                if type(entry) ~= "table" then return i end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function _vanishFindThiefPlot(thiefPlayer)
        if not thiefPlayer then return nil end
        local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not plots then return nil end
        for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
            local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
            if podiums and podiums:FindFirstChild("1") then
                local data = _G.VanishGetSyncData and _G.VanishGetSyncData(plot.Name)
                if data then
                    local owner = data.Owner
                    if owner == thiefPlayer or (typeof(owner) == "Instance" and owner.Name == thiefPlayer.Name) then
                        return plot
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function _vanishLookupStolenPetInfo(stealingValue)
        if not stealingValue then return nil end
        local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not plots then return nil end
        for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
            local data = _G.VanishGetSyncData and _G.VanishGetSyncData(plot.Name)
            if data then
                local al = data.AnimalList
                if al then
                    for _, ad in pairs(al) do
                        if type(ad) == "table" then
                            if ad.Index == stealingValue or tostring(ad.Index) == tostring(stealingValue) then
                                return ad, plot
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return nil, nil
    end

    local function _vanishHandleStealingChange(player)
        if not _G.IncomingSlotPredictor then return end
        if player == _ip_LP then return end
        local isStealing = player:GetAttribute("Stealing")
        if not isStealing then
            local rec = _vanishIncomingByPlayer[player]
            if rec and rec.plot and rec.slot then
                _vanishIncomingHide(rec.plot, rec.slot)
            end
            _vanishIncomingByPlayer[player] = nil
            return
        end
        local thiefPlot = _vanishFindThiefPlot(player)
        if not thiefPlot then return end
        local stealingIndex = player:GetAttribute("StealingIndex")
        local petInfo, sourcePlot
        if stealingIndex then
            petInfo, sourcePlot = _vanishLookupStolenPetInfo(stealingIndex)
        end
        if sourcePlot and sourcePlot == thiefPlot then return end
        local emptySlot = _vanishFirstEmptySlot(thiefPlot)
        if not emptySlot then return end
        local displayName = "Unknown"
        local mutation
        local genText
        if stealingIndex then
            local AnimalsData = _ip_getAnimalsData()
            local def = AnimalsData and AnimalsData[stealingIndex]
            displayName = (def and def.DisplayName) or tostring(stealingIndex)
            if petInfo then mutation = petInfo.Mutation end
            local sharedCache = SharedState and SharedState.AllAnimalsCache
            if sharedCache then
                for _, cached in ipairs(sharedCache) do
                    if cached.Index == stealingIndex then
                        genText = cached.genText
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        local prev = _vanishIncomingByPlayer[player]
        if prev and (prev.plot ~= thiefPlot or prev.slot ~= emptySlot) then
            _vanishIncomingHide(prev.plot, prev.slot)
        end
        _vanishIncomingShow(thiefPlot, emptySlot, displayName, mutation, genText)
        _vanishIncomingByPlayer[player] = {plot = thiefPlot, slot = emptySlot, index = stealingIndex}
        if stealingIndex then
            local plotIndices = _vanishRecentStealersByPlot[thiefPlot]
            if type(plotIndices) ~= "table" then plotIndices = {} end
            plotIndices[stealingIndex] = true
            _vanishRecentStealersByPlot[thiefPlot] = plotIndices
        end
    end

    local function _vanishMarkExpected(player)
        if not player:GetAttribute("Stealing") then return end
        local thiefPlot = _vanishFindThiefPlot(player)
        if not thiefPlot then return end
        local idx = player:GetAttribute("StealingIndex")
        if not idx then return end
        local plotIndices = _vanishRecentStealersByPlot[thiefPlot]
        if type(plotIndices) ~= "table" then plotIndices = {} end
        plotIndices[idx] = true
        _vanishRecentStealersByPlot[thiefPlot] = plotIndices
    end

    local function _vanishHookPlayer(player)
        if player == _ip_LP then return end
        pcall(function()
            player:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
                _vanishHandleStealingChange(player)
                _vanishMarkExpected(player)
            end)
            player:GetAttributeChangedSignal("StealingIndex"):Connect(function()
                if player:GetAttribute("Stealing") then
                    _vanishHandleStealingChange(player)
                    _vanishMarkExpected(player)
                end
            end)
        end)
        if player:GetAttribute("Stealing") then
            task.spawn(_vanishHandleStealingChange, player)
            _vanishMarkExpected(player)
        end
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        for _, p in ipairs(_ip_Players:GetPlayers()) do _vanishHookPlayer(p) end
        _ip_Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(_vanishHookPlayer)
        _ip_Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p)
            local rec = _vanishIncomingByPlayer[p]
            if rec and rec.plot and rec.slot then
                _vanishIncomingHide(rec.plot, rec.slot)
            end
            _vanishIncomingByPlayer[p] = nil
        end)
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        local plots = workspace:WaitForChild("Plots", 8)
        if not plots then return end
        local Sync = _ip_getSync()
        if not Sync then return end

        local function _vanishCheckArrivals(plot)
            -- AutoIncomingTP feature removed. This stub keeps the seen-slot
            -- baseline updated so the Incoming Slot Predictor visuals work.
            local data = _G.VanishGetSyncData and _G.VanishGetSyncData(plot.Name)
            if not data then return end
            local al = data.AnimalList
            if type(al) ~= "table" then return end
            local seen = _vanishLastSeenSlots[plot.Name] or {}
            for k, v in pairs(al) do
                seen[tostring(k)] = (type(v) == "table") or nil
            end
            _vanishLastSeenSlots[plot.Name] = seen
            _vanishBaselined[plot.Name] = true
        end

        local function refreshActive()
            if not _G.IncomingSlotPredictor then return end
            for player, _ in pairs(_vanishIncomingByPlayer) do
                if player and player.Parent and player:GetAttribute("Stealing") then
                    task.spawn(_vanishHandleStealingChange, player)
                end
            end
        end

        local function setup(plot)
            local ch
            local tries = 0
            while not ch and tries < 100 do
                local ok, r = pcall(function() return Sync:Get(plot.Name) end)
                if ok and r then ch = r; break else tries = tries + 1; task.wait(0.05) end
            end
            if not ch then return end
            local function onChange()
                _vanishCheckArrivals(plot)
                refreshActive()
            end
            pcall(function() ch:OnDictionaryInserted({"AnimalList"}, onChange) end)
            pcall(function() ch:OnDictionaryRemoved({"AnimalList"}, onChange) end)
            pcall(function() ch:OnChanged({"AnimalList"}, onChange) end)
            _vanishCheckArrivals(plot)
        end

        for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do task.spawn(setup, plot) end
        plots.ChildAdded:Connect(function(plot) task.spawn(setup, plot) end)
    end)
end)

task.spawn(function()
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

    local function clearGummyToolBlockState(char)
        local touched = false
        for _, inst in ipairs({ LocalPlayer, char }) do
            if inst then
                if inst:GetAttribute("BlockTools") ~= nil and inst:GetAttribute("BlockTools") ~= false then
                    inst:SetAttribute("BlockTools", false)
                    touched = true
                end
                if inst:GetAttribute("Web") ~= nil and inst:GetAttribute("Web") ~= false then
                    inst:SetAttribute("Web", false)
                    touched = true
                end
            end
        end
        if char and char:GetAttribute("BackpackReady") == false then
            char:SetAttribute("BackpackReady", true)
            touched = true
        end
        return touched
    end

    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        if not _G.AntiGummyBearEnabled then return end
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not char then return end
        clearGummyToolBlockState(char)
    end))

    LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        if not _G.AntiGummyBearEnabled then return end
        task.wait(0.1)
        clearGummyToolBlockState(char)
    end)
end)

task.spawn(function()
    local _abs_TOGGLE_KEY      = nil
    local _abs_POTION_NAME     = "Body Swap Potion"
    local _abs_HOLDER_JUMP_MIN = 3
    local _abs_SWAP_RADIUS     = 5
    local _abs_LOCAL_MOVED_MIN = 3
    local _abs_PER_PLAYER_CD   = 30

    local Players    = game:GetService("Players")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

    local hitCooldowns   = {}
    local localJustFired = false
    local prevHolderPos  = nil
    local prevLocalPos   = nil

    local function getLocalHRP()
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        return char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end

    local function getHolder()
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= LocalPlayer
               and plr.Character
               and plr.Character:FindFirstChild(_abs_POTION_NAME) then
                return plr
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function hookLocalPotion()
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not char then return end
        local function hook(potion)
            potion.Activated:Connect(function()
                localJustFired = true
                task.delay(1, function() localJustFired = false end)
            end)
        end
        local existing = char:FindFirstChild(_abs_POTION_NAME)
        if existing then hook(existing) end
        char.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if child.Name == _abs_POTION_NAME then hook(child) end
        end)
    end
    LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        task.wait(0.1)
        hookLocalPotion()
    end)
    hookLocalPotion()

    local function swapBack(targetPlayer)
        pcall(function()
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            if not char then return end
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not hum then return end

            local potion = char:FindFirstChild(_abs_POTION_NAME)
                        or (LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack") and LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild(_abs_POTION_NAME))
            if not potion then return end

            hum:EquipTool(potion)
            task.wait(0.05)

            local targetChar = targetPlayer.Character
            local targetHRP  = targetChar and targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local localHRP   = getLocalHRP()
            if targetHRP and localHRP then
                localHRP.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(
                    targetHRP.Position,
                    targetHRP.Position + targetHRP.CFrame.LookVector
                )
            end

            if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") then
                while LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") do task.wait() end
                task.wait(0.2)
                hum:EquipTool(potion)
                task.wait(0.05)
                if targetPlayer.Character then
                    local tHRP = targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local lHRP = getLocalHRP()
                    if tHRP and lHRP then
                        lHRP.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(
                            tHRP.Position,
                            tHRP.Position + tHRP.CFrame.LookVector
                        )
                    end
                end
            end

            potion = char:FindFirstChild(_abs_POTION_NAME)
            if potion then potion:Activate() end
        end)
    end

    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        if not _G.AntiBodySwapEnabled then
            prevHolderPos = nil
            prevLocalPos  = nil
            return
        end

        local holder = getHolder()
        if not holder then
            prevHolderPos = nil
            prevLocalPos  = nil
            return
        end

        local cd = hitCooldowns[holder.Name]
        if cd and tick() - cd < _abs_PER_PLAYER_CD then return end

        local holderHRP = holder.Character and holder.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local localHRP  = getLocalHRP()
        if not holderHRP or not localHRP then return end

        local currHolder = holderHRP.Position
        local currLocal  = localHRP.Position

        if prevHolderPos and prevLocalPos then
            local holderJumped        = (currHolder - prevHolderPos).Magnitude
            local holderNowAtMyOldPos = (currHolder - prevLocalPos).Magnitude < _abs_SWAP_RADIUS
            local iMoved              = (currLocal  - prevLocalPos).Magnitude > _abs_LOCAL_MOVED_MIN

            if holderJumped > _abs_HOLDER_JUMP_MIN
               and holderNowAtMyOldPos
               and iMoved
               and not localJustFired then
                hitCooldowns[holder.Name] = tick()
                task.spawn(function() swapBack(holder) end)
            end
        end

        prevHolderPos = currHolder
        prevLocalPos  = currLocal
    end))
end)

-- ============================================================
-- GLOBAL MOBILE GUI SWEEPER
-- Catches every hub-created ScreenGui in PlayerGui, ensures it has
-- a UIScale that responds to MobileGuiScale + viewport size, and
-- forces Active=true on draggable parents so touch drag works.
-- Runs on a 2s delay so all UIs have time to construct, then keeps
-- watching for new ScreenGuis added later.
-- ============================================================
task.spawn(function()
    if not IS_MOBILE then return end

    -- Bump default scale a bit so things are more readable on phone
    if (tonumber(Config.MobileGuiScale) or 0) < 0.5 then
        Config.MobileGuiScale = 0.55
        pcall(SaveConfig)
    end

    local function recomputeScale()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return tonumber(Config.MobileGuiScale) or 0.55 end
        local vw = cam.ViewportSize.X
        -- shrink further on very small screens, expand on tablets
        local base = tonumber(Config.MobileGuiScale) or 0.55
        if vw < 700 then return math.clamp(base * 0.85, 0.35, 0.9) end
        if vw > 1400 then return math.clamp(base * 1.15, 0.4, 1.0) end
        return base
    end

    local function ensureScale(sg)
        if not sg or not sg:IsA("ScreenGui") then return end
        local n = sg.Name
        -- Only touch hub-owned GUIs (Vanish*, AutoSteal*, Trade*, etc.)
        if not (n:find("Vanish") or n:find("AutoSteal") or n:find("Anti")
            or n:find("Trade") or n:find("AdminPanel") or n:find("Float")
            or n == "ClickLoggerGui") then return end

        for _, child in ipairs(sg:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("GuiObject") then
                local existing = child:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")
                if not existing then
                    existing = Instance.new("UIScale")
                    existing.Parent = child
                    SharedState.MobileScaleObjects[child] = existing
                end
                existing.Scale = recomputeScale()
                -- Active=true so touch drag works
                if child:IsA("Frame") and not child.Active then
                    pcall(function() child.Active = true end)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    task.wait(2)
    for _, sg in ipairs(LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:GetChildren()) do
        pcall(ensureScale, sg)
    end
    LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.ChildAdded:Connect(function(sg)
        task.wait(0.5)
        pcall(ensureScale, sg)
    end)

    -- React to viewport changes (orientation flip on phones)
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if cam then
        cam:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(function()
            local s = recomputeScale()
            for frame, sc in pairs(SharedState.MobileScaleObjects) do
                if frame and frame.Parent and sc and sc.Parent == frame then
                    sc.Scale = s
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end)


-- ============================================================
-- X-RAY BASES (Visuals tab toggle)
-- LocalTransparencyModifier-based xray on parts whose name (or
-- parent name) contains "base" or "claim". Hookmetamethod guard
-- on __index/__newindex prevents the game from resetting our
-- transparency. Camera CFrame refresh per RenderStepped so the
-- Invisicam occlusion mode keeps the see-through effect.
-- ============================================================
task.spawn(function()
    local ws = workspace
    local plrs = game:GetService("Players")
    local rs = game:GetService("RunService")
    local lp = plrs.LocalPlayer

    local saved = {}
    local enabled = false
    local descconn

    local isbasewall = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(obj)
        if not obj:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
        local name = obj.Name:lower()
        local parent = obj.Parent and obj.Parent.Name:lower() or ""
        return name:find("base") or name:find("claim") or parent:find("base") or parent:find("claim")
    end)

    local apply = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(obj)
        if not enabled or not isbasewall(obj) then return end
        if saved[obj] == nil then
            saved[obj] = obj.LocalTransparencyModifier
        end
        obj.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0.7
    end)

    if typeof(hookmetamethod) == "function" then
        local OldIndex
        OldIndex = hookmetamethod(game, "__index", newcclosure(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(self, key)
            if not checkcaller() and key == "LocalTransparencyModifier" and saved[self] ~= nil then
                return saved[self]
            end
            return OldIndex(self, key)
        end)))
        local OldNewIndex
        OldNewIndex = hookmetamethod(game, "__newindex", newcclosure(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(self, key, value)
            if not checkcaller() and key == "LocalTransparencyModifier" and saved[self] ~= nil then
                return
            end
            return OldNewIndex(self, key, value)
        end)))
    end

    local function enable()
        enabled = true
        if descconn then descconn:Disconnect() end
        local desc = ws:GetDescendants()
        for i = 1, #desc do apply(desc[i]) end
        descconn = ws.DescendantAdded:Connect(apply)
        pcall(function() lp.DevEnableMouseLock = true end)
        pcall(function() lp.DevCameraOcclusionMode = Enum.DevCameraOcclusionMode.Invisicam end)
    end

    local function disable()
        enabled = false
        if descconn then descconn:Disconnect(); descconn = nil end
        for part, val in pairs(saved) do
            if part and part.Parent then
                pcall(function() part.LocalTransparencyModifier = val end)
            end
            saved[part] = nil
        end
    end

    _G.VanishXrayBases = { enable = enable, disable = disable }

    if Config.XrayBases then enable() end

    lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        task.wait(0.5)
        if not enabled then return end
        local desc = ws:GetDescendants()
        for i = 1, #desc do apply(desc[i]) end
    end)

    rs.RenderStepped:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        if not enabled then return end
        local cam = ws.CurrentCamera
        if cam and cam.CameraSubject and cam.CameraType == Enum.CameraType.Custom then
            cam.CFrame = cam.CFrame
        end
    end))
end)

-- ============================================================
-- SKY PRESET SYSTEM — single unified controller.
-- Presets: Off | Moonlight | Space | Dark | Classic
-- ============================================================
task.spawn(function()
    local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
    local SKY_NAME = "VanishSkyPreset"

    local PRESETS = {
        Off     = nil, -- restores original sky
        Moonlight = {
            bk = "rbxassetid://12064107", dn = "rbxassetid://12064152",
            ft = "rbxassetid://12064121", lf = "rbxassetid://12063984",
            rt = "rbxassetid://12064115", up = "rbxassetid://12064131",
            celestial = false, stars = 0,
        },
        Space = {
            bk = "rbxassetid://149397692", dn = "rbxassetid://149397686",
            ft = "rbxassetid://149397697", lf = "rbxassetid://149397684",
            rt = "rbxassetid://149397688", up = "rbxassetid://149397702",
            celestial = false, stars = 3000,
        },
        Dark = { _clocktime = 0 },  -- no textures; uses midnight gradient sky
        Classic = {
            bk = "rbxassetid://159454299", dn = "rbxassetid://159454296",
            ft = "rbxassetid://159454293", lf = "rbxassetid://159454286",
            rt = "rbxassetid://159454300", up = "rbxassetid://159454288",
            celestial = true, stars = 3000,
        },
    }

    local stashed        = {}
    local addedConn, removedConn, refreshConn
    local activePreset   = nil  -- nil = Off
    local savedClockTime = nil  -- saved when Dark preset is active

    local function isObstruction(c)
        return (c:IsA("Sky") and c.Name ~= SKY_NAME)
            or c:IsA("Atmosphere") or c:IsA("Clouds")
    end

    local function stash(c)
        if stashed[c] then return end
        stashed[c] = c.Parent
        pcall(function() c.Parent = nil end)
    end

    local function applyToSky(s, p)
        pcall(function()
            s.SkyboxBk = p.bk; s.SkyboxDn = p.dn; s.SkyboxFt = p.ft
            s.SkyboxLf = p.lf; s.SkyboxRt = p.rt; s.SkyboxUp = p.up
            s.CelestialBodiesShown = p.celestial
            s.StarCount = p.stars
        end)
    end

    local function ensureSkyIn(p)
        local s = Lighting:FindFirstChild(SKY_NAME)
        if not s then
            s = Instance.new("Sky")
            s.Name = SKY_NAME
            applyToSky(s, p)
            s.Parent = Lighting
        else
            applyToSky(s, p)
        end
    end

    local function teardown()
        if addedConn   then addedConn:Disconnect();   addedConn   = nil end
        if removedConn then removedConn:Disconnect(); removedConn = nil end
        refreshConn = nil
        local mine = Lighting:FindFirstChild(SKY_NAME)
        if mine then pcall(function() mine:Destroy() end) end
        for inst, parent in pairs(stashed) do
            if inst then pcall(function() inst.Parent = parent or Lighting end) end
        end
        stashed = {}
        -- Restore clock time saved by the Dark preset
        if savedClockTime ~= nil then
            pcall(function() Lighting.ClockTime = savedClockTime end)
            savedClockTime = nil
        end
        activePreset = nil
    end

    local function applyPreset(name)
        local p = PRESETS[name]
        teardown()
        if not p then return end  -- "Off" — just restore original
        activePreset = p

        for _, c in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
            if isObstruction(c) then stash(c) end
        end

        if p._clocktime ~= nil then
            -- Dark mode: no sky textures needed — set midnight clock so
            -- Roblox renders its own built-in dark gradient sky.
            pcall(function()
                savedClockTime     = Lighting.ClockTime
                Lighting.ClockTime = p._clocktime
            end)
            addedConn = Lighting.ChildAdded:Connect(function(c)
                if activePreset and isObstruction(c) then stash(c) end
            end)
            refreshConn = task.spawn(function()
                while activePreset == p do
                    task.wait(2)
                    if activePreset ~= p then break end
                    pcall(function() Lighting.ClockTime = p._clocktime end)
                end
            end)
        else
            ensureSkyIn(p)

            addedConn = Lighting.ChildAdded:Connect(function(c)
                if activePreset and isObstruction(c) then stash(c) end
            end)
            removedConn = Lighting.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(c)
                if activePreset and c.Name == SKY_NAME then
                    task.defer(function() if activePreset then ensureSkyIn(activePreset) end end)
                end
            end)
            refreshConn = task.spawn(function()
                while activePreset == p do
                    task.wait(2)
                    if activePreset ~= p then break end
                    local s = Lighting:FindFirstChild(SKY_NAME)
                    if not s then ensureSkyIn(p) else applyToSky(s, p) end
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    _G.VanishSkyPreset = { set = applyPreset }

    -- Validate saved preset then apply
    local saved = Config.SkyPreset
    if type(saved) ~= "string" or not PRESETS[saved] and saved ~= "Off" then
        saved = "Off"
    end
    applyPreset(saved)
end)

-- ============================================================
-- PLOT TIMER ESP — BillboardGui over each plot showing the
-- lock countdown or "Unlocked". Uses the hub's blue accent
-- (was indigo in the source snippet).
-- ============================================================
task.spawn(function()
    -- Defer 2s — PlotTimerESP iterates every plot, requires Synchronizer,
    -- creates BillboardGui per plot, hooks attribute signals. Outer LPH
    -- wrap removed (Luraph rejects nesting); inner wraps cover hot paths.
    task.wait(2)
    local PT_Players           = game:GetService("Players")
    local PT_ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local PT_Workspace         = game:GetService("Workspace")
    local PT_LocalPlayer       = PT_Players.LocalPlayer

    local PT_THEME    = Color3.fromRGB(0, 170, 255)    -- hub blue accent
    local PT_UNLOCKED = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 120)    -- green for "Unlocked"
    local PT_REFRESH  = 0.5
    local PT_TEXTSIZE = 28

    local PT_Sync
    pcall(function()
        PT_Sync = require(PT_ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Packages"):WaitForChild("Synchronizer"))
    end)

    local function PT_getOwnerName(plot)
        local data = _G.VanishGetSyncData and _G.VanishGetSyncData(plot.Name)
        local owner = data and data.Owner
        if owner and owner.Name then return owner.Name end
        return nil
    end

    local function PT_getEndTime(plot)
        local data = _G.VanishGetSyncData and _G.VanishGetSyncData(plot.Name)
        local v = data and data.BlockEndTime
        if type(v) == "number" then return v end
        return nil
    end

    local function PT_getBlock(plot)
        local p = plot:FindFirstChild("Purchases")
        local b = p and p:FindFirstChild("PlotBlock")
        local m = b and b:FindFirstChild("Main")
        local g = m and m:FindFirstChild("BillboardGui")
        if not g then return nil end
        return {
            main       = m,
            gui        = g,
            remaining  = g:FindFirstChild("RemainingTime"),
            lockStudio = g:FindFirstChild("LockStudio"),
        }
    end

    local function PT_getState(plot, ownerName)
        local b = PT_getBlock(plot)
        if not b then return "UNKNOWN", nil end
        if b.remaining and b.remaining.Visible then
            local endT = PT_getEndTime(plot)
            if endT then
                local now = PT_Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
                local rem = math.max(0, math.ceil(endT - now))
                if rem <= 0 then return "LOCKED", 0 end
                return "LOCKING", rem
            end
            local txt = b.remaining.Text or ""
            local sec = tonumber(txt:match("(%d+)%s*s"))
            return "LOCKING", sec
        end
        if not ownerName then return "UNCLAIMED", nil end
        local mainPresent       = b.main and b.main:IsA("BasePart") and b.main.Transparency <= 0.5
        local lockStudioShowing = b.lockStudio and b.lockStudio.Visible
        if mainPresent and not lockStudioShowing then return "LOCKED", nil end
        return "UNLOCKED", nil
    end

    local function PT_createBillboard(plot)
        local friendPanel = plot:FindFirstChild("FriendPanel")
        local adornPart
        if friendPanel then
            adornPart = friendPanel:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart") or friendPanel.PrimaryPart
        end
        if not adornPart then adornPart = plot:FindFirstChild("MainRoot") end
        if not adornPart then return nil end
        local existing = adornPart:FindFirstChild("VanishPlotTimerESP")
        if existing then existing:Destroy() end
        local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        bb.Name        = "VanishPlotTimerESP"
        bb.Size        = UDim2.new(0, 250, 0, 50)
        bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
        bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
        bb.MaxDistance = math.huge
        bb.Adornee     = adornPart
        bb.Parent      = adornPart
        local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        lbl.Name                 = "TimerLabel"
        lbl.Size                 = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lbl.TextColor3           = PT_THEME
        lbl.Font                 = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        lbl.TextSize             = PT_TEXTSIZE
        lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.2
        lbl.TextStrokeColor3     = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        lbl.Text                 = "Loading..."
        lbl.Parent               = bb
        return bb
    end

    local function PT_updateBillboard(plot, bb)
        if not bb or not bb.Parent then return false end
        local lbl = bb:FindFirstChild("TimerLabel")
        if not lbl then return false end
        local ownerName     = PT_getOwnerName(plot)
        local state, secs   = PT_getState(plot, ownerName)
        if state == "LOCKING" and secs and secs > 0 then
            lbl.Text       = secs .. "s"
            lbl.TextColor3 = PT_THEME
        else
            lbl.Text       = "Unlocked"
            lbl.TextColor3 = PT_UNLOCKED
        end
        return true
    end

    local PT_billboards = {}
    local PT_enabled    = false
    local PT_loopTask   = nil

    local function PT_clearAll()
        for plot, bb in pairs(PT_billboards) do
            if bb and bb.Parent then pcall(function() bb:Destroy() end) end
            PT_billboards[plot] = nil
        end
    end

    local function PT_ensurePlots()
        local plots = PT_Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not plots then return end
        for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
            local bb = PT_billboards[plot]
            if not bb or not bb.Parent then
                bb = PT_createBillboard(plot)
                PT_billboards[plot] = bb
            end
            if bb then PT_updateBillboard(plot, bb) end
        end
    end

    local function PT_enable()
        if PT_enabled then return end
        PT_enabled = true
        PT_loopTask = task.spawn(function()
            while PT_enabled do
                pcall(PT_ensurePlots)
                task.wait(PT_REFRESH)
            end
        end)
    end

    local function PT_disable()
        if not PT_enabled then return end
        PT_enabled = false
        PT_loopTask = nil
        PT_clearAll()
    end

    _G.VanishPlotTimerESP = { enable = PT_enable, disable = PT_disable }
    if Config.PlotTimerESP then PT_enable() end
end)


-- ============================================================
-- KRYO-STYLE DESYNC (raknet 0x1B garble + force-sync heartbeat)
-- Active ONLY while LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") is true —
-- turns off on its own when not stealing. Keeps the existing
-- setJoin/suspend/resume/isActive interface so all other code stays wired.
-- Send hook: garbles position packets (PacketId 0x1B) by writing
--   0xFFFFFFFF into bytes 1, 5, 9. Lets one packet through every 5s
--   so the server never marks us offline (no lagback).
-- Heartbeat: forces a periodic CFrame nudge (sync flush) every
--   SyncInterval studs or TimeInterval seconds.
-- Visualizer: ball part with billboard "SERVER POS X.X studs" label
--   in blue. Freezes while desyncing, follows HRP otherwise.
-- ============================================================
task.spawn(function()
    local Players    = game:GetService("Players")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local TweenSvc   = game:GetService("TweenService")
    local LP         = Players.LocalPlayer
    while not LP do task.wait(0.2); LP = Players.LocalPlayer end

    local SYNC_TIME  = 6    -- re-sync (flush a clean packet) at least every Ns while stealing
    local SYNC_DIST  = 12   -- ...or after moving this many studs (server follows you while walking)
    local PULSE_GAP  = 6    -- safety pulse: at most one clean packet per Ns even between forced syncs

    local enabled        = false   -- master flag (Desync-on-Steal toggle / setJoin)
    local desync_active  = false   -- true ONLY while the Stealing attribute is true
    local hooksInstalled = false
    local frozenPos      = Vector3.zero
    local lastSyncTime   = 0
    local lastPulse      = 0

    -- ── BLUE VISUALIZER (SERVER POS tracer) ──────────────────────────────
    local Visualizer = {
        visualPart    = nil,
        visualGui     = nil,
        visualText    = nil,
        serverPos     = nil,
        lastRealPos   = nil,
        cframeConn    = nil,
        renderConn    = nil,
        BALL_COLOR    = Color3.fromRGB(40, 90, 200),
        BALL_COLOR2   = Color3.fromRGB(80, 140, 255),
        Y_OFFSET      = -1,
    }

    local function destroyVisualizer()
        if Visualizer.cframeConn then
            pcall(function() Visualizer.cframeConn:Disconnect() end)
            Visualizer.cframeConn = nil
        end
        if Visualizer.renderConn then
            pcall(function() Visualizer.renderConn:Disconnect() end)
            Visualizer.renderConn = nil
        end
        if Visualizer.visualPart then
            pcall(function() Visualizer.visualPart:Destroy() end)
            Visualizer.visualPart = nil
        end
        Visualizer.visualGui   = nil
        Visualizer.visualText  = nil
        Visualizer.serverPos   = nil
        Visualizer.lastRealPos = nil
    end

    local function attachVisualizer()
        destroyVisualizer()
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then return end
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return end

        Visualizer.serverPos   = hrp.Position
        Visualizer.lastRealPos = hrp.Position

        local part = Instance.new("Part")
        part.Name         = "VanishDesyncServerPos"
        part.Shape        = Enum.PartType.Ball
        part.Size         = Vector3.new(2.5, 2.5, 2.5)
        part.Color        = Visualizer.BALL_COLOR
        part.Material     = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
        part.Transparency = 0.1
        part.Reflectance  = 0.2
        part.CanCollide   = false
        part.CanTouch     = false
        part.CanQuery     = false
        part.CastShadow   = false
        part.Anchored     = true
        part.Locked       = true
        part.CFrame       = CFrame.new(Visualizer.serverPos + Vector3.new(0, Visualizer.Y_OFFSET, 0))
        part.Parent       = workspace
        Visualizer.visualPart = part

        local sel = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
        sel.Adornee       = part
        sel.Color3        = Visualizer.BALL_COLOR2
        sel.LineThickness = 0.08
        sel.Transparency  = 0.7
        sel.Parent        = part

        local gui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        gui.Name        = "DesyncGui"
        gui.Size        = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 55)
        gui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0)
        gui.AlwaysOnTop = true
        gui.Enabled     = true
        gui.Parent      = part
        Visualizer.visualGui = gui

        local bg = Instance.new("Frame")
        bg.Size                   = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        bg.BackgroundColor3       = Color3.fromRGB(20, 30, 55)
        bg.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6
        bg.BorderSizePixel        = 0
        bg.Parent                 = gui
        Instance.new("UICorner", bg).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

        local border = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        border.Color        = Visualizer.BALL_COLOR2
        border.Thickness    = 1
        border.Transparency = 0.3
        border.Parent       = bg

        local txt = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        txt.Size                   = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        txt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        txt.Font                   = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        txt.TextColor3             = Color3.fromRGB(120, 200, 255)
        txt.TextStrokeColor3       = Color3.new(0.1, 0.3, 0.6)
        txt.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
        txt.TextScaled             = false
        txt.TextSize               = 13
        txt.Text                   = "SERVER POS\n0.0 studs"
        txt.Parent                 = bg
        Visualizer.visualText = txt

        -- No CFrame signal — that just made the orb trail the character.
        -- The orb's position is driven by `frozenPos` (the engine's force-sync
        -- anchor) while desyncing, and snaps to HRP otherwise.
    end

    local function snapVisualizerToHRP()
        local char = LP.Character
        local hrp  = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp then
            Visualizer.serverPos   = hrp.Position
            Visualizer.lastRealPos = hrp.Position
            if Visualizer.visualPart then
                Visualizer.visualPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, Visualizer.Y_OFFSET, 0))
            end
        end
    end

    local function setVisualizerVisible(v)
        if not Visualizer.visualPart then return end
        if v then
            Visualizer.visualPart.Transparency = 0.1
        else
            Visualizer.visualPart.Transparency = 1
        end
        if Visualizer.visualGui then
            Visualizer.visualGui.Enabled = v
        end
        for _, c in ipairs(Visualizer.visualPart:GetChildren()) do
            if c:IsA("SelectionBox") then
                c.Transparency = v and 0.7 or 1
            end
        end
    end

    local function updateVisualizer()
        local char = LP.Character
        local hrp  = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return end
        if not Visualizer.visualPart or not Visualizer.visualPart.Parent then
            attachVisualizer()
            return
        end
        -- Only show the orb while actively desyncing. Hide otherwise so it
        -- isn't stuck on the character when there's no gap to display.
        if not desync_active then
            setVisualizerVisible(false)
            Visualizer.serverPos = hrp.Position
            Visualizer.visualPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, Visualizer.Y_OFFSET, 0))
            return
        end
        Visualizer.serverPos = frozenPos
        Visualizer.visualPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(Visualizer.serverPos + Vector3.new(0, Visualizer.Y_OFFSET, 0))
        local dist0 = (Visualizer.serverPos - hrp.Position).Magnitude
        -- Hide while there's no meaningful gap yet (avoids the orb popping
        -- onto the character at the instant Stealing fires).
        setVisualizerVisible(dist0 > 0.5)
        if Visualizer.visualText then
            local dist = dist0
            Visualizer.visualText.Text = string.format("SERVER POS\n%.1f studs", dist)
            if dist > 20 then
                Visualizer.visualText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 220, 255)
                Visualizer.visualPart.Color      = Color3.fromRGB(80, 160, 255)
            elseif dist > 10 then
                Visualizer.visualText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(140, 200, 255)
                Visualizer.visualPart.Color      = Color3.fromRGB(60, 120, 220)
            else
                Visualizer.visualText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 180, 255)
                Visualizer.visualPart.Color      = Visualizer.BALL_COLOR
            end
        end
    end

    local function startTracker()
        if Visualizer.renderConn then return end
        attachVisualizer()
        -- Throttle the orb update to 20Hz — it's a position/label visual that
        -- looks identical at 20Hz but was running updateVisualizer() (CFrame +
        -- distance math + text format) every RenderStepped frame.
        local _vizLast = 0
        Visualizer.renderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            local _vNow = os.clock()
            if _vNow - _vizLast < 0.05 then return end
            _vizLast = _vNow
            updateVisualizer()
        end))
    end

    -- ── Force-sync helper: actually flush ONE clean position packet. ──
    -- Resetting lastPulse makes the very next 0x1B packet pass through the send
    -- hook UN-garbled (see hook's PULSE_GAP check), so the server snaps to our
    -- real position. (Previously this only did a no-op CFrame nudge whose packet
    -- got garbled anyway — so the periodic re-sync never actually synced, and the
    -- server stayed stuck behind you while you walked.)
    local function forceSync()
        local char = LP.Character
        local hrp  = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return end
        lastPulse = 0
        pcall(function() hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 0) end)
    end

    -- ── RakNet send hook ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    local function installHooks()
        if hooksInstalled then return end
        if typeof(raknet) ~= "table" or typeof(raknet.add_send_hook) ~= "function" then
            return
        end
        hooksInstalled = true
        raknet.add_send_hook(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(packet)
            if not desync_active then return end
            if packet.PacketId ~= 0x1B then return end
            -- Every PULSE_GAP seconds, let one clean packet through so the
            -- server never marks us offline (prevents lagback).
            if tick() - lastPulse > PULSE_GAP then
                lastPulse = tick()
                return
            end
            local ok, b = pcall(function() return packet.AsBuffer end)
            if ok and b then
                pcall(function()
                    if buffer.len(b) >= 13 then
                        buffer.writeu32(b, 1, 0xFFFFFFFF)
                        buffer.writeu32(b, 5, 0xFFFFFFFF)
                        buffer.writeu32(b, 9, 0xFFFFFFFF)
                        packet:SetData(b)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end))
    end

    -- ── Single driver: the Stealing attribute. ────────────────────────
    -- Desync is ON only while Stealing is true, OFF the instant it drops
    -- (brainrot grabbed/dropped). No poll loop, no watchdog, no suspend
    -- window — GetAttributeChangedSignal is reliable and the connection is
    -- on the Player so it survives respawns. Keeping the state this simple
    -- is the whole point: it can't get stuck on or fight itself.
    local function setActive(on)
        if on then
            local char = LP.Character
            local hrp  = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then frozenPos = hrp.Position end
            lastSyncTime = tick()
            lastPulse    = tick()
            desync_active = true
        else
            desync_active = false
            frozenPos     = Vector3.zero
            snapVisualizerToHRP()
        end
    end

    LP:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
        if not enabled then return end
        setActive(LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true)
    end)

    -- ── Periodic re-sync (only while desyncing): flush one clean position
    --    packet + advance the orb every SYNC_TIME (7s) OR after moving
    --    SYNC_DIST studs, whichever first.
    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        if not desync_active then return end
        local char = LP.Character
        local hrp  = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return end
        if (hrp.Position - frozenPos).Magnitude >= SYNC_DIST
           or (tick() - lastSyncTime) >= SYNC_TIME then
            forceSync()
            frozenPos    = hrp.Position
            lastSyncTime = tick()
        end
    end))

    _G.VanishDesync = {
        setJoin = function(v)
            if v then
                installHooks()
                enabled = true
                startTracker()
                -- If we're already mid-steal when enabled, turn on now.
                if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") then setActive(true) end
            else
                enabled = false
                setActive(false)
                destroyVisualizer()
            end
        end,
        rollback = function()
            local char = LP.Character
            local hrp  = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp and Visualizer.visualPart then
                pcall(function() hrp.CFrame = Visualizer.visualPart.CFrame end)
            end
        end,
        -- No-ops: desync is purely Stealing-gated now, so there's nothing
        -- to suspend/resume. (The old 15s suspend window had a bug where it
        -- blocked desync from re-activating on a steal within that window.)
        suspend  = function() end,
        resume   = function() end,
        isActive = function() return enabled end,
    }

    if Config.DesyncOnJoin then
        -- Arm directly on (re)join. Desync only activates on the Stealing
        -- attribute, so arming now never garbles until an actual steal. The old
        -- defer-until-first-clone path was never triggered by the Side TP, so
        -- desync stayed un-armed after a rejoin until a manual off/on toggle.
        _G.VanishDesync.setJoin(true)
    end
end)

-- =============================================================
-- QUICK PANEL — matches Controls panel / steal-target UI style
-- =============================================================
task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(2) -- let all feature systems finish initialising

    -- ── Scale helpers (same pattern as controls panel) ────────
    local mobileScale       = IS_MOBILE and 0.6 or 1
    local mobileButtonScale = IS_MOBILE and 1.3  or 1

    -- ── Colours — exact match to MiniTargetColors / Controls ──
    local QP_BG    = Color3.fromRGB(12,  14,  18)
    local QP_SURF  = Color3.fromRGB(20,  22,  28)
    local QP_SURF2 = Color3.fromRGB(28,  32,  40)
    local QP_TEXT  = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    local QP_DIM   = Color3.fromRGB(150, 155, 165)
    local QP_ACC   = GetVanishAccent()                          -- resolved locally, not from outer scope
    local QP_ACC2  = QP_ACC:Lerp(Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 0.18)
    local QP_ACCL  = QP_ACC:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.47)

    -- ── Dimensions — fixed, same as controls panel ────────────
    local QP_W        = 265    -- steal-target UI width
    local QP_H        = 294    -- controls panel auto-sized height (6 rows × 32 + gaps + header)
    local QP_HEADER_H = 44
    local QP_FOOTER_H = 44
    local QP_ROW_H    = math.floor(32 * mobileButtonScale)
    local QP_ROW_GAP  = 6

    -- ── Config bootstrap ──────────────────────────────────────
    if type(Config.Positions) ~= "table" then Config.Positions = {} end
    -- Default to right side, but DO NOT reset existing user drags. Old
    -- "X < 0.5 → reset" check was clobbering every legit drag to the
    -- left half of the screen, which broke dragging-save.
    if type(Config.Positions.QuickPanel) ~= "table" then
        Config.Positions.QuickPanel = {X = 0.76, Y = 0.10}
    end

    -- ── Feature registry ──────────────────────────────────────
    -- Two feature types supported:
    --   • Toggle (default) — has `get` and `set`. Renders a switch.
    --   • Action — has `kind = "action"` and a `run` function. Renders a
    --     sleek RUN button; click → run() fires once, no on/off state.
    local QP_FEATURES = {
        -- ── Actions (one-shot, top of list) ─────────────────────
        { key = "InstantRespawn", label = "Instant Respawn", kind = "action",
          run = function()
              -- Prefer the script's own executeReset (handles anti-die
              -- bypass + balloon-reset remote). Fall back to BreakJoints
              -- on the local character when executeReset isn't available.
              if _G.executeReset then
                  pcall(_G.executeReset)
                  return
              end
              local char = LocalPlayer.Character
              if not char then return end
              local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
              if hum then pcall(function() hum.Health = 0 end) end
              pcall(function() char:BreakJoints() end)
          end },

        -- ── Automation: Steal section ──────────────────────────
