        { key = "AutoInvisDuringSteal", label = "Auto Invis During Steal",
          get = function() return Config.AutoInvisDuringSteal == true end,
          set = function(v)
              Config.AutoInvisDuringSteal = v
              _G.AutoInvisDuringSteal = v
              SaveConfig()
              if _G.syncMainAutoInvisToggle then pcall(_G.syncMainAutoInvisToggle, v) end
          end },
        { key = "AutoTpOnFailedSteal",  label = "Auto TP on Failed Steal",
          get = function() return Config.AutoTpOnFailedSteal == true end,
          set = function(v) Config.AutoTpOnFailedSteal = v; SaveConfig() end },
        { key = "AutoTPPriority",       label = "Auto TP Priority Mode",
          get = function() return Config.AutoTPPriority == true end,
          set = function(v) Config.AutoTPPriority = v; SaveConfig() end },
        { key = "AutoKickOnSteal",      label = "Auto Kick on Steal",
          get = function() return Config.AutoKickOnSteal == true end,
          set = function(v) Config.AutoKickOnSteal = v; SaveConfig() end },
        { key = "AutoUnlockOnSteal",    label = "Auto Unlock on Steal",
          get = function() return Config.AutoUnlockOnSteal == true end,
          set = function(v) Config.AutoUnlockOnSteal = v; SaveConfig() end },
        { key = "AutoBack",             label = "Auto Back on Hit",
          get = function() return Config.AutoBack == true end,
          set = function(v) Config.AutoBack = v; SaveConfig() end },
        -- ── Automation: Server section ─────────────────────────
        { key = "DesyncOnJoin",         label = "Desync on Steal",
          get = function() return Config.DesyncOnJoin == true end,
          set = function(v)
              Config.DesyncOnJoin = v; SaveConfig()
              if _G.VanishDesync then pcall(_G.VanishDesync.setJoin, v) end
          end },
        { key = "DesyncOnDelta",        label = "Desync on Delta",
          get = function() return Config.DesyncOnDelta == true end,
          set = function(v)
              Config.DesyncOnDelta = v; SaveConfig()
              if _G.VanishDeltaDesync then pcall(_G.VanishDeltaDesync.set, v) end
          end },
        -- ── Controls panel: steal mode ─────────────────────────
        { key = "StealNearest",         label = "Steal Nearest",
          get = function() return Config.StealNearest == true end,
          set = function(v) Config.StealNearest = v; SaveConfig() end },
        { key = "StealHighest",         label = "Steal Highest",
          get = function() return Config.StealHighest == true end,
          set = function(v) Config.StealHighest = v; SaveConfig() end },
        { key = "StealPriority",        label = "Steal Priority",
          get = function() return Config.StealPriority == true end,
          set = function(v) Config.StealPriority = v; SaveConfig() end },
    }

    local featureByKey = {}
    for _, f in ipairs(QP_FEATURES) do featureByKey[f.key] = f end

    -- ── Tween helper ──────────────────────────────────────────
    local function qpTween(obj, t, props)
        TweenService:Create(obj, TweenInfo.new(t, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), props):Play()
    end

    -- ── ScreenGui ─────────────────────────────────────────────
    if type(Config.QuickPanelEnabled) ~= "boolean" then Config.QuickPanelEnabled = false end

    local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    sg.Name           = "VanishQuickPanel"
    sg.ResetOnSpawn   = false
    sg.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    sg.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    sg.DisplayOrder   = 999
    sg.Enabled        = Config.QuickPanelEnabled
    sg.Parent         = PlayerGui

    _G.SetQuickPanelEnabled = function(enabled)
        Config.QuickPanelEnabled = enabled == true
        sg.Enabled = Config.QuickPanelEnabled
        SaveConfig()
    end

    -- ── Panel — Frame, same style as targetControlsFrame ──────
    local panel = Instance.new("Frame")
    panel.Name               = "QuickPanel"
    panel.Size               = UDim2.new(0, QP_W * mobileScale, 0, QP_H * mobileScale)
    -- MakeDraggable saves BOTH Scale (X/Y) and Offset (OffsetX/OffsetY).
    -- Earlier load only used Scale, so the pixel-precise drag was lost
    -- every reload. Read both fields with `or 0` fallback for backward
    -- compat with pre-existing saved configs.
    panel.Position           = UDim2.new(
        Config.Positions.QuickPanel.X, Config.Positions.QuickPanel.OffsetX or 0,
        Config.Positions.QuickPanel.Y, Config.Positions.QuickPanel.OffsetY or 0)
    panel.BackgroundColor3   = QP_BG
    panel.BackgroundTransparency = 0.04
    panel.BorderSizePixel    = 0
    panel.ClipsDescendants   = true
    panel.ZIndex             = 100
    panel.Parent             = sg
    do local c = Instance.new("UICorner"); c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8); c.Parent = panel end
    ApplyViewportUIScale(panel, QP_W, 500, 0.45, 0.8)

    -- Animated glow border (identical to targetControlsFrame)
    do
        local glow = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        glow.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        glow.Thickness       = 1.5
        glow.Color           = QP_ACC
        glow.Transparency    = 0.4
        glow.Parent          = panel
        local grad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        grad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,   QP_ACC),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, QP_ACCL),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,   QP_ACC),
        })
        grad.Parent = glow
        task.spawn(function()
            while panel.Parent do
                TweenService:Create(glow, TweenInfo.new(1.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Transparency = 0.15, Thickness = 2.2}):Play()
                task.wait(1.4)
                if not panel.Parent then break end
                TweenService:Create(glow, TweenInfo.new(1.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Transparency = 0.45, Thickness = 1.4}):Play()
                task.wait(1.4)
            end
        end)
    end

    -- ── Header — matches targetControlsHeader ─────────────────
    local header = Instance.new("Frame", panel)
    header.Size               = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, QP_HEADER_H)
    header.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    header.ZIndex             = 101
    MakeDraggable(header, panel, "QuickPanel")

    local titleLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", header)
    titleLbl.Size               = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 24)
    titleLbl.Position           = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 10)
    titleLbl.ZIndex             = 102
    titleLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    titleLbl.Text               = "CUSTOM PANEL"
    titleLbl.Font               = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    titleLbl.TextSize           = 16
    titleLbl.TextColor3         = QP_TEXT
    titleLbl.TextXAlignment     = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center

    -- ── Content — fixed-height scroll (panel never resizes) ───
    local contentScroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame", panel)
    contentScroll.Name                  = "Content"
    contentScroll.Size                  = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, (QP_H - QP_HEADER_H - QP_FOOTER_H - 4) * mobileScale)
    contentScroll.Position              = UDim2.fromOffset(5, QP_HEADER_H + 4)
    contentScroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    contentScroll.BorderSizePixel       = 0
    contentScroll.ScrollBarThickness    = 3
    contentScroll.ScrollBarImageColor3  = QP_ACC
    contentScroll.CanvasSize            = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    contentScroll.AutomaticCanvasSize   = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    contentScroll.ZIndex                = 101

    local toggleBtnContainer = Instance.new("Frame", contentScroll)
    toggleBtnContainer.AutomaticSize    = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    toggleBtnContainer.Size             = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
    toggleBtnContainer.Position         = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 4)
    toggleBtnContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    toggleBtnContainer.ZIndex           = 101

    local toggleLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", toggleBtnContainer)
    toggleLayout.Padding    = UDim.new(0, QP_ROW_GAP)
    toggleLayout.SortOrder  = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

    -- ── Footer bar ────────────────────────────────────────────
    local footer = Instance.new("Frame", panel)
    footer.Name               = "Footer"
    footer.Size               = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, QP_FOOTER_H)
    footer.Position           = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -QP_FOOTER_H)
    footer.BackgroundColor3   = QP_SURF
    footer.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
    footer.BorderSizePixel    = 0
    footer.ZIndex             = 101
    do local c = Instance.new("UICorner"); c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8); c.Parent = footer end
    -- top border line
    do
        local fl = Instance.new("Frame", footer)
        fl.Size             = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
        fl.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 50, 60)
        fl.BorderSizePixel  = 0
        fl.ZIndex           = 102
    end

    local addBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", footer)
    addBtn.Size                 = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 28)
    addBtn.Position             = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, -14)
    addBtn.BackgroundColor3     = QP_ACC
    addBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    addBtn.Text                 = "+ Customize"
    addBtn.Font                 = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    addBtn.TextSize             = 14
    addBtn.TextColor3           = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    addBtn.TextTransparency     = 0
    addBtn.AutoButtonColor      = false
    addBtn.BorderSizePixel      = 0
    addBtn.ZIndex               = 102
    do local c = Instance.new("UICorner"); c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7); c.Parent = addBtn end

    addBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        qpTween(addBtn, 0.12, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.25})
    end)
    addBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        qpTween(addBtn, 0.12, {BackgroundTransparency = 0})
    end)

    -- ── Pin helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────
    if type(Config.QuickPanelPinned) ~= "table" then Config.QuickPanelPinned = {} end

    local function isPinned(key)
        for _, k in ipairs(Config.QuickPanelPinned) do
            if k == key then return true end
        end
        return false
    end

    local function setPinned(key, pin)
        if pin then
            if not isPinned(key) then table.insert(Config.QuickPanelPinned, key) end
        else
            for i, k in ipairs(Config.QuickPanelPinned) do
                if k == key then table.remove(Config.QuickPanelPinned, i); break end
            end
        end
        SaveConfig()
    end

    -- ── Empty-state label ─────────────────────────────────────
    local emptyLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", panel)
    emptyLbl.Size               = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 60)
    emptyLbl.Position           = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, QP_HEADER_H + 20)
    emptyLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    emptyLbl.Text               = "No features added.\nPress '+ Customize' below."
    emptyLbl.Font               = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    emptyLbl.TextSize           = 12
    emptyLbl.TextColor3         = QP_DIM
    emptyLbl.TextWrapped        = true
    emptyLbl.TextXAlignment     = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    emptyLbl.TextYAlignment     = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    emptyLbl.ZIndex             = 102
    emptyLbl.Visible            = true

    -- ── Row builder: main view (toggle switch) ────────────────
    local refreshFns = {}

    local function buildFeatureRow(feat, order)
        local row = Instance.new("Frame", toggleBtnContainer)
        row.Name               = feat.key .. "Row"
        row.Size               = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, QP_ROW_H)
        row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        row.BorderSizePixel    = 0
        row.LayoutOrder        = order
        row.ZIndex             = 102

        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.Position           = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 0)
        label.Size               = UDim2.new(1, -64, 1, 0)
        label.Font               = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        label.Text               = feat.label
        label.TextColor3         = QP_TEXT
        label.TextSize           = 13 * mobileButtonScale
        label.TextXAlignment     = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        label.ZIndex             = 103

        -- ── Action variant: sleek RUN button (one-shot click) ─────
        if feat.kind == "action" then
            local btnW = math.floor(54 * mobileButtonScale)
            local btnH = math.floor(22 * mobileButtonScale)
            local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
            btn.AutoButtonColor = false
            btn.Size            = UDim2.fromOffset(btnW, btnH)
            btn.Position        = UDim2.new(1, -btnW - 8, 0.5, -math.floor(btnH / 2))
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = QP_ACC
            btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0
            btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
            btn.Text            = "RUN"
            btn.Font            = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
            btn.TextSize        = 11 * mobileButtonScale
            btn.TextColor3      = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            btn.ZIndex          = 103
            do local c = Instance.new("UICorner"); c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6); c.Parent = btn end
            local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", btn)
            stroke.Color = QP_ACC
            stroke.Thickness = 1
            stroke.Transparency = 0.4

            btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                qpTween(btn, 0.12, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.2})
            end)
            btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                qpTween(btn, 0.12, {BackgroundTransparency = 0})
            end)
            btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                -- Flash white briefly on press so the click feels responsive
                local prevText = btn.Text
                btn.Text = "•••"
                qpTween(btn, 0.08, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)})
                task.spawn(function()
                    pcall(feat.run)
                end)
                task.delay(0.18, function()
                    if not btn.Parent then return end
                    qpTween(btn, 0.18, {BackgroundColor3 = QP_ACC})
                    btn.Text = prevText
                end)
            end)
            return  -- action button done; skip the toggle-switch code below
        end

        -- ── Toggle variant (default) ──────────────────────────────
        local isOn = false
        local ok, v = pcall(feat.get)
        if ok then isOn = v == true end

        local trackW = math.floor(46 * mobileButtonScale)
        local knobS  = math.floor(20 * mobileButtonScale)
        local trackH = math.floor(22 * mobileButtonScale)

        local sw = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        sw.AutoButtonColor = false
        sw.Size      = UDim2.fromOffset(trackW, trackH)
        sw.Position  = UDim2.new(1, -math.floor(54 * mobileButtonScale), 0.5, -math.floor(trackH / 2))
        sw.BackgroundColor3 = isOn and QP_ACC or Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)
        sw.BorderSizePixel  = 0
        sw.Text      = ""
        sw.ZIndex    = 103
        do local c = Instance.new("UICorner"); c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0); c.Parent = sw end

        local knob = Instance.new("Frame", sw)
        knob.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(knobS, knobS)
        knob.Position = isOn
            and UDim2.fromOffset(trackW - knobS - 1, 1)
            or  UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1)
        knob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        knob.BorderSizePixel  = 0
        knob.ZIndex           = 104
        do local c = Instance.new("UICorner"); c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0); c.Parent = knob end

        local function paint(state)
            qpTween(sw,   0.12, {BackgroundColor3 = state and QP_ACC or Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)})
            qpTween(knob, 0.12, {
                Position         = state and UDim2.fromOffset(trackW - knobS - 1, 1) or UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1),
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
            })
        end

        sw.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            isOn = not isOn; paint(isOn); pcall(feat.set, isOn)
        end)

        refreshFns[feat.key] = function()
            local ok2, v2 = pcall(feat.get)
            local s = ok2 and v2 == true or false
            if s ~= isOn then isOn = s; paint(isOn) end
        end
    end

    -- ── Row builder: add mode (pin/unpin button) ──────────────
    local function buildAddRow(feat, order)
        local row = Instance.new("Frame", toggleBtnContainer)
        row.Name               = feat.key .. "AddRow"
        row.Size               = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, QP_ROW_H)
        row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        row.BorderSizePixel    = 0
        row.LayoutOrder        = order
        row.ZIndex             = 102

        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.Position           = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 0)
        label.Size               = UDim2.new(1, -52, 1, 0)
        label.Font               = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        label.Text               = feat.label
        label.TextColor3         = QP_TEXT
        label.TextSize           = 13 * mobileButtonScale
        label.TextXAlignment     = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        label.ZIndex             = 103

        local pinned = isPinned(feat.key)
        local pinBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        pinBtn.Size             = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 22)
        pinBtn.Position         = UDim2.new(1, -36, 0.5, -11)
        pinBtn.BackgroundColor3 = pinned and QP_ACC or QP_SURF2
        pinBtn.BackgroundTransparency = pinned and 0 or 0.2
        pinBtn.Text             = pinned and "✓" or "+"
        pinBtn.Font             = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        pinBtn.TextSize         = 13
        pinBtn.TextColor3       = QP_TEXT
        pinBtn.AutoButtonColor  = false
        pinBtn.BorderSizePixel  = 0
        pinBtn.ZIndex           = 103
        do local c = Instance.new("UICorner"); c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6); c.Parent = pinBtn end

        pinBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local nowPinned = not isPinned(feat.key)
            setPinned(feat.key, nowPinned)
            qpTween(pinBtn, 0.12, {
                BackgroundColor3       = nowPinned and QP_ACC or QP_SURF2,
                BackgroundTransparency = nowPinned and 0 or 0.2,
            })
            pinBtn.Text = nowPinned and "✓" or "+"
        end)
    end

    -- ── Content management ────────────────────────────────────
    local isAddMode = false

    local function clearRows()
        for _, ch in ipairs(toggleBtnContainer:GetChildren()) do
            if ch:IsA("Frame") then ch:Destroy() end
        end
        refreshFns = {}
    end

    local function rebuildMain()
        clearRows()
        local pinned = Config.QuickPanelPinned
        local hasPinned = false
        for i, key in ipairs(pinned) do
            local feat = featureByKey[key]
            if feat then
                buildFeatureRow(feat, i)
                hasPinned = true
            end
        end
        emptyLbl.Visible = not hasPinned
        contentScroll.Visible = hasPinned
    end

    local function rebuildAddMode()
        clearRows()
        emptyLbl.Visible = false
        contentScroll.Visible = true
        for i, feat in ipairs(QP_FEATURES) do
            buildAddRow(feat, i)
        end
    end

    local function setAddMode(add)
        isAddMode = add
        if add then
            addBtn.Text = "← Done"
            rebuildAddMode()
        else
            addBtn.Text = "+ Customize"
            rebuildMain()
        end
    end

    addBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        setAddMode(not isAddMode)
    end)

    -- ── Live state sync (poll every 0.5 s) ───────────────────
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(0.5)
            if not isAddMode then
                for _, refresh in pairs(refreshFns) do pcall(refresh) end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- ── Initial render (starts empty) ────────────────────────
    rebuildMain()
end)

-- =============================================================
-- TP AFTER CLONE
-- Called via _G.triggerTpAfterClone() from inside instantClone()
-- once the clone+swap completes. findAdorneeGlobal and SharedState
-- are both in scope here (defined above this point in the file).
-- =============================================================
_G.triggerTpAfterClone = function()
    if not Config.TpAfterClone then return end
    if State.isTpMoving then return end
    task.spawn(function()
        -- Settle: let the server replicate our new position after the swap
        task.wait(0.25)

        -- Resolve target brainrot (selected pet, else highest-gen cache entry)
        local targetData = SharedState.SelectedPetData
            and SharedState.SelectedPetData.animalData
        if not targetData then
            local cache = SharedState.AllAnimalsCache
            if type(cache) == "table" and #cache > 0 then
                targetData = cache[1]
            end
        end
        if not targetData then return end

        local targetPart = findAdorneeGlobal(targetData)
        if not targetPart then return end

        -- Fresh character refs post-swap
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp  = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum  = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end

        -- Equip carpet
        local carpetName = (Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.Tool) or "Flying Carpet"
        local carpetTool = LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild(carpetName)
            or char:FindFirstChild(carpetName)
        if carpetTool then
            pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(carpetTool) end)
            task.wait(0.08)
            char = LocalPlayer.Character
            hrp  = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            hum  = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not hrp or not hum then return end
        end

        -- Build destination position (mirrors Tween TP float step)
        local itemPos = targetPart.Position
        local isUpper = (itemPos.Y - hrp.Position.Y) > 2
        local destPos = isUpper
            and Vector3.new(itemPos.X, itemPos.Y - 5,   itemPos.Z)
            or  Vector3.new(itemPos.X, itemPos.Y + 1.5, itemPos.Z)

        -- Invisible platform to land on for upper-floor brainrots
        local plat
        if isUpper then
            plat              = Instance.new("Part")
            plat.Name         = "VANISH_TPC_PLATFORM"
            plat.Size         = Vector3.new(8, 1, 8)
            plat.Anchored     = true
            plat.CanCollide   = true
            plat.CanTouch     = false
            plat.CanQuery     = false
            plat.Transparency = 1
            plat.CastShadow   = false
            plat.Massless     = true
            plat.Locked       = true
            plat.CFrame       = CFrame.new(destPos - Vector3.new(0, 3.35, 0))
            plat.Parent       = Workspace
        end

        -- PlatformStand + massless for clean velocity flight
        local prevPS = hum.PlatformStand
        pcall(function() hum.PlatformStand = true end)
        local prevMassless = {}
        for _, p in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if p:IsA("BasePart") then
                prevMassless[p] = p.Massless
                pcall(function() p.Massless = true end)
            end
        end

        -- Velocity float toward brainrot
        local _speed   = (Config.TpSettings.Tool == "Grapple Hook")
            and math.clamp(tonumber(Config.TpGrappleSpeed) or 200, 180, 230)
            or  math.clamp(tonumber(Config.TpV2Speed) or 150, 150, 180)
        local _arrival = 1.4
        local _timeout = 5
        local _t0      = tick()
        local _done    = false
        local _conn
        _conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            if _done then _conn:Disconnect(); return end
            if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then
                _done = true; _conn:Disconnect(); return
            end
            if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") then
                pcall(function() hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end)
                _done = true; _conn:Disconnect(); return
            end
            if tick() - _t0 > _timeout then
                pcall(function() hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end)
                _done = true; _conn:Disconnect(); return
            end
            local diff = destPos - hrp.Position
            if diff.Magnitude <= _arrival then
                pcall(function() hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end)
                _done = true; _conn:Disconnect(); return
            end
            pcall(function() hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = diff.Unit * _speed end)
        end))
        while not _done do task.wait(0.05) end

        -- Restore physics state
        pcall(function() hum.PlatformStand = prevPS end)
        for p, m in pairs(prevMassless) do
            pcall(function() p.Massless = m end)
        end
        if plat and plat.Parent then
            task.spawn(function()
                local s = tick()
                while tick() - s < 20 do
                    if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") then break end
                    task.wait(0.1)
                end
                if plat and plat.Parent then plat:Destroy() end
            end)
        end

        -- Fire steal prompt
        if _G.vanishAttemptSteal and _G.vanishFindPromptForAnimal then
            local pr = _G.vanishFindPromptForAnimal(targetData)
            if pr then pcall(_G.vanishAttemptSteal, pr, targetData.uid) end
        end
    end)
end

-- =============================================================
-- Delta-specific desync (uses raknet.desync(boolean) directly).
-- Same lifecycle hooks as VanishDesync: set on join, suspend at the
-- start of runAutoSnipe, resume 0.2s after swap-with-clone. Honors
-- the same "defer on TpOnLoad" rule as VanishDesync.
-- =============================================================
task.spawn(function()
    local active = false
    local suspended = false

    local function callDesync(v)
        if typeof(raknet) ~= "table" or typeof(raknet.desync) ~= "function" then
            return false
        end
        local ok = pcall(raknet.desync, v and true or false)
        return ok
    end

    _G.VanishDeltaDesync = {
        -- Mirrors VanishDesync.setJoin: true = arm and turn on, false = turn off.
        set = function(v)
            if v then
                active = true
                if not suspended then callDesync(true) end
            else
                active = false
                suspended = false
                callDesync(false)
            end
        end,
        suspend = function()
            if not active then return end
            suspended = true
            callDesync(false)
            -- Safety: auto-resume after 15s in case the post-swap resume
            -- never fires (matches VanishDesync.suspend behavior).
            task.delay(15, function()
                if suspended and active then
                    suspended = false
                    callDesync(true)
                end
            end)
        end,
        resume = function()
            if not active then return end
            suspended = false
            callDesync(true)
        end,
        isActive = function()
            return active
        end,
    }

    if Config.DesyncOnDelta then
        local willDefer = Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.TpOnLoad
        if willDefer then
            _G.VanishDeltaDesyncJoinDeferred = true
        else
            _G.VanishDeltaDesync.set(true)
        end
    end
end)
